<script type="application/json" id="mg-state">{"modelKey":"M1-4"}</script>
<script>
/* ---- js/core/base.js ---- */
/* Namespace and small helpers shared by every module.
 *
 * Modules attach to the global MG namespace so that the page works as one offline file
 * (opened from disk, ES modules are blocked) and so that Node can load the same files for
 * tests and for figure export from Python. Load order is set in viz/page.py.
 */
(function (root) {
  "use strict";
  const MG = (root.MG = root.MG || {});

  MG.R_PREFIX = "R_";
  MG.PROXY_SUFFIX = "_star";

  /** Sorted copy of an iterable of strings (code-unit order, the same as Python's sorted). */
  MG.sorted = (items) => Array.from(items).sort((a, b) => (a < b ? -1 : a > b ? 1 : 0));

  /** Compare two sets for equality. */
  MG.setEq = (a, b) => a.size === b.size && [...a].every((v) => b.has(v));

  /** a is a proper subset of b. */
  MG.properSubset = (a, b) => a.size < b.size && [...a].every((v) => b.has(v));

  MG.subset = (a, b) => [...a].every((v) => b.has(v));

  /** Order separating sets as Python does: by size, then by sorted members. */
  MG.compareSets = (a, b) => {
    if (a.size !== b.size) return a.size - b.size;
    const sa = MG.sorted(a), sb = MG.sorted(b);
    for (let i = 0; i < sa.length; i++) if (sa[i] !== sb[i]) return sa[i] < sb[i] ? -1 : 1;
    return 0;
  };

  MG.setKey = (s) => MG.sorted(s).join("\u0001");

  MG.clamp = (v, lo, hi) => Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, v));

  MG.escapeXml = (s) =>
    String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[c]));

  MG.plural = (n, word, many) => `${n.toLocaleString("en-US")} ${n === 1 ? word : many || word + "s"}`;
})(typeof globalThis !== "undefined" ? globalThis : window);

;
/* ---- js/core/graph.js ---- */
/* MG.Graph: an m-graph with node roles, mirroring amr_identifiability.mgraph.MGraph.
 *
 *   X        substantive variable
 *   R_X      missingness indicator (0 observed, 1 missing)
 *   X_star   recorded proxy
 *
 * A non-latent node with both R_X and X_star present is partially observed.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const ROLES = ["fully observed", "partially observed", "missingness indicator", "recorded proxy", "latent"];

  class Graph {
    /**
     * @param {object} spec
     * @param {Array<string|{id:string}>} spec.nodes
     * @param {Array<[string,string]>} spec.edges
     * @param {Iterable<string>} [spec.latent]
     * @param {string} [spec.name]
     */
    constructor({ nodes = [], edges = [], latent = [], name = "", key = "" }) {
      this.name = name;
      this.key = key;
      this.ids = [];
      this.meta = new Map();
      this._par = new Map();
      this._ch = new Map();
      for (const n of nodes) this._addNode(typeof n === "string" ? { id: n } : n);
      for (const [a, b] of edges) {
        if (a === b) throw new Error(`self-loop on ${a}`);
        this._addNode({ id: a });
        this._addNode({ id: b });
        this._ch.get(a).add(b);
        this._par.get(b).add(a);
      }
      this.latent = new Set(latent);
      for (const l of this.latent) if (!this.meta.has(l)) throw new Error(`latent node not in the graph: ${l}`);
      const cycle = this._findCycle();
      if (cycle) throw new Error(`graph has a cycle: ${cycle.join(" -> ")}`);
      this._cache = new Map();
      this._roles();
    }

    _addNode(m) {
      if (this.meta.has(m.id)) return;
      this.ids.push(m.id);
      this.meta.set(m.id, { ...m });
      this._par.set(m.id, new Set());
      this._ch.set(m.id, new Set());
    }

    _findCycle() {
      const state = new Map();
      const stack = [];
      const visit = (n) => {
        state.set(n, 1);
        stack.push(n);
        for (const c of this._ch.get(n)) {
          if (state.get(c) === 1) return stack.slice(stack.indexOf(c)).concat(c);
          if (!state.has(c)) {
            const found = visit(c);
            if (found) return found;
          }
        }
        stack.pop();
        state.set(n, 2);
        return null;
      };
      for (const n of this.ids) if (!state.has(n)) {
        const found = visit(n);
        if (found) return found;
      }
      return null;
    }

    _roles() {
      const has = (n) => this.meta.has(n);
      this.partiallyObserved = new Set(
        this.ids.filter((n) => !this.latent.has(n) && has(MG.R_PREFIX + n) && has(n + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX))
      );
      this.indicators = new Set([...this.partiallyObserved].map((v) => MG.R_PREFIX + v));
      this.proxies = new Set([...this.partiallyObserved].map((v) => v + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX));
      this.fullyObserved = new Set(
        this.ids.filter(
          (n) => !this.latent.has(n) && !this.partiallyObserved.has(n) && !this.indicators.has(n) && !this.proxies.has(n)
        )
      );
      this.recorded = new Set(this.ids.filter((n) => !this.latent.has(n)));
    }

    static fromPayload(model) {
      return new Graph({
        nodes: model.nodes,
        edges: model.edges,
        latent: model.nodes.filter((n) => n.latent).map((n) => n.id),
        name: model.name,
        key: model.key,
      });
    }

    get nodes() {
      return this.ids.slice();
    }

    get edges() {
      const out = [];
      for (const a of this.ids) for (const b of this._ch.get(a)) out.push([a, b]);
      return out;
    }

    get edgeCount() {
      let n = 0;
      for (const s of this._ch.values()) n += s.size;
      return n;
    }

    has(n) {
      return this.meta.has(n);
    }

    require(nodes) {
      const unknown = [...nodes].filter((n) => !this.meta.has(n));
      if (unknown.length) throw new Error(`not in the graph: ${MG.sorted(unknown).join(", ")}`);
    }

    parents(n) {
      return this._par.get(n);
    }

    children(n) {
      return this._ch.get(n);
    }

    hasEdge(a, b) {
      return this._ch.has(a) && this._ch.get(a).has(b);
    }

    adjacent(a, b) {
      return this.hasEdge(a, b) || this.hasEdge(b, a);
    }

    neighbours(n) {
      return new Set([...this._par.get(n), ...this._ch.get(n)]);
    }

    _walk(start, next) {
      const out = new Set();
      const stack = [start];
      while (stack.length) for (const m of next(stack.pop())) if (!out.has(m)) {
        out.add(m);
        stack.push(m);
      }
      out.delete(start);
      return out;
    }

    ancestors(n) {
      const k = "a:" + n;
      if (!this._cache.has(k)) this._cache.set(k, this._walk(n, (v) => this._par.get(v)));
      return this._cache.get(k);
    }

    descendants(n) {
      const k = "d:" + n;
      if (!this._cache.has(k)) this._cache.set(k, this._walk(n, (v) => this._ch.get(v)));
      return this._cache.get(k);
    }

    /** The nodes and all their ancestors. */
    ancestralClosure(nodes) {
      const out = new Set(nodes);
      for (const n of nodes) for (const a of this.ancestors(n)) out.add(a);
      return out;
    }

    role(n) {
      if (this.latent.has(n)) return "latent";
      if (this.partiallyObserved.has(n)) return "partially observed";
      if (this.indicators.has(n)) return "missingness indicator";
      if (this.proxies.has(n)) return "recorded proxy";
      return "fully observed";
    }

    wouldCreateCycle(a, b) {
      return a === b || this.descendants(b).has(a);
    }

    /** A new graph with edits applied; this graph is unchanged. */
    with({ add = [], remove = [], latent = [], unlatent = [], name, key } = {}) {
      const removeKeys = new Set(remove.map(([a, b]) => a + "\u0001" + b));
      const edges = this.edges.filter(([a, b]) => !removeKeys.has(a + "\u0001" + b));
      const seen = new Set(edges.map(([a, b]) => a + "\u0001" + b));
      for (const [a, b] of add) if (!seen.has(a + "\u0001" + b)) {
        edges.push([a, b]);
        seen.add(a + "\u0001" + b);
      }
      const lat = new Set([...this.latent, ...latent]);
      for (const n of unlatent) lat.delete(n);
      return new Graph({
        nodes: this.ids.map((id) => this.meta.get(id)),
        edges,
        latent: lat,
        name: name || this.name,
        key: key || this.key,
      });
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------- missing-data structure

    /** Pairs of non-latent nodes with a common latent ancestor reached through latent nodes only. */
    latentLinks() {
      const links = new Set();
      for (const u of this.latent) {
        const reached = new Set();
        const seen = new Set([u]);
        const stack = [u];
        while (stack.length) for (const c of this._ch.get(stack.pop())) {
          if (this.latent.has(c)) {
            if (!seen.has(c)) {
              seen.add(c);
              stack.push(c);
            }
          } else reached.add(c);
        }
        const r = MG.sorted(reached);
        for (let i = 0; i < r.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < r.length; j++) links.add(r[i] + "\u0001" + r[j]);
      }
      return links;
    }

    latentLinked(a, b) {
      const [p, q] = MG.sorted([a, b]);
      return this.latentLinks().has(p + "\u0001" + q);
    }

    latentDirected(source, target) {
      const stack = [source];
      const seen = new Set([source]);
      while (stack.length) for (const c of this._ch.get(stack.pop())) {
        if (c === target) return true;
        if (this.latent.has(c) && !seen.has(c)) {
          seen.add(c);
          stack.push(c);
        }
      }
      return false;
    }

    /** {variable: reason} for variables whose value can drive their own missingness. */
    selfMasking() {
      const links = this.latentLinks();
      const out = {};
      for (const v of MG.sorted(this.partiallyObserved)) {
        const r = MG.R_PREFIX + v;
        const [p, q] = MG.sorted([v, r]);
        if (this.hasEdge(v, r)) out[v] = `direct edge ${v} -> ${r}`;
        else if (this.latentDirected(v, r)) out[v] = `${v} reaches ${r} through latent nodes only`;
        else if (links.has(p + "\u0001" + q)) out[v] = `${v} and ${r} share an unrecorded cause`;
      }
      return out;
    }

    /** Structural problems as an m-graph; mirrors MGraph.validate. */
    validate() {
      const problems = [];
      for (const v of MG.sorted(this.partiallyObserved)) {
        const r = MG.R_PREFIX + v, star = v + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX;
        const ps = this.parents(star);
        if (!(ps.size === 2 && ps.has(v) && ps.has(r)))
          problems.push(`${star} must have exactly the parents ${v} and ${r}; has ${MG.sorted(ps).join(", ") || "none"}`);
        if (this.children(star).size) problems.push(`${star} is a proxy and must have no children`);
        for (const c of MG.sorted(this.children(r)))
          if (c !== star && !this.indicators.has(c))
            problems.push(`${r} is a missingness indicator; its child ${c} is neither its proxy nor an indicator`);
      }
      for (const n of MG.sorted(this.ids)) {
        if (n.startsWith(MG.R_PREFIX) && !this.indicators.has(n))
          problems.push(`${n} looks like an indicator but ${n.slice(2)} has no proxy or is latent`);
        if (n.endsWith(MG.PROXY_SUFFIX) && !this.proxies.has(n))
          problems.push(`${n} looks like a proxy but ${n.slice(0, -5)} has no indicator or is latent`);
      }
      return problems;
    }
  }

  MG.ROLES = ROLES;
  MG.Graph = Graph;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/statement.js ---- */
/* MG.Statement: the independence X _||_ Y | Z, with parsing, testability and the recorded form.
 * Mirrors amr_identifiability.mgraph.Independence and MGraph.is_testable / recorded_form.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const SEPARATORS = /\s*(?:_\|\|_|⊥⊥|⫫|⊥)\s*/;

  class Statement {
    constructor(x, y, z = []) {
      this.x = new Set(x);
      this.y = new Set(y);
      this.z = new Set(z);
    }

    /** Parse "X, Y _||_ Z | A, B". Also accepts ⊥⊥ or ⫫ in place of _||_. */
    static parse(text) {
      const t = String(text || "").trim();
      const m = t.split(SEPARATORS);
      if (m.length !== 2) throw new Error("write it as  X _||_ Y | Z");
      const [left, rest] = m;
      const bar = rest.indexOf("|");
      const right = bar === -1 ? rest : rest.slice(0, bar);
      const given = bar === -1 ? "" : rest.slice(bar + 1);
      const names = (s) => s.split(/[,\s]+/).filter(Boolean);
      const x = names(left), y = names(right);
      if (!x.length || !y.length) throw new Error("both sides of _||_ need at least one variable");
      return new Statement(x, y, names(given));
    }

    /** Like parse, but never throws: missing parts come back empty (for building a query). */
    static parseLoose(text) {
      const t = String(text || "").trim();
      const names = (s) => s.split(/[,\s]+/).filter(Boolean);
      const m = t.split(SEPARATORS);
      if (m.length < 2) return new Statement(names(t.split("|")[0]), [], []);
      const rest = m.slice(1).join(" ");
      const bar = rest.indexOf("|");
      return new Statement(names(m[0]), names(bar === -1 ? rest : rest.slice(0, bar)), bar === -1 ? [] : names(rest.slice(bar + 1)));
    }

    /** Text for a possibly incomplete statement. */
    toLooseString() {
      const f = (s) => MG.sorted(s).join(", ");
      let t = `${f(this.x)} _||_ ${f(this.y)}`.trim();
      if (this.z.size) t += ` | ${f(this.z)}`;
      return t;
    }

    get involved() {
      return new Set([...this.x, ...this.y, ...this.z]);
    }

    /** Check against a graph: unknown nodes and overlaps. Throws with a readable message. */
    check(graph) {
      graph.require(this.involved);
      const both = [...this.x].filter((n) => this.y.has(n));
      if (both.length) throw new Error(`${MG.sorted(both).join(", ")} on both sides`);
      const zx = [...this.z].filter((n) => this.x.has(n) || this.y.has(n));
      if (zx.length) throw new Error(`${MG.sorted(zx).join(", ")} is also conditioned on`);
      return this;
    }

    toString() {
      const f = (s) => MG.sorted(s).join(", ");
      const head = `${f(this.x)} _||_ ${f(this.y)}`;
      return this.z.size ? `${head} | ${f(this.z)}` : head;
    }

    /** Typeset form for the screen. */
    pretty() {
      const f = (s) => MG.sorted(s).join(", ");
      const head = `${f(this.x)} ⊥⊥ ${f(this.y)}`;
      return this.z.size ? `${head} | ${f(this.z)}` : head;
    }

    key() {
      return this.toString();
    }

    equals(other) {
      return other && MG.setEq(this.x, other.x) && MG.setEq(this.y, other.y) && MG.setEq(this.z, other.z);
    }
  }

  /** Sufficient condition for testability (Mohan & Pearl 2014); mirrors MGraph.is_testable. */
  Statement.testable = function (graph, s) {
    return Statement.whyNotTestable(graph, s) === null;
  };

  Statement.whyNotTestable = function (graph, s) {
    const involved = s.involved;
    const latent = [...involved].filter((n) => graph.latent.has(n));
    if (latent.length) return `involves latent ${MG.sorted(latent).join(", ")}`;
    const proxies = [...involved].filter((n) => graph.proxies.has(n));
    if (proxies.length) return `involves proxy ${MG.sorted(proxies).join(", ")}; use the variable and its indicator instead`;
    const needed = [...involved].filter((n) => graph.partiallyObserved.has(n)).map((n) => MG.R_PREFIX + n);
    const own = needed.filter((r) => s.x.has(r) || s.y.has(r));
    if (own.length)
      return `${MG.sorted(own).map((r) => r.slice(MG.R_PREFIX.length)).join(", ")} is only recorded where ${MG.sorted(own).join(", ")} = 0, so its independence from its own indicator cannot be checked in recorded data`;
    const missing = needed.filter((r) => !s.z.has(r));
    if (missing.length) return `needs ${MG.sorted(missing).join(", ")} in the conditioning set`;
    return null;
  };

  Statement.recordedForm = function (graph, s) {
    if (!Statement.testable(graph, s)) return null;
    const rename = (n) => (graph.partiallyObserved.has(n) ? n + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX : n);
    const fixed = new Set([...s.involved].filter((n) => graph.partiallyObserved.has(n)).map((n) => MG.R_PREFIX + n));
    const fmt = (nodes) => MG.sorted([...nodes].map(rename)).join(", ");
    const rest = [...s.z].filter((n) => !fixed.has(n));
    const given = rest.length ? [fmt(rest)] : [];
    for (const r of MG.sorted(fixed)) given.push(`${r} = 0`);
    const head = `${fmt(s.x)} _||_ ${fmt(s.y)}`;
    return given.length ? `${head} | ${given.join(", ")}` : head;
  };

  MG.Statement = Statement;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/dsep.js ---- */
/* d-separation and path analysis.
 *
 * dSeparated: the reachability ("Bayes-ball") algorithm of Koller & Friedman (2009, Alg. 3.1).
 * paths:      simple paths in the skeleton, each marked open or blocked with the reason, using
 *             the same wording as MGraph.paths in Python.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  function dSeparated(graph, X, Y, Z = []) {
    X = new Set(X);
    Y = new Set(Y);
    Z = new Set(Z);
    const anc = graph.ancestralClosure(Z); // Z and its ancestors: colliders here are opened
    const visited = new Set();
    const queue = [];
    for (const x of X) queue.push([x, "up"]);
    while (queue.length) {
      const [n, dir] = queue.pop();
      const k = n + "|" + dir;
      if (visited.has(k)) continue;
      visited.add(k);
      if (!Z.has(n) && Y.has(n)) return false;
      if (dir === "up" && !Z.has(n)) {
        for (const p of graph.parents(n)) queue.push([p, "up"]);
        for (const c of graph.children(n)) queue.push([c, "down"]);
      } else if (dir === "down") {
        if (!Z.has(n)) for (const c of graph.children(n)) queue.push([c, "down"]);
        if (anc.has(n)) for (const p of graph.parents(n)) queue.push([p, "up"]);
      }
    }
    return true;
  }

  /** Is the statement implied by the graph? Checks node names and overlaps first. */
  function implies(graph, statement) {
    statement.check(graph);
    return dSeparated(graph, statement.x, statement.y, statement.z);
  }

  /** Status of one path given Z; forward[i] is true when the edge points path[i] -> path[i+1]. */
  function pathStatus(graph, path, Z) {
    const forward = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < path.length - 1; i++) forward.push(graph.hasEdge(path[i], path[i + 1]));
    for (let i = 1; i < path.length - 1; i++) {
      const node = path[i];
      const collider = forward[i - 1] && !forward[i];
      if (collider) {
        if (!Z.has(node) && ![...graph.descendants(node)].some((d) => Z.has(d)))
          return { path, forward, open: false, blocker: node, reason: `collider ${node} not conditioned on` };
      } else if (Z.has(node)) {
        return { path, forward, open: false, blocker: node, reason: `${node} conditioned on` };
      }
    }
    return { path, forward, open: true, blocker: null, reason: "no blocking node" };
  }

  /**
   * Simple paths between x and y with their status.
   * With onlyOpen, branches are pruned as soon as an inner node blocks, so open paths are found
   * quickly even in dense graphs. Results are sorted shortest first.
   */
  function paths(graph, x, y, Z = [], { onlyOpen = false, maxPaths = 300, maxLength = 14 } = {}) {
    Z = new Set(Z);
    const zAnc = graph.ancestralClosure(Z);
    const out = [];
    let truncated = false;
    const path = [x];
    const forward = [];
    const onPath = new Set([x]);

    const innerBlocks = (i) => {
      // the inner node path[i], with edges forward[i-1] and forward[i]
      const node = path[i];
      const collider = forward[i - 1] && !forward[i];
      return collider ? !zAnc.has(node) : Z.has(node);
    };

    const step = (n) => {
      if (out.length >= maxPaths) {
        truncated = true;
        return;
      }
      for (const [m, fwd] of neighbourEdges(n)) {
        if (onPath.has(m)) continue;
        path.push(m);
        forward.push(fwd);
        const i = path.length - 2; // n is now an inner node when it is not x
        const prunable = onlyOpen && i >= 1 && innerBlocks(i);
        if (!prunable) {
          if (m === y) {
            const st = pathStatus(graph, path.slice(), Z);
            if (st.open || !onlyOpen) out.push(st);
          } else if (path.length <= maxLength) {
            onPath.add(m);
            step(m);
            onPath.delete(m);
          }
        }
        path.pop();
        forward.pop();
        if (out.length >= maxPaths) {
          truncated = true;
          return;
        }
      }
    };

    const neighbourEdges = (n) => {
      const list = [];
      for (const c of MG.sorted(graph.children(n))) list.push([c, true]);
      for (const p of MG.sorted(graph.parents(n))) list.push([p, false]);
      return list;
    };

    step(x);
    out.sort((a, b) => a.path.length - b.path.length || (a.path.join() < b.path.join() ? -1 : 1));
    return { paths: out, truncated };
  }

  /** Text form used by the Python CLI: "Yk <- U_B -> Yj1". */
  function formatPath(p) {
    const parts = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < p.path.length - 1; i++) parts.push(p.path[i], p.forward[i] ? "→" : "←");
    parts.push(p.path[p.path.length - 1]);
    return parts.join(" ");
  }

  MG.dsep = { dSeparated, implies, pathStatus, paths, formatPath };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/separators.js ---- */
/* Every minimal d-separating set between two nodes; mirrors amr_identifiability.separators.
 *
 * Minimal separators lie in the ancestral set An(x, y, required) and there equal vertex
 * separators of the moral graph. Required nodes are removed, nodes that may not be
 * conditioned on are eliminated (their neighbours joined), and the minimal x-y vertex
 * separators are listed with the close-separator algorithm (Berry, Bordat & Cogis 1999).
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  function moralGraph(graph, nodes) {
    const adj = new Map();
    for (const n of nodes) adj.set(n, new Set());
    for (const child of nodes) {
      const parents = [...graph.parents(child)].filter((p) => nodes.has(p));
      for (const p of parents) {
        adj.get(p).add(child);
        adj.get(child).add(p);
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < parents.length; i++)
        for (let j = i + 1; j < parents.length; j++) {
          adj.get(parents[i]).add(parents[j]);
          adj.get(parents[j]).add(parents[i]);
        }
    }
    return adj;
  }

  function eliminate(adj, nodes) {
    const gone = new Set([...nodes].filter((n) => adj.has(n)));
    const out = new Map();
    for (const [n, nb] of adj) if (!gone.has(n)) out.set(n, new Set([...nb].filter((m) => !gone.has(m))));
    const seen = new Set();
    for (const start of gone) {
      if (seen.has(start)) continue;
      const group = new Set([start]);
      const border = new Set();
      const stack = [start];
      while (stack.length) for (const m of adj.get(stack.pop())) {
        if (gone.has(m)) {
          if (!group.has(m)) {
            group.add(m);
            stack.push(m);
          }
        } else border.add(m);
      }
      for (const g of group) seen.add(g);
      for (const m of border) for (const o of border) if (o !== m) out.get(m).add(o);
    }
    return out;
  }

  function component(adj, start, removed) {
    const seen = new Set([start]);
    const stack = [start];
    while (stack.length) for (const m of adj.get(stack.pop())) if (!removed.has(m) && !seen.has(m)) {
      seen.add(m);
      stack.push(m);
    }
    return seen;
  }

  function border(adj, comp) {
    const out = new Set();
    for (const n of comp) for (const m of adj.get(n)) if (!comp.has(m)) out.add(m);
    return out;
  }

  function minimalVertexSeparators(adj, a, b, limit = null) {
    if (adj.get(a).has(b)) return { separators: [], truncated: false };
    if (!component(adj, a, new Set()).has(b)) return { separators: [new Set()], truncated: false };
    const first = border(adj, component(adj, b, new Set([...adj.get(a), a])));
    const seen = new Set([MG.setKey(first)]);
    const queue = [first];
    const out = [];
    let head = 0;
    while (head < queue.length) {
      const sep = queue[head++];
      out.push(sep);
      if (limit !== null && out.length >= limit) return { separators: out.sort(MG.compareSets), truncated: head < queue.length };
      for (const x of sep) {
        if (adj.get(x).has(b)) continue;
        const nxt = border(adj, component(adj, b, new Set([...sep, ...adj.get(x)])));
        const k = MG.setKey(nxt);
        if (!seen.has(k)) {
          seen.add(k);
          queue.push(nxt);
        }
      }
    }
    return { separators: out.sort(MG.compareSets), truncated: false };
  }

  /** Every minimal d-separator Z of x and y with included <= Z <= included | allowed. */
  function minimalDSeparators(graph, x, y, { included = [], allowed = null, limit = null } = {}) {
    included = new Set(included);
    if (x === y) throw new Error("x and y must differ");
    if (included.has(x) || included.has(y)) throw new Error("x and y cannot be in the conditioning set");
    allowed = allowed === null ? new Set(graph.ids) : new Set(allowed);
    const anc = graph.ancestralClosure(new Set([x, y, ...included]));
    const moral = moralGraph(graph, anc);
    let adj = new Map();
    for (const [n, nb] of moral) if (!included.has(n)) adj.set(n, new Set([...nb].filter((m) => !included.has(m))));
    const forbidden = [...adj.keys()].filter((n) => !allowed.has(n) && n !== x && n !== y);
    adj = eliminate(adj, forbidden);
    const { separators, truncated } = minimalVertexSeparators(adj, x, y, limit);
    return { separators: separators.map((s) => new Set([...s, ...included])), truncated };
  }

  MG.separators = { moralGraph, eliminate, minimalVertexSeparators, minimalDSeparators };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/catalogue.js ---- */
/* MG.Catalogue: every minimal conditional independence between recorded nodes.
 * Mirrors amr_identifiability.catalogue.ConditionCatalogue (the tests compare them).
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const pairKey = (a, b) => (a < b ? a + "\u0001" + b : b + "\u0001" + a);

  class Pair {
    constructor(x, y, adjacent, separators, testable, truncated) {
      this.x = x;
      this.y = y;
      this.adjacent = adjacent;
      this.separators = separators; // Array<Set>
      this.testable = testable; // Array<Set>
      this.truncated = truncated;
      this._testableKeys = new Set(testable.map(MG.setKey));
    }

    get separable() {
      return this.separators.length > 0;
    }

    get reason() {
      if (this.separators.length) return `${this.separators.length} minimal separating set(s)`;
      if (this.adjacent) return "adjacent: joined by an edge";
      return "no set of recorded nodes separates them";
    }

    isTestableSet(z) {
      return this._testableKeys.has(MG.setKey(z));
    }

    other(node) {
      return node === this.x ? this.y : this.x;
    }
  }

  /** X _||_ (non-descendants minus parents) | parents, or null when that set is empty. */
  function localMarkov(g, node) {
    const exclude = new Set([node, ...g.descendants(node), ...g.parents(node)]);
    const rest = g.ids.filter((n) => !exclude.has(n));
    return rest.length ? new MG.Statement([node], rest, g.parents(node)) : null;
  }

  class Catalogue {
    constructor(graph, { limit = null } = {}) {
      this.graph = graph;
      this.limit = limit;
      this.nodes = MG.sorted(graph.recorded);
      this._allowed = graph.recorded;
      this._testableAllowed = new Set([...graph.recorded].filter((n) => !graph.proxies.has(n)));
      this.pairs = [];
      this._byKey = new Map();
      for (let i = 0; i < this.nodes.length; i++)
        for (let j = i + 1; j < this.nodes.length; j++) {
          const p = this._build(this.nodes[i], this.nodes[j]);
          this.pairs.push(p);
          this._byKey.set(pairKey(p.x, p.y), p);
        }
    }

    _build(x, y) {
      const g = this.graph;
      if (g.adjacent(x, y)) return new Pair(x, y, true, [], [], false);
      const { separators, truncated } = MG.separators.minimalDSeparators(g, x, y, {
        allowed: this._allowed,
        limit: this.limit,
      });
      return new Pair(x, y, false, separators, this._testable(x, y), truncated);
    }

    _testable(x, y) {
      const g = this.graph;
      if (g.proxies.has(x) || g.proxies.has(y) || g.latent.has(x) || g.latent.has(y)) return [];
      const partial = g.partiallyObserved;
      const start = new Set([x, y].filter((v) => partial.has(v)).map((v) => MG.R_PREFIX + v));
      if (start.has(x) || start.has(y)) return [];
      const found = new Map();
      const queue = [start];
      const tried = new Set();
      while (queue.length) {
        const required = queue.pop();
        const rk = MG.setKey(required);
        if (tried.has(rk)) continue;
        tried.add(rk);
        const { separators } = MG.separators.minimalDSeparators(g, x, y, {
          included: required,
          allowed: this._testableAllowed,
          limit: this.limit,
        });
        for (const z of separators) {
          const needed = new Set([...z].filter((v) => partial.has(v)).map((v) => MG.R_PREFIX + v));
          if (MG.subset(needed, z)) found.set(MG.setKey(z), z);
          else if (!needed.has(x) && !needed.has(y)) queue.push(new Set([...required, ...needed]));
        }
      }
      const all = [...found.values()];
      return all.filter((z) => !all.some((o) => MG.properSubset(o, z))).sort(MG.compareSets);
    }

    pair(x, y) {
      return this._byKey.get(pairKey(x, y)) || null;
    }

    forNode(node) {
      return this.pairs.filter((p) => p.x === node || p.y === node).sort((a, b) => (a.other(node) < b.other(node) ? -1 : 1));
    }

    localMarkov(node) {
      return localMarkov(this.graph, node);
    }

    /** Flat rows, one per minimal statement (testable forms included when testableOnly). */
    rows({ testableOnly = false } = {}) {
      const out = [];
      for (const p of this.pairs) {
        const sets = testableOnly ? p.testable : p.separators;
        for (const z of sets) {
          const s = new MG.Statement([p.x], [p.y], z);
          const testable = p.isTestableSet(z);
          out.push({ pair: p, statement: s, testable, recorded: testable ? MG.Statement.recordedForm(this.graph, s) : null });
        }
      }
      return out;
    }

    summary() {
      let sep = 0, sets = 0, test = 0, adj = 0;
      for (const p of this.pairs) {
        if (p.separable) sep++;
        if (p.adjacent) adj++;
        sets += p.separators.length;
        test += p.testable.length;
      }
      return {
        nodes: this.nodes.length,
        pairs: this.pairs.length,
        separable: sep,
        sets,
        testable: test,
        adjacent: adj,
        inseparable: this.pairs.length - sep - adj,
      };
    }
  }

  MG.Catalogue = Catalogue;
  MG.localMarkov = localMarkov;
  MG.Catalogue.Pair = Pair;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/recovery.js ---- */
/* Recoverability checks; mirrors amr_identifiability.recovery.
 *
 * orderedFactorisation  Mohan & Pearl (2021) Theorem 1, checked step by step
 * findRecovery          smallest covariate set, helper set and ordering that recover a target
 * rFactorisationProblems  failures of the sufficient conditions for R-factorisation
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const R = (v) => MG.R_PREFIX + v;

  function combinations(items, k) {
    const out = [];
    const rec = (start, acc) => {
      if (acc.length === k) return out.push(acc.slice());
      for (let i = start; i < items.length; i++) {
        acc.push(items[i]);
        rec(i + 1, acc);
        acc.pop();
      }
    };
    rec(0, []);
    return out;
  }

  function permutations(items) {
    if (items.length <= 1) return [items.slice()];
    const out = [];
    // lexicographic by position, matching itertools.permutations
    const rec = (acc, rest) => {
      if (!rest.length) return out.push(acc);
      for (let i = 0; i < rest.length; i++) rec(acc.concat(rest[i]), rest.slice(0, i).concat(rest.slice(i + 1)));
    };
    rec([], items);
    return out;
  }

  function orderedFactorisation(graph, order, covariates = []) {
    order = order.slice();
    covariates = MG.sorted(covariates);
    if (!order.length) throw new Error("the order must contain at least the target");
    if (new Set(order).size !== order.length) throw new Error("the order repeats a variable");
    const notPartial = order.filter((v) => !graph.partiallyObserved.has(v));
    if (notPartial.length) throw new Error(`variables in the order must be partially observed: ${MG.sorted(notPartial).join(", ")}`);
    const bad = covariates.filter((v) => !graph.fullyObserved.has(v));
    if (bad.length) throw new Error(`covariates must be fully observed: ${MG.sorted(bad).join(", ")}`);
    const steps = order.map((variable, i) => {
      const earlier = order.slice(0, i);
      const given = earlier.concat(covariates);
      const indicators = earlier.concat(variable).map(R);
      const st = new MG.Statement([variable], indicators, given);
      return { variable, given, requires: st, holds: MG.dsep.dSeparated(graph, st.x, st.y, st.z) };
    });
    return makeRecovery(order[order.length - 1], order, covariates, steps);
  }

  function makeRecovery(target, order, covariates, steps) {
    const recoverable = steps.every((s) => s.holds);
    const factors = steps.map((s) => {
      const zeros = MG.sorted(s.requires.y).map((r) => `${r} = 0`);
      return `P(${s.variable} | ${s.given.concat(zeros).join(", ")})`;
    });
    const helpers = order.filter((v) => v !== target);
    const lhs = covariates.length ? `P(${target} | ${covariates.join(", ")})` : `P(${target})`;
    const functional = `${lhs} = ${helpers.length ? `sum over ${helpers.join(", ")} of ` : ""}${factors.join(" * ")}`;
    return { target, order, covariates, steps, recoverable, functional };
  }

  function findRecovery(graph, target, { helpers = null, covariates = null, maxHelpers = 4, maxCovariates = 4, limit = 10 } = {}) {
    if (!graph.partiallyObserved.has(target)) throw new Error(`${target} is not partially observed`);
    const helperPool = MG.sorted(helpers ? new Set(helpers) : [...graph.partiallyObserved].filter((v) => v !== target));
    const covPool = MG.sorted(covariates ? new Set(covariates) : graph.fullyObserved);
    const memo = new Map();
    const sep = (x, ys, zs) => {
      const k = x + "|" + MG.setKey(ys) + "|" + MG.setKey(zs);
      if (!memo.has(k)) memo.set(k, MG.dsep.dSeparated(graph, [x], ys, zs));
      return memo.get(k);
    };
    const found = [];
    let best = null;
    for (let nCov = 0; nCov <= Math.min(maxCovariates, covPool.length); nCov++) {
      for (const cov of combinations(covPool, nCov)) {
        for (let nHelp = 0; nHelp <= Math.min(maxHelpers, helperPool.length); nHelp++) {
          if (best !== null && nCov + nHelp > best) break;
          for (const chosen of combinations(helperPool, nHelp)) {
            const last = sep(target, new Set(chosen.concat(target).map(R)), new Set(chosen.concat(cov)));
            if (!last) continue;
            for (const order of permutations(chosen)) {
              const ok = order.every((v, i) => sep(v, new Set(order.slice(0, i + 1).map(R)), new Set(order.slice(0, i).concat(cov))));
              if (ok) {
                found.push(orderedFactorisation(graph, order.concat(target), cov));
                best = nCov + nHelp;
                break;
              }
            }
            if (found.length >= limit) return found;
          }
        }
      }
      if (best !== null && nCov >= best) break;
    }
    return found;
  }

  function rFactorisationProblems(graph, variables = null) {
    variables = MG.sorted(variables ? new Set(variables) : graph.partiallyObserved);
    const problems = [];
    const masking = graph.selfMasking();
    for (const v of variables) if (masking[v]) problems.push(`self-masking: ${masking[v]}`);
    const indicators = new Set(variables.map(R));
    const ind = MG.sorted(indicators);
    for (const a of ind) for (const b of ind) if (a !== b && graph.hasEdge(a, b)) problems.push(`indicator ${a} is a parent of indicator ${b}`);
    // an all-collider path has exactly one inner node: v -> c <- R_v with c recorded
    const recorded = new Set([...graph.fullyObserved, ...graph.partiallyObserved]);
    for (const v of variables) {
      const r = R(v);
      for (const c of MG.sorted(graph.children(v))) if (graph.children(r).has(c) && recorded.has(c)) problems.push(`all-collider path ${v} - ${c} - ${r}`);
    }
    const links = MG.sorted(graph.latentLinks());
    for (const link of links) {
      const [a, b] = link.split("\u0001");
      if (indicators.has(a) && indicators.has(b)) problems.push(`unrecorded common cause of ${a} and ${b}`);
    }
    return problems;
  }

  MG.recovery = { orderedFactorisation, findRecovery, rFactorisationProblems };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/admg.js ---- */
/* MG.ADMG: acyclic directed mixed graphs and the latent projection of a DAG.
 *
 * The latent projection (Verma 1993; Tian & Pearl 2002) of a DAG over observed nodes O keeps
 *   a -> b   when a directed path a -> ... -> b has only latent intermediate nodes
 *   a <-> b  when some latent node reaches both a and b by directed paths through latent
 *            nodes only (an unrecorded common cause)
 * It has the same d-separations among O as the DAG, and it is the graph the theorems of
 * Mohan & Pearl (2021) and the ID algorithm (Shpitser & Pearl 2006) are stated on.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const pk = (a, b) => (a < b ? a + "\u0001" + b : b + "\u0001" + a);

  class ADMG {
    /**
     * @param {string[]} nodes
     * @param {Array<[string,string]>} directed
     * @param {Array<[string,string]>} bidirected
     */
    constructor(nodes, directed = [], bidirected = []) {
      this.ids = MG.sorted(new Set(nodes));
      this._set = new Set(this.ids);
      this._pa = new Map(this.ids.map((n) => [n, new Set()]));
      this._ch = new Map(this.ids.map((n) => [n, new Set()]));
      this._sib = new Map(this.ids.map((n) => [n, new Set()]));
      for (const [a, b] of directed) {
        if (!this._set.has(a) || !this._set.has(b)) continue;
        this._ch.get(a).add(b);
        this._pa.get(b).add(a);
      }
      for (const [a, b] of bidirected) {
        if (!this._set.has(a) || !this._set.has(b) || a === b) continue;
        this._sib.get(a).add(b);
        this._sib.get(b).add(a);
      }
    }

    /** Latent projection of an MG.Graph onto its non-latent nodes (or onto `keep`). */
    static project(graph, keep = null) {
      const observed = new Set(keep ? keep : graph.ids.filter((n) => !graph.latent.has(n)));
      const hidden = (n) => !observed.has(n);
      const directed = [];
      for (const a of observed) {
        // directed paths from a through hidden nodes only
        const stack = [...graph.children(a)];
        const seen = new Set();
        while (stack.length) {
          const c = stack.pop();
          if (seen.has(c)) continue;
          seen.add(c);
          if (hidden(c)) stack.push(...graph.children(c));
          else directed.push([a, c]);
        }
      }
      const bidirected = [];
      for (const u of graph.ids) {
        if (!hidden(u)) continue;
        const reach = new Set();
        const stack = [...graph.children(u)];
        const seen = new Set();
        while (stack.length) {
          const c = stack.pop();
          if (seen.has(c)) continue;
          seen.add(c);
          if (hidden(c)) stack.push(...graph.children(c));
          else reach.add(c);
        }
        const r = MG.sorted(reach);
        for (let i = 0; i < r.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < r.length; j++) bidirected.push([r[i], r[j]]);
      }
      return new ADMG([...observed], directed, bidirected);
    }

    has(n) {
      return this._set.has(n);
    }

    parents(n) {
      return this._pa.get(n);
    }

    children(n) {
      return this._ch.get(n);
    }

    siblings(n) {
      return this._sib.get(n);
    }

    hasDirected(a, b) {
      return this._ch.has(a) && this._ch.get(a).has(b);
    }

    hasBidirected(a, b) {
      return this._sib.has(a) && this._sib.get(a).has(b);
    }

    /** Joined by any edge: a -> b, b -> a or a <-> b. */
    adjacent(a, b) {
      return this.hasDirected(a, b) || this.hasDirected(b, a) || this.hasBidirected(a, b);
    }

    get directedEdges() {
      const out = [];
      for (const a of this.ids) for (const b of MG.sorted(this._ch.get(a))) out.push([a, b]);
      return out;
    }

    get bidirectedEdges() {
      const out = [];
      for (const a of this.ids) for (const b of MG.sorted(this._sib.get(a))) if (a < b) out.push([a, b]);
      return out;
    }

    /** The nodes and their ancestors (directed edges only). */
    ancestors(nodes) {
      const out = new Set(nodes);
      const stack = [...out];
      while (stack.length) for (const p of this._pa.get(stack.pop())) if (!out.has(p)) {
        out.add(p);
        stack.push(p);
      }
      return out;
    }

    descendants(nodes) {
      const out = new Set(nodes);
      const stack = [...out];
      while (stack.length) for (const c of this._ch.get(stack.pop())) if (!out.has(c)) {
        out.add(c);
        stack.push(c);
      }
      return out;
    }

    /** Induced subgraph on S. */
    sub(S) {
      S = new Set(S);
      return new ADMG([...S], this.directedEdges.filter(([a, b]) => S.has(a) && S.has(b)), this.bidirectedEdges.filter(([a, b]) => S.has(a) && S.has(b)));
    }

    /** G with edges into X removed (both directed and bidirected): the graph after do(X). */
    withoutIncoming(X) {
      X = new Set(X);
      return new ADMG(this.ids, this.directedEdges.filter(([, b]) => !X.has(b)), this.bidirectedEdges.filter(([a, b]) => !X.has(a) && !X.has(b)));
    }

    /** G with directed edges out of X removed (used by Rule 2 of the do-calculus). */
    withoutOutgoing(X) {
      X = new Set(X);
      return new ADMG(this.ids, this.directedEdges.filter(([a]) => !X.has(a)), this.bidirectedEdges);
    }

    /** Districts (c-components): classes of nodes joined by bidirected paths. */
    districts() {
      const seen = new Set();
      const out = [];
      for (const n of this.ids) {
        if (seen.has(n)) continue;
        const comp = new Set([n]);
        const stack = [n];
        seen.add(n);
        while (stack.length) for (const s of this._sib.get(stack.pop())) if (!seen.has(s)) {
          seen.add(s);
          comp.add(s);
          stack.push(s);
        }
        out.push(comp);
      }
      return out;
    }

    /** A topological order of the directed part (ties broken by name). */
    topological() {
      const indeg = new Map(this.ids.map((n) => [n, this._pa.get(n).size]));
      const ready = this.ids.filter((n) => !indeg.get(n));
      const out = [];
      while (ready.length) {
        ready.sort();
        const n = ready.shift();
        out.push(n);
        for (const c of this._ch.get(n)) {
          indeg.set(c, indeg.get(c) - 1);
          if (!indeg.get(c)) ready.push(c);
        }
      }
      return out;
    }

    /**
     * m-separation of X and Y given Z (the d-separation of mixed graphs), by converting to a
     * DAG with one latent node per bidirected edge and using MG.dsep.
     */
    separated(X, Y, Z = []) {
      const g = this.asDag();
      return MG.dsep.dSeparated(g, X, Y, Z);
    }

    asDag() {
      if (this._dag) return this._dag;
      const edges = this.directedEdges.slice();
      const nodes = this.ids.slice();
      const latent = [];
      for (const [a, b] of this.bidirectedEdges) {
        const u = `\u0000u_${a}_${b}`;
        nodes.push(u);
        latent.push(u);
        edges.push([u, a], [u, b]);
      }
      this._dag = new MG.Graph({ nodes, edges, latent });
      return this._dag;
    }

    /** Paths whose inner nodes are all colliders: a *-> c1 <-> c2 <-> ... <-* b, inner nodes in `allowed`. */
    colliderPaths(a, b, allowed, { maxLength = 12 } = {}) {
      // inner nodes must have arrowheads on both sides; an inner-inner step must therefore be <->
      const out = [];
      const into = (x, y) => this.hasDirected(x, y) || this.hasBidirected(x, y); // an edge with an arrowhead at y
      const first = [...this.ids].filter((c) => c !== a && c !== b && allowed.has(c) && into(a, c));
      const walk = (path) => {
        if (path.length > maxLength) return;
        const last = path[path.length - 1];
        if (into(b, last)) out.push([a, ...path, b]);
        for (const s of this._sib.get(last)) if (s !== a && s !== b && allowed.has(s) && !path.includes(s)) walk([...path, s]);
      };
      for (const c of first) walk([c]);
      return out;
    }

    toString() {
      return [...this.directedEdges.map(([a, b]) => `${a} -> ${b}`), ...this.bidirectedEdges.map(([a, b]) => `${a} <-> ${b}`)].join("\n");
    }
  }

  ADMG.pairKey = pk;
  MG.ADMG = ADMG;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/expr.js ---- */
/* MG.expr: probability expressions (estimands) as trees.
 *
 * Values are written with symbols. A symbol is the name of a substantive variable (Y, X, ...)
 * and stands for its value; sums range over the symbol's values. Each term says which observed
 * column carries the symbol, so the same tree can be printed and evaluated on data:
 *
 *   {t: "P", vars: [[column, value]], given: [[column, value]], do: [[column, value]]}
 *       value is a symbol (string) or a constant (number, e.g. R_X = 0)
 *   {t: "sum", over: [symbol], body}
 *   {t: "prod", factors: [...]}
 *   {t: "frac", num, den}
 *   {t: "one"}
 *
 * Example: P(Y* = y | X* = x, R_X = 0, R_Y = 0) is
 *   P([["Y_star", "Y"]], [["X_star", "X"], ["R_X", 0], ["R_Y", 0]])
 * Python's amr_identifiability.estimand.evaluate computes these trees on a probability table.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const ONE = Object.freeze({ t: "one" });

  function P(vars, given = [], doSet = []) {
    return { t: "P", vars: norm(vars), given: norm(given), do: norm(doSet) };
  }

  function norm(list) {
    return list.map((x) => (Array.isArray(x) ? [x[0], x[1]] : [x, x]));
  }

  function sum(over, body) {
    over = [...over];
    if (!over.length) return body;
    if (body.t === "one") return body;
    if (body.t === "sum") return { t: "sum", over: [...over, ...body.over], body: body.body };
    return { t: "sum", over, body };
  }

  function prod(...factors) {
    const flat = [];
    for (const f of factors.flat()) {
      if (!f || f.t === "one") continue;
      if (f.t === "prod") flat.push(...f.factors);
      else flat.push(f);
    }
    if (!flat.length) return ONE;
    if (flat.length === 1) return flat[0];
    return { t: "prod", factors: flat };
  }

  function frac(num, den) {
    if (den.t === "one") return num;
    return { t: "frac", num, den };
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- inspection

  /** Symbols that are free (not bound by a sum). */
  function freeSymbols(e, bound = new Set()) {
    const out = new Set();
    const visit = (x, b) => {
      if (x.t === "P") for (const list of [x.vars, x.given, x.do]) for (const [, v] of list) if (typeof v === "string" && !b.has(v)) out.add(v);
      if (x.t === "sum") visit(x.body, new Set([...b, ...x.over]));
      if (x.t === "prod") x.factors.forEach((f) => visit(f, b));
      if (x.t === "frac") {
        visit(x.num, b);
        visit(x.den, b);
      }
    };
    visit(e, bound);
    return out;
  }

  function hasDo(e) {
    if (e.t === "P") return e.do.length > 0;
    if (e.t === "sum") return hasDo(e.body);
    if (e.t === "prod") return e.factors.some(hasDo);
    if (e.t === "frac") return hasDo(e.num) || hasDo(e.den);
    return false;
  }

  /** Every observed column the expression reads. */
  function columns(e, out = new Set()) {
    if (e.t === "P") for (const list of [e.vars, e.given, e.do]) for (const [c] of list) out.add(c);
    if (e.t === "sum") columns(e.body, out);
    if (e.t === "prod") e.factors.forEach((f) => columns(f, out));
    if (e.t === "frac") {
      columns(e.num, out);
      columns(e.den, out);
    }
    return out;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- rendering

  const STAR = MG.PROXY_SUFFIX;

  /** Column name in the paper's notation: Y_star -> Y*. */
  function colName(c) {
    return c.endsWith(STAR) ? c.slice(0, -STAR.length) + "*" : c;
  }

  /** A term entry: "Y*" when the column carries its own symbol, "R_X = 0" for a constant. */
  function entry([c, v], { values = false } = {}) {
    const name = colName(c);
    if (typeof v === "number") return `${name} = ${v}`;
    const base = c.endsWith(STAR) ? c.slice(0, -STAR.length) : c;
    if (v === base && !values) return name;
    return `${name} = ${v.toLowerCase ? v.toLowerCase() : v}`;
  }

  function toText(e, opts = {}) {
    switch (e.t) {
      case "one":
        return "1";
      case "P": {
        const given = [...e.given.map((x) => entry(x, opts))];
        const left = e.vars.map((x) => entry(x, opts)).join(", ");
        const doPart = e.do.length ? `do(${e.do.map((x) => entry(x, opts)).join(", ")})` : null;
        const right = [doPart, ...given].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
        return right ? `P(${left} | ${right})` : `P(${left})`;
      }
      case "sum":
        return `Σ_{${e.over.join(", ")}} ${wrap(e.body, opts, "sum")}`;
      case "prod":
        return e.factors.map((f) => wrap(f, opts, "prod")).join(" ");
      case "frac":
        return `${wrap(e.num, opts, "frac")} / ${wrap(e.den, opts, "frac")}`;
      default:
        throw new Error(`unknown expression ${e.t}`);
    }
  }

  function wrap(e, opts, ctx) {
    const s = toText(e, opts);
    if (e.t === "P" || e.t === "one") return s;
    if (ctx === "sum" && e.t === "prod") return s;
    if (ctx === "prod" && e.t === "sum") return `[${s}]`;
    return `(${s})`;
  }

  /** Plain-ASCII form for files and the PDF: "sum_{a} P(Y* | X*, R_X = 0)". */
  function toAscii(e) {
    return toText(e).replace(/Σ_/g, "sum_").replace(/·/g, "*");
  }

  /** LaTeX, for papers. */
  function toLatex(e) {
    const name = (c) => {
      const star = c.endsWith(STAR);
      let base = star ? c.slice(0, -STAR.length) : c;
      base = base.replace(/^R_(.+)$/, "R_{$1}").replace(/^([A-Za-z]+)(\d+)$/, "$1_{$2}");
      return star ? `${base}^{*}` : base;
    };
    const ent = ([c, v]) => (typeof v === "number" ? `${name(c)}=${v}` : name(c));
    const go = (x, ctx) => {
      switch (x.t) {
        case "one":
          return "1";
        case "P": {
          const left = x.vars.map(ent).join(", ");
          const right = [x.do.length ? `\\mathrm{do}(${x.do.map(ent).join(", ")})` : null, ...x.given.map(ent)].filter(Boolean).join(", ");
          return right ? `P(${left} \\mid ${right})` : `P(${left})`;
        }
        case "sum":
          return `\\sum_{${x.over.map((s) => name(s).toLowerCase()).join(", ")}} ${go(x.body, "sum")}`;
        case "prod":
          return x.factors.map((f) => (f.t === "sum" ? `\\left[${go(f)}\\right]` : go(f))).join("\\,");
        case "frac":
          return `\\frac{${go(x.num)}}{${go(x.den)}}`;
      }
      return "";
    };
    return go(e);
  }

  /** The closed-form estimator: each P term becomes a ratio of counts (Remark 1 of the paper). */
  function estimatorText(e) {
    const count = (list) => `#(${list.map((x) => entry(x)).join(", ")})`;
    const go = (x) => {
      switch (x.t) {
        case "one":
          return "1";
        case "P":
          return x.given.length ? `${count([...x.vars, ...x.given])} / ${count(x.given)}` : `${count(x.vars)} / N`;
        case "sum":
          return `Σ_{${x.over.join(", ")}} ${go(x.body)}`;
        case "prod":
          return x.factors.map((f) => `[${go(f)}]`).join(" · ");
        case "frac":
          return `(${go(x.num)}) / (${go(x.den)})`;
      }
      return "";
    };
    return go(e);
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- simplification

  const key = (e) => JSON.stringify(e);

  /** Symbols a term depends on (free in it). */
  function deps(e) {
    return freeSymbols(e);
  }

  /**
   * Algebraic tidying that never changes the value:
   *   Σ_A P(A | B) ... = ...           when the symbols A appear in no other factor and not in B
   *   Σ_S c · f = c · Σ_S f            factors that do not depend on S leave the sum
   *   (c · a) / (c · b) = a / b        common factors cancel
   *   x / 1 = x
   */
  function simplify(e) {
    const go = (x) => {
      switch (x.t) {
        case "sum": {
          const body = go(x.body);
          let over = x.over.slice();
          const factors = body.t === "prod" ? body.factors.slice() : [body];
          // Σ_A P(A | B) = 1 when the symbols A are summed, appear nowhere else, and every proxy in
          // A has its indicator fixed at 0 in B (a proxy also takes the value "missing")
          let changed = true;
          while (changed) {
            changed = false;
            for (let i = 0; i < factors.length; i++) {
              const f = factors[i];
              if (f.t !== "P" || f.do.length || !f.vars.length) continue;
              const outs = f.vars.map(([, v]) => v);
              if (!outs.every((v) => typeof v === "string" && over.includes(v))) continue;
              if (new Set(outs).size !== outs.length) continue;
              if (f.given.some(([, v]) => outs.includes(v))) continue;
              const proxyOk = f.vars.every(([c]) => !c.endsWith(STAR) || f.given.some(([g, v]) => g === MG.R_PREFIX + c.slice(0, -STAR.length) && v === 0));
              if (!proxyOk) continue;
              if (factors.some((g, j) => j !== i && outs.some((v) => deps(g).has(v)))) continue;
              factors.splice(i, 1);
              over = over.filter((v) => !outs.includes(v));
              changed = true;
              break;
            }
          }
          const used = new Set();
          for (const f of factors) for (const v of deps(f)) used.add(v);
          // a summed symbol that nothing uses would multiply by its number of values: keep as is
          if (over.some((v) => !used.has(v))) return { t: "sum", over: x.over, body };
          // factors that do not depend on the summed symbols leave the sum
          const outside = factors.filter((f) => ![...deps(f)].some((v) => over.includes(v)));
          const inside = factors.filter((f) => !outside.includes(f));
          return prod(...outside, over.length ? sum(over, prod(...inside)) : prod(...inside));
        }
        case "prod":
          return prod(...x.factors.map(go));
        case "frac": {
          const num = go(x.num), den = go(x.den);
          if (den.t === "one") return num;
          let nf = num.t === "prod" ? num.factors.slice() : [num];
          let df = den.t === "prod" ? den.factors.slice() : [den];
          for (let i = nf.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
            const j = df.findIndex((d) => key(d) === key(nf[i]));
            if (j >= 0) {
              nf.splice(i, 1);
              df.splice(j, 1);
            }
          }
          const n2 = prod(...nf), d2 = prod(...df);
          if (key(n2) === key(d2)) return ONE;
          return frac(n2, d2);
        }
        default:
          return x;
      }
    };
    let prev, cur = e;
    for (let k = 0; k < 6 && key(cur) !== prev; k++) {
      prev = key(cur);
      cur = go(cur);
    }
    return cur;
  }

  /** Rename summation symbols that shadow a symbol of an enclosing scope: X -> X′. */
  function tidy(e) {
    const go = (x, inScope) => {
      if (x.t === "sum") {
        const ren = {};
        const over = x.over.map((s) => {
          if (!inScope.has(s)) return s;
          let n = s + "′";
          while (inScope.has(n)) n += "′";
          ren[s] = n;
          return n;
        });
        let body = Object.keys(ren).length ? renameFree(x.body, ren) : x.body;
        return { ...x, over, body: go(body, new Set([...inScope, ...over])) };
      }
      if (x.t === "prod") return { ...x, factors: x.factors.map((f) => go(f, inScope)) };
      if (x.t === "frac") return { ...x, num: go(x.num, inScope), den: go(x.den, inScope) };
      return x;
    };
    return go(e, freeSymbols(e));
  }

  /** Rename free occurrences of symbols (bound ones are left alone). */
  function renameFree(e, map) {
    const go = (x, bound) => {
      const val = (v) => (typeof v === "string" && !bound.has(v) && map[v] !== undefined ? map[v] : v);
      if (x.t === "P") {
        const f = (list) => list.map(([c, v]) => [c, val(v)]);
        return { ...x, vars: f(x.vars), given: f(x.given), do: f(x.do) };
      }
      if (x.t === "sum") return { ...x, body: go(x.body, new Set([...bound, ...x.over])) };
      if (x.t === "prod") return { ...x, factors: x.factors.map((f) => go(f, bound)) };
      if (x.t === "frac") return { ...x, num: go(x.num, bound), den: go(x.den, bound) };
      return x;
    };
    return go(e, new Set());
  }

  /** The form shown to people: simplified, with shadowed sum variables renamed. */
  function present(e) {
    return tidy(simplify(e));
  }

  MG.expr = { P, sum, prod, frac, ONE, freeSymbols, hasDo, columns, toText, toAscii, toLatex, estimatorText, colName, simplify, tidy, present, renameFree };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/missing.js ---- */
/* Missing data: the graphical results of Mohan & Pearl (2021, JASA). Numbers are JASA numbers.
 *
 *   classify(graph)                 MCAR / v-MAR / MNAR (Section 2.2), with the deciding d-separation
 *   recover(graph, target, given)   is P(target | given) recoverable, and how:
 *       direct          every variable is fully observed
 *       Corollary 2     complete cases / available cases
 *       Corollary 1     v-MAR joint distribution
 *       Theorem 1       admissible ordered factorisation (Definition 2), summing over helper
 *                       variables if needed; helpers and conditioning sets may include
 *                       indicators, which are fully observed (as in Example 4)
 *       R-factorisation the joint as P(V*, R = 0) / P(R = 0 | V) (Section 3.2, Theorem 2),
 *                       each factor simplified by a d-separation, then marginalised
 *       Theorem 3       proof that it is NOT recoverable
 *   testability(graph)              Theorems 4-6 and Corollary 3
 *
 * Every recoverable answer is an estimand (MG.expr) built step by step from d-separations
 * that the graph implies; the tests evaluate each one numerically against the truth.
 * Recovery by intervention (Section 3.3) uses the ID algorithm and lives in MG.causal.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const E = () => MG.expr;
  const R = (v) => MG.R_PREFIX + v;

  /** Roles in the paper's notation. */
  function sets(graph) {
    return {
      Vo: new Set(graph.fullyObserved),
      Vm: new Set(graph.partiallyObserved),
      U: new Set(graph.latent),
      R: new Set(graph.indicators),
      Vstar: new Set(graph.proxies),
    };
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Section 2.2

  function classify(graph) {
    const { Vo, Vm, U, R: Rs } = sets(graph);
    if (!Rs.size) return { mechanism: "complete", text: "No variable is partially observed: there is no missing data." };
    const all = new Set([...Vm, ...Vo, ...U]);
    const sep = (X, Z) => MG.dsep.dSeparated(graph, X, Rs, Z);
    const mcar = new MG.Statement(all, Rs, []);
    const vmar = new MG.Statement(new Set([...Vm, ...U]), Rs, Vo);
    const out = { mcarStatement: mcar, vmarStatement: vmar };
    out.mcar = sep(all, []);
    out.vmar = Vm.size + U.size === 0 ? true : sep(new Set([...Vm, ...U]), Vo);
    out.mechanism = out.mcar ? "MCAR" : out.vmar ? "v-MAR" : "MNAR";
    // a witness: an open path that breaks the stronger condition
    const witness = (from, Z) => {
      for (const a of MG.sorted(from)) for (const r of MG.sorted(Rs)) {
        const { paths } = MG.dsep.paths(graph, a, r, Z, { onlyOpen: true, maxPaths: 1, maxLength: 30 });
        if (paths.length) return paths[0];
      }
      return null;
    };
    if (!out.mcar) out.mcarWitness = witness(all, []);
    if (!out.vmar) out.vmarWitness = witness(new Set([...Vm, ...U]), Vo);
    out.text = {
      MCAR: "Missingness is independent of every variable in the model (Vm ∪ Vo ∪ U ⊥⊥ R).",
      "v-MAR": "Given the fully observed variables, missingness is independent of the partially observed and latent ones (Vm ∪ U ⊥⊥ R | Vo).",
      MNAR: "Missingness depends on partially observed or latent variables even given the fully observed ones.",
    }[out.mechanism];
    return out;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers

  /** The observed column that carries a variable's value. */
  function column(graph, v) {
    return graph.partiallyObserved.has(v) ? v + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX : v;
  }

  /** Indicators that must be 0 to observe these variables. */
  function indicatorsOf(graph, vars) {
    return new Set([...vars].filter((v) => graph.partiallyObserved.has(v)).map(R));
  }

  function termOf(graph, vars, given, zeros) {
    return E().P(
      [...vars].map((v) => [column(graph, v), v]),
      [...MG.sorted(given).map((v) => [column(graph, v), v]), ...MG.sorted(zeros).filter((r) => !given.has(r)).map((r) => [r, 0])]
    );
  }

  function makeSep(graph) {
    const memo = new Map();
    return (X, Y, Z) => {
      X = new Set(X);
      Y = new Set(Y);
      Z = new Set(Z);
      for (const z of Z) {
        X.delete(z);
        Y.delete(z);
      }
      if (!X.size || !Y.size) return true;
      const k = MG.setKey(X) + "|" + MG.setKey(Y) + "|" + MG.setKey(Z);
      if (!memo.has(k)) memo.set(k, MG.dsep.dSeparated(graph, X, Y, Z));
      return memo.get(k);
    };
  }

  function subsetsBySize(items, maxSize = items.length) {
    const out = [];
    const rec = (start, acc) => {
      out.push(acc.slice());
      if (acc.length === maxSize) return;
      for (let i = start; i < items.length; i++) {
        acc.push(items[i]);
        rec(i + 1, acc);
        acc.pop();
      }
    };
    rec(0, []);
    return out.sort((a, b) => a.length - b.length);
  }

  /** Permutations in lexicographic order of positions, generated lazily. */
  function* permutations(items) {
    if (items.length <= 1) {
      yield items.slice();
      return;
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
      const rest = items.slice(0, i).concat(items.slice(i + 1));
      for (const p of permutations(rest)) yield [items[i], ...p];
    }
  }

  const ind = (x, y, z) => new MG.Statement([].concat(x), [...y], [...z]);

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Theorem 3 (non-recoverability)

  /**
   * Proof that P(target | given) is not recoverable, or null. For each partially observed X in
   * the target, Theorem 3 applies to P(X | Y) with Y = (target ∪ given) \ {X}; P(X | Y) is
   * computable from P(target | given) because given ⊆ Y, so the target is not recoverable either.
   */
  function theorem3(graph, target, given) {
    const adm = MG.ADMG.project(graph);
    const { Vm } = sets(graph);
    for (const X of MG.sorted(target)) {
      if (!Vm.has(X)) continue;
      const rx = R(X);
      const Y = new Set([...target, ...given].filter((v) => v !== X));
      if (adm.adjacent(X, rx)) {
        const how = adm.hasDirected(X, rx) ? `${X} → ${rx}` : adm.hasDirected(rx, X) ? `${rx} → ${X}` : `${X} ↔ ${rx}`;
        return { variable: X, kind: "neighbours", text: `${X} and ${rx} are neighbours (${how}${graph.hasEdge(X, rx) || graph.hasEdge(rx, X) ? "" : ", through latent variables"}), so P(${X}${Y.size ? " | " + MG.sorted(Y).join(", ") : ""}) is not recoverable (Theorem 3).` };
      }
      const paths = adm.colliderPaths(X, rx, Y);
      if (paths.length) {
        const p = paths[0];
        return { variable: X, kind: "collider path", path: p, text: `${X} and ${rx} are joined by ${p.join(" ⋯ ")}, a path whose inner nodes are all colliders in the conditioning set, so P(${X} | ${MG.sorted(Y).join(", ")}) is not recoverable (Theorem 3).` };
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Corollaries 1 and 2

  function corollary2(graph, target, given, sep) {
    const T = new Set(target), S = new Set(given);
    const { R: all } = sets(graph);
    const avail = indicatorsOf(graph, [...T, ...S]);
    const out = {};
    // (b) available cases: T ⊥⊥ (R_T, R_S) | S
    out.available = { statement: ind([...T], avail, S), holds: avail.size === 0 || sep(T, avail, S) };
    // (a) complete cases: T ⊥⊥ R | S, every indicator in the dataset
    out.complete = { statement: ind([...T], all, S), holds: all.size === 0 || sep(T, all, S) };
    if (out.available.holds) out.available.expr = termOf(graph, T, S, avail);
    if (out.complete.holds) out.complete.expr = termOf(graph, T, S, all);
    return out;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Theorem 1

  /**
   * An admissible ordered factorisation of P(T | S), summing over helper variables W if needed:
   *   P(T | S) = Σ_W Π_i P(Y_i | X_i),   X_i ⊆ {Y_{i+1}, ..., Y_n} ∪ S
   * with, for every factor,
   *   (a) Y_i ⊥⊥ ({Y_{i+1}, ..., Y_n} ∪ S) \ X_i | X_i   (the factor equals the chain-rule term)
   *   (b) Y_i ⊥⊥ (R_{Y_i}, R_{X_i}) | X_i                (Theorem 1: the factor is recoverable)
   * Returns the factorisation with the fewest helpers, or null.
   */
  function theorem1(graph, target, given, sep, opts = {}) {
    // fast pass: larger helper sets, but only the natural order (descendants first, so each
    // factor conditions on its causes); then the full search over orders with fewer helpers
    const topo = new Map(MG.ADMG.project(graph).topological().map((v, i) => [v, i]));
    const natural = (vars) => [vars.slice().sort((a, b) => (topo.get(b) ?? 0) - (topo.get(a) ?? 0))];
    // first pass: the helpers are a minimal set separating the target from its indicators,
    // which is exactly what the target's own factor needs
    const { Vo, Vm, R: Rs } = sets(graph);
    const T = [...target], S = [...given];
    const need = [...indicatorsOf(graph, T)];
    if (T.length === 1 && need.length === 1) {
      const allowed = new Set([...Vo, ...Vm, ...Rs].filter((v) => !T.includes(v) && v !== need[0]));
      const inc = S.filter((v) => allowed.has(v));
      let seps = [];
      try {
        seps = MG.separators.minimalDSeparators(graph, T[0], need[0], { allowed, included: inc, limit: 20 }).separators;
      } catch (e) {
        seps = [];
      }
      for (const Z of seps) {
        const W = [...Z].filter((v) => !S.includes(v));
        if (W.some((w) => Rs.has(w))) continue;
        const r = theorem1Search(graph, target, given, sep, { ...opts, fixedHelpers: W, maxVars: 14, orders: natural });
        if (r) return r;
      }
    }
    // the wider natural-order pass only at the top level; recursive sub-queries use the full search
    const wide = (opts.depth || 0) === 0 ? theorem1Search(graph, target, given, sep, { ...opts, maxHelpers: opts.maxHelpersNatural ?? 6, maxVars: 12, orders: natural, maxSubsets: 400, maxDepth: 0 }) : null;
    return wide || theorem1Search(graph, target, given, sep, opts);
  }

  function theorem1Search(graph, target, given, sep, { maxHelpers = 3, maxVars = 7, depth = 0, maxDepth = 2, orders = null, maxSubsets = Infinity, fixedHelpers = null } = {}) {
    const { Vo, Vm, R: Rs } = sets(graph);
    const T = MG.sorted(target), S = new Set(given);
    const used = new Set([...T, ...S]);
    const usedPartial = new Set([...used].filter((v) => Vm.has(v)));
    // helpers: substantive ancestors of the target and given variables, and indicators of
    // variables that are not themselves in the factorisation
    const anc = graph.ancestralClosure(new Set([...used, ...indicatorsOf(graph, used)]));
    // a partially observed variable and its own indicator can never both be free symbols:
    // the variable is only observed where its indicator is 0
    const pool = MG.sorted([...anc].filter((v) => !used.has(v) && (Vo.has(v) || (Vm.has(v) && !used.has(R(v))) || (Rs.has(v) && !usedPartial.has(v.slice(2))))));
    const subMemo = new Map();
    // a factor whose condition (b) fails may still be recoverable on its own (Example 4, G1)
    const subRecover = (Yi, Xi) => {
      const k = Yi + "|" + Xi.join(",");
      if (!subMemo.has(k)) {
        let r = null;
        if (depth < maxDepth) {
          try {
            r = recoverRaw(graph, [Yi], Xi, { maxHelpers, maxVars, depth: depth + 1, maxDepth });
          } catch (e) {
            r = null;
          }
        }
        subMemo.set(k, r && r.recoverable === true ? r : null);
      }
      return subMemo.get(k);
    };
    let nSubsets = 0;
    for (const W of fixedHelpers ? [MG.sorted(fixedHelpers)] : subsetsBySize(pool, maxHelpers)) {
      if (++nSubsets > maxSubsets) break;
      if (T.length + W.length > maxVars) continue;
      // an indicator helper R_v cannot sit with v in the same factorisation
      if (W.some((w) => Rs.has(w) && W.includes(w.slice(2)))) continue;
      const vars = [...T, ...W];
      let tried = 0;
      for (const order of orders ? orders(vars) : permutations(vars)) {
        if (++tried > 5040) break;
        const steps = [];
        let ok = true;
        for (let i = 0; i < order.length && ok; i++) {
          const Yi = order[i];
          const C = MG.sorted([...order.slice(i + 1), ...S]);
          let found = null;
          const fallback = [];
          for (const Xi of subsetsBySize(C, Math.min(C.length, 8))) {
            const Xs = new Set(Xi);
            const rest = C.filter((c) => !Xs.has(c));
            const need = indicatorsOf(graph, [Yi, ...Xi]);
            if (need.has(Yi)) continue; // Y_i is the indicator of a variable it is conditioned on
            if (Vm.has(Yi) && Xs.has(R(Yi))) continue; // Y_i conditioned on its own indicator
            if (Xi.some((x) => Rs.has(x) && Xi.includes(x.slice(2)))) continue;
            const a = rest.length === 0 || sep([Yi], rest, Xs);
            if (!a) continue;
            const b = need.size === 0 || sep([Yi], need, Xs);
            if (!b) {
              fallback.push({ Xi, rest });
              continue;
            }
            found = { Xi, need, a: rest.length ? ind(Yi, rest, Xs) : null, b: need.size ? ind(Yi, need, Xs) : null };
            break;
          }
          if (!found) {
            for (const { Xi, rest } of fallback) {
              const sub = subRecover(Yi, Xi);
              if (sub) {
                found = { Xi, need: new Set(), a: rest.length ? ind(Yi, rest, new Set(Xi)) : null, b: null, sub };
                break;
              }
            }
          }
          if (!found) ok = false;
          else steps.push({ variable: Yi, given: found.Xi, zeros: MG.sorted(found.need), validity: found.a, recoverability: found.b, sub: found.sub || null });
        }
        if (!ok) continue;
        const factors = steps.map((s) => (s.sub ? s.sub.expr : termOf(graph, [s.variable], new Set(s.given), new Set(s.zeros))));
        const expr = E().sum(W, E().prod(...factors));
        return { order, helpers: W, steps, expr };
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- R-factorisation (Section 3.2)

  /**
   * P(V) = P(V*, Vo, R = 0) / P(R = 0 | V), with P(R = 0 | V) = Π_i P(R_i = 0 | V, R_<i = 0)
   * (chain rule in some order of the indicators). Each factor is reduced to P(R_i = 0 | S_i)
   * by a d-separation R_i ⊥⊥ (V ∪ R_<i) \ S_i | S_i, and is observable when the indicators of
   * the partially observed variables in S_i can be set to 0 (they are in R_<i, or adding them
   * keeps the separation). Theorem 2's formula (4) is the special case with no edges between
   * indicators.
   */
  const rfCache = new WeakMap();

  function rFactorisation(graph, sep, opts = {}) {
    if (!rfCache.has(graph)) rfCache.set(graph, rFactorisationSearch(graph, sep, opts));
    return rfCache.get(graph);
  }

  function rFactorisationSearch(graph, sep) {
    const { Vo, Vm, R: Rs } = sets(graph);
    const V = MG.sorted([...Vo, ...Vm]);
    const Rlist = MG.sorted(Rs);
    if (!Rlist.length || Rlist.length > 14) return null;
    // a factor depends only on the indicator and the set of indicators before it: memoise it
    const memo = new Map();
    const factorFor = (Ri, before) => {
      const k = Ri + "|" + before.slice().sort().join(",");
      if (memo.has(k)) return memo.get(k);
      const pool = [...V, ...before];
      // greedy minimal S: drop partially observed variables first, then indicators, then the rest
      const rank = (n) => (Vm.has(n) ? 0 : Rs.has(n) ? 1 : 2);
      let S = new Set(pool);
      for (const n of pool.slice().sort((a, b) => rank(a) - rank(b) || (a < b ? -1 : 1))) {
        const trial = new Set(S);
        trial.delete(n);
        const rest = pool.filter((q) => !trial.has(q));
        if (sep([Ri], rest, trial)) S = trial;
      }
      let f = null;
      const need = indicatorsOf(graph, [...S].filter((n) => Vm.has(n)));
      if (!need.has(Ri)) {
        const extraBefore = [...need].filter((r) => before.includes(r) && !S.has(r));
        const extraAfter = [...need].filter((r) => !before.includes(r));
        const cond = new Set([...S, ...extraBefore]);
        const restOfPool = pool.filter((q) => !S.has(q));
        if (!extraAfter.length || sep([Ri], extraAfter, cond)) {
          f = {
            indicator: Ri,
            given: MG.sorted([...S].filter((n) => !Rs.has(n))),
            zeros: MG.sorted(new Set([...[...S].filter((n) => Rs.has(n)), ...need])),
            drop: restOfPool.length ? ind(Ri, restOfPool, S) : null,
            add: extraAfter.length ? ind(Ri, extraAfter, cond) : null,
          };
        } else {
          // Appendix A.2: recover the factor as a causal query
          f = causalFactor(graph, Ri, S, before);
        }
      }
      memo.set(k, f);
      return f;
    };
    // dynamic programming over sets of indicators already placed in the order
    const n = Rlist.length;
    const depth = new Map(Rlist.map((r) => [r, [...graph.ancestors(r)].filter((a) => Rs.has(a)).length]));
    const byDepth = Rlist.map((r, i) => i).sort((a, b) => depth.get(Rlist[a]) - depth.get(Rlist[b]) || a - b);
    const reach = new Map([[0, []]]); // mask -> order reaching it
    const queue = [0];
    while (queue.length) {
      const mask = queue.shift();
      const order = reach.get(mask);
      if (mask === (1 << n) - 1) {
        const factors = order.map((i, k) => factorFor(Rlist[i], order.slice(0, k).map((j) => Rlist[j])));
        const num = E().P([...V.map((v) => [column(graph, v), v]), ...Rlist.map((r) => [r, 0])]);
        const den = E().prod(...factors.map((f) => f.expr || E().P([[f.indicator, 0]], [...f.given.map((v) => [column(graph, v), v]), ...f.zeros.map((r) => [r, 0])])));
        return { order: order.map((i) => Rlist[i]), factors, expr: E().frac(num, den), V };
      }
      for (const i of byDepth) {
        if (mask & (1 << i)) continue;
        const next = mask | (1 << i);
        if (reach.has(next)) continue;
        const before = order.map((j) => Rlist[j]);
        if (factorFor(Rlist[i], before)) {
          reach.set(next, [...order, i]);
          queue.push(next);
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  /** One R-factorisation factor P(R_i = 0 | S) recovered as a causal query (Appendix A.2). */
  function causalFactor(graph, Ri, S, before) {
    if (!MG.causal) return null;
    const vars = [...S].filter((n) => !graph.indicators.has(n));
    const Rin = [...S].filter((n) => graph.indicators.has(n));
    if (Rin.length) return null; // conditioning on other indicators is not handled here
    const Rset = MG.sorted(indicatorsOf(graph, vars.filter((v) => graph.partiallyObserved.has(v))));
    const anc = graph.ancestors(Ri);
    if (Rset.some((r) => anc.has(r) || r === Ri)) return null;
    const r = MG.causal.interventionRoute(graph, [Ri], [], vars, { only: Rset });
    if (!r) return null;
    const expr = MG.expr.renameFree(r.expr, { [Ri]: 0 });
    return { indicator: Ri, given: MG.sorted(vars), zeros: [], expr, viaDo: Rset, trace: r.trace, drop: null, add: null };
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the whole question

  /**
   * Is P(target | given) recoverable from the observed-data distribution P(V*, Vo, R)?
   * target, given: substantive variables (fully or partially observed); given may include indicators.
   * Returns {recoverable: true | false | null, method, expr, steps, ...}.
   */
  const answerCache = new WeakMap();

  function recover(graph, target, given = [], opts = {}) {
    const r = recoverRaw(graph, target, given, opts);
    if (!opts.depth && r.expr) r.expr = MG.expr.present(r.expr);
    return r;
  }

  function recoverRaw(graph, target, given = [], opts = {}) {
    if (!answerCache.has(graph)) answerCache.set(graph, new Map());
    const cache = answerCache.get(graph);
    const key = JSON.stringify([MG.sorted(new Set(target)), MG.sorted(new Set(given)), opts.depth || 0, opts.maxDepth ?? 2, !!opts.ignoreTheorem3]);
    if (!cache.has(key)) cache.set(key, recoverUncached(graph, target, given, opts));
    return { ...cache.get(key) };
  }

  function recoverUncached(graph, target, given = [], opts = {}) {
    target = MG.sorted(new Set(target));
    given = MG.sorted(new Set(given));
    graph.require([...target, ...given]);
    const { Vo, Vm, R: Rs, U } = sets(graph);
    for (const v of [...target, ...given]) {
      if (U.has(v)) throw new Error(`${v} is latent; only recorded and partially observed variables can be recovered`);
      if (graph.proxies.has(v)) throw new Error(`${v} is a proxy; ask about the variable ${v.slice(0, -MG.PROXY_SUFFIX.length)} instead`);
    }
    for (const v of target) if (Rs.has(v)) throw new Error(`${v} is an indicator; it is fully observed, so its distribution is directly estimable`);
    const overlap = target.filter((v) => given.includes(v));
    if (overlap.length) throw new Error(`${overlap.join(", ")} is on both sides`);
    if (!target.length) throw new Error("the target needs at least one variable");
    const sep = makeSep(graph);
    const T = new Set(target), S = new Set(given);
    const out = { target, given, query: queryText(target, given), attempts: [] };
    const clash = [...T, ...S].filter((v) => Vm.has(v) && S.has(R(v)));
    if (clash.length)
      return { ...out, recoverable: null, method: null, reason: `The query conditions on ${clash.map(R).join(", ")} as a variable while asking about ${clash.join(", ")}, which is only observed where its indicator is 0. Ask about the variable with its indicator fixed instead.` };

    if ([...T, ...S].every((v) => Vo.has(v) || Rs.has(v))) {
      return { ...out, recoverable: true, method: "direct", reason: "Every variable in the query is fully observed.", expr: termOf(graph, T, S, new Set()) };
    }

    const proof = theorem3(graph, T, S);
    if (proof && !opts.ignoreTheorem3) return { ...out, recoverable: false, method: "Theorem 3", reason: proof.text, proof };

    const c2 = corollary2(graph, T, S, sep);
    out.corollary2 = c2;
    if (c2.complete.holds) return { ...out, recoverable: true, method: "Corollary 2(a)", reason: `Complete cases suffice: ${c2.complete.statement.pretty()}.`, expr: c2.complete.expr, completeCases: true, availableCases: true };
    if (c2.available.holds) return { ...out, recoverable: true, method: "Corollary 2(b)", reason: `Available cases suffice: ${c2.available.statement.pretty()}.`, expr: c2.available.expr, availableCases: true, completeCases: false };

    // simplest first: Theorem 1 with directly recoverable factors, then R-factorisation, then
    // Theorem 1 with factors that are themselves recovered (as in Example 4)
    const depth0 = opts.depth || 0;
    const t1 = theorem1(graph, T, S, sep, { ...opts, maxDepth: depth0 });
