    if (t1) return { ...out, recoverable: true, method: "Theorem 1", reason: "An admissible ordered factorisation recovers every factor.", expr: t1.expr, factorisation: t1, completeCases: false, availableCases: false };

    const rf = [...S].every((v) => Vo.has(v) || Vm.has(v)) ? rFactorisation(graph, sep, opts) : null;
    if (rf) {
      const V = rf.V;
      const outside = V.filter((v) => !T.has(v) && !S.has(v));
      const joint = E().sum(outside, rf.expr);
      const expr = S.size ? E().frac(joint, E().sum([...T, ...outside], rf.expr)) : joint;
      return { ...out, recoverable: true, method: "R-factorisation", reason: "The joint distribution is recovered by R-factorisation, then marginalised.", expr, rfactorisation: rf, completeCases: false, availableCases: false };
    }

    const t1r = theorem1(graph, T, S, sep, opts);
    // a single factor recovered on its own is that recovery, not a factorisation
    if (t1r && t1r.steps.length === 1 && !t1r.helpers.length && t1r.steps[0].sub) {
      const sub = t1r.steps[0].sub;
      return { ...out, recoverable: true, method: sub.method, reason: sub.reason, expr: sub.expr, trace: sub.trace, completeCases: false, availableCases: false };
    }
    if (t1r) return { ...out, recoverable: true, method: "Theorem 1", reason: "An admissible ordered factorisation in which some factors are recovered on their own.", expr: t1r.expr, factorisation: t1r, completeCases: false, availableCases: false };

    // recovery by intervention on the indicators (Section 3.3, Example 6)
    if (MG.causal && [...S].every((v) => !Rs.has(v))) {
      const viaR = MG.causal.interventionRoute(graph, [...T], [], [...S]);
      if (viaR) return { ...out, recoverable: true, method: "intervention", reason: `Intervening on ${viaR.R.join(", ")} turns the query into an identification problem that the ID algorithm solves (Section 3.3).`, expr: viaR.expr, trace: viaR.trace, completeCases: false, availableCases: false };
    }
    // P(T | S) = P(T, S) / P(S) when both are recoverable
    const depth = opts.depth || 0;
    if (S.size && depth < (opts.maxDepth ?? 2)) {
      const sub = { ...opts, depth: depth + 1, ignoreTheorem3: false };
      const both = recover(graph, [...T, ...S], [], sub);
      const marg = both.recoverable === true ? recover(graph, [...S], [], sub) : null;
      if (both.recoverable === true && marg && marg.recoverable === true) {
        return { ...out, recoverable: true, method: "ratio", reason: `P(${[...T, ...S].join(", ")}) and P(${[...S].join(", ")}) are both recoverable, so their ratio is.`, expr: E().frac(both.expr, marg.expr), parts: [both, marg], completeCases: false, availableCases: false };
      }
    }
    return { ...out, recoverable: null, method: null, reason: "None of the graphical criteria in Sections 3.1–3.2 recovers this query, and Theorem 3 does not prove it non-recoverable. It may still be recoverable through intervention (Section 3.3) or with assumptions beyond the graph (Section 3.4)." };
  }

  /** Theorem 2's own statement, for the joint distribution: its precondition and conditions. */
  function theorem2(graph) {
    const adm = MG.ADMG.project(graph);
    const { Vo, Vm, R: Rs } = sets(graph);
    const rEdges = [];
    for (const a of Rs) for (const b of Rs) if (a < b && adm.adjacent(a, b)) rEdges.push([a, b]);
    const violations = [];
    for (const X of MG.sorted(Vm)) {
      const rx = R(X);
      if (adm.adjacent(X, rx)) violations.push({ variable: X, condition: 1, text: `${X} and ${rx} are neighbours` });
      else {
        const p = adm.colliderPaths(X, rx, new Set([...Vm, ...Vo]));
        if (p.length) violations.push({ variable: X, condition: 2, path: p[0], text: `${X} and ${rx} are joined by ${p[0].join(" ⋯ ")}, all inner nodes colliders` });
      }
    }
    return {
      applies: rEdges.length === 0,
      edgesBetweenIndicators: rEdges,
      violations,
      recoverable: rEdges.length === 0 ? violations.length === 0 : violations.length ? false : null,
    };
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Section 4: testability

  /**
   * Untestable claims (Theorem 4: X ⊥⊥ R_X | Z, W, R_Z with X, Z partially observed, W fully
   * observed) that the graph makes, and the testability of the v-MAR and MCAR claims
   * (Theorem 6, Corollary 3) with the tests they imply.
   */
  function testability(graph, catalogue = new MG.Catalogue(graph)) {
    const { Vo, Vm, R: Rs } = sets(graph);
    const untestable = [];
    for (const X of MG.sorted(Vm)) {
      const p = catalogue.pair(X, R(X));
      if (!p) continue;
      for (const z of p.separators) {
        const zs = [...z];
        const partial = zs.filter((n) => Vm.has(n));
        // Theorem 4's form: the other conditioning variables are partially observed with their
        // indicators present, or fully observed
        const form = zs.every((n) => Vo.has(n) || Vm.has(n) || Rs.has(n)) && partial.every((n) => z.has(R(n)));
        untestable.push({ statement: new MG.Statement([X], [R(X)], z), theorem4: form });
      }
    }
    const k = Vm.size;
    const vmarTests = [];
    const mcarTests = [];
    for (const A of MG.sorted(Vm)) for (const B of MG.sorted(Vm)) if (A !== B) {
      vmarTests.push(new MG.Statement([A], [R(B)], [...Vo, R(A)]));
      mcarTests.push(new MG.Statement([A], [R(B)], [R(A)]));
    }
    for (const C of MG.sorted(Vo)) for (const A of MG.sorted(Vm)) mcarTests.push(new MG.Statement([C], [R(A)], []));
    return {
      untestable,
      vmar: { testable: k > 1, tests: vmarTests, text: k > 1 ? `With ${k} partially observed variables, v-MAR is testable (Theorem 6).` : "With one partially observed variable, v-MAR cannot be tested (Theorem 6)." },
      mcar: { testable: k > 0 && k + Vo.size >= 2, tests: mcarTests, text: k + Vo.size >= 2 ? "MCAR is testable (Corollary 3)." : "MCAR cannot be tested (Corollary 3)." },
    };
  }

  function queryText(target, given) {
    return given.length ? `P(${target.join(", ")} | ${given.join(", ")})` : `P(${target.join(", ")})`;
  }

  MG.missing = { sets, classify, recover, theorem1, theorem2, theorem3, rFactorisation, corollary2, testability, column, indicatorsOf, queryText, makeSep };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/causal.js ---- */
/* Causal effects: the ID and IDC algorithms, the do-calculus, adjustment, front-door and
 * instrumental variables.
 *
 *   id(admg, Y, X)            Shpitser & Pearl (2006) ID: P(Y | do(X)) as an estimand, or the hedge
 *                             that makes it non-identifiable. Complete for identification.
 *   idc(admg, Y, X, Z)        conditional version: P(Y | do(X), Z)
 *   doRule(graph, n, ...)     checks Rule 1, 2 or 3 of the do-calculus and says which graph it used
 *   adjustment(graph, X, Y)   generalised adjustment criterion (Shpitser et al. 2010; Perković et
 *                             al. 2018): every minimal adjustment set, a valid set if any exists,
 *                             and the optimal set when there are no latent variables
 *   frontDoor, instruments    Pearl's front-door criterion; conditional instruments
 *   effect(graph, X, Y, Z)    everything above for one query, plus Section 3.5 of Mohan & Pearl
 *                             (2021) when variables are partially observed
 *
 * Estimands are MG.expr trees; the tests evaluate them against truncated factorisation.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const E = () => MG.expr;

  class NotIdentifiable extends Error {
    constructor(hedge, trace) {
      super(`not identifiable: hedge over {${MG.sorted(hedge.F).join(", ")}} and {${MG.sorted(hedge.Fprime).join(", ")}}`);
      this.hedge = hedge;
      this.trace = trace;
    }
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- distributions inside ID

  /**
   * A distribution over `scope` given as an expression (null for the observed joint itself).
   * valueOf(v) is the symbol or constant carrying v's value.
   */
  function baseDist(scope) {
    return { scope: new Set(scope), expr: null };
  }

  function marginal(dist, keep, ctx) {
    keep = MG.sorted(keep);
    if (!dist.expr) return E().P(keep.map((v) => ctx.entry(v)));
    const drop = MG.sorted([...dist.scope].filter((v) => !keep.includes(v)));
    return E().sum(drop.map(ctx.sym), dist.expr);
  }

  function conditional(dist, v, prior, ctx) {
    prior = MG.sorted([...prior].filter((p) => dist.scope.has(p) || !dist.expr));
    if (!dist.expr) return E().P([ctx.entry(v)], prior.map((p) => ctx.entry(p)));
    const inScope = prior.filter((p) => dist.scope.has(p));
    const num = marginal(dist, [...inScope, v], ctx);
    const den = marginal(dist, inScope, ctx);
    return E().frac(num, den);
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- ID

  /**
   * P(Y | do(X)) in the ADMG `G` (observed nodes only; bidirected edges for latent causes).
   * opts.symbol(v): the symbol of v's value (default v); opts.constants: {v: value} for
   * interventions at fixed values (do(R = 0)).
   */
  function id(G, Y, X, opts = {}) {
    const constants = opts.constants || {};
    const symbolOf = opts.symbol || ((v) => v);
    const column = opts.column || ((v) => v);
    // values stay symbolic while the estimand is built (ID sums over some intervened variables
    // after they leave the intervention set, at line 2); fixed values are substituted at the end
    const ctx = {
      sym: (v) => symbolOf(v),
      entry: (v) => [column(v), symbolOf(v)],
    };
    const order = G.topological();
    const trace = [];
    let expr = idRec(new Set(Y), new Set(X), baseDist(G.ids), G, order, ctx, trace, 0);
    const fixed = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(constants).map(([v, c]) => [symbolOf(v), c]));
    if (Object.keys(fixed).length) expr = renameSymbols(expr, fixed);
    return { expr, trace };
  }

  function idRec(Y, X, dist, G, order, ctx, trace, depth) {
    const V = new Set(G.ids);
    const fmt = (s) => `{${MG.sorted(s).join(", ")}}`;
    const note = (line, text) => trace.push({ line, depth, text });
    const priorOf = (v) => {
      const i = order.indexOf(v);
      return order.slice(0, i).filter((u) => V.has(u));
    };

    // line 1
    if (!X.size) {
      note(1, `No intervention left: sum the distribution over ${fmt([...V].filter((v) => !Y.has(v)))}.`);
      return marginal(dist, Y, ctx);
    }
    // line 2
    const anY = G.ancestors(Y);
    if (anY.size < V.size) {
      note(2, `Only ancestors of ${fmt(Y)} matter: drop ${fmt([...V].filter((v) => !anY.has(v)))}.`);
      const sub = G.sub(anY);
      const d2 = { scope: new Set(anY), expr: dist.expr ? marginal(dist, anY, ctx) : null };
      return idRec(Y, new Set([...X].filter((x) => anY.has(x))), d2, sub, order, ctx, trace, depth + 1);
    }
    // line 3
    const anYnoX = G.withoutIncoming(X).ancestors(Y);
    const W = [...V].filter((v) => !X.has(v) && !anYnoX.has(v));
    if (W.length) {
      note(3, `Intervening on ${fmt(W)} changes nothing (they are not ancestors of ${fmt(Y)} once ${fmt(X)} is fixed): add them to the intervention, at any value.`);
      const r3 = idRec(Y, new Set([...X, ...W]), dist, G, order, ctx, trace, depth + 1);
      // the result does not depend on the values given to W; fix them at 0
      return renameSymbols(r3, Object.fromEntries(W.map((w) => [ctx.sym(w), 0])));
    }
    // line 4
    const rest = G.sub([...V].filter((v) => !X.has(v)));
    const C = rest.districts();
    if (C.length > 1) {
      note(4, `G without ${fmt(X)} splits into districts ${C.map(fmt).join(", ")}: identify each, multiply, and sum over ${fmt([...V].filter((v) => !Y.has(v) && !X.has(v)))}.`);
      const parts = C.map((S) => idRec(S, new Set([...V].filter((v) => !S.has(v))), dist, G, order, ctx, trace, depth + 1));
      return E().sum([...V].filter((v) => !Y.has(v) && !X.has(v)).map(ctx.sym), E().prod(...parts));
    }
    const S = C[0];
    const CG = G.districts();
    // line 5
    if (CG.length === 1 && CG[0].size === V.size) {
      note(5, `Hedge: ${fmt(V)} is a single district and so is ${fmt(S)} inside it. The effect is not identifiable.`);
      throw new NotIdentifiable({ F: V, Fprime: S }, trace);
    }
    // line 6
    const same = CG.find((c) => MG.setEq(c, S));
    if (same) {
      note(6, `${fmt(S)} is a district of G: P(${fmt(S)} | do(rest)) is a product of conditionals in topological order.`);
      const factors = order.filter((v) => S.has(v)).map((v) => conditional(dist, v, priorOf(v), ctx));
      return E().sum([...S].filter((v) => !Y.has(v)).map(ctx.sym), E().prod(...factors));
    }
    // line 7
    const bigger = CG.find((c) => MG.subset(S, c));
    if (bigger) {
      note(7, `${fmt(S)} sits inside the district ${fmt(bigger)}: restrict to it and continue with the intervention ${fmt([...X].filter((x) => bigger.has(x)))}.`);
      const factors = order.filter((v) => bigger.has(v)).map((v) => conditional(dist, v, priorOf(v), ctx));
      const d7 = { scope: new Set(bigger), expr: E().prod(...factors) };
      return idRec(Y, new Set([...X].filter((x) => bigger.has(x))), d7, G.sub(bigger), order, ctx, trace, depth + 1);
    }
    throw new Error("ID: no line applies (this cannot happen in an ADMG)");
  }

  /** P(Y | do(X), Z): Shpitser & Pearl (2006b) IDC. */
  function idc(G, Y, X, Z, opts = {}) {
    Y = new Set(Y);
    X = new Set(X);
    Z = new Set(Z);
    const trace = [];
    for (const z of MG.sorted(Z)) {
      const others = new Set([...Z].filter((v) => v !== z));
      const H = G.withoutIncoming(X).withoutOutgoing([z]);
      if (H.separated(Y, [z], new Set([...X, ...others]))) {
        trace.push({ line: "IDC", depth: 0, text: `Rule 2: ${z} can be moved into the intervention, since ${MG.sorted(Y).join(", ")} ⊥⊥ ${z} | ${[...X, ...others].join(", ") || "∅"} once edges into the intervention and out of ${z} are removed.` });
        const r = idc(G, Y, new Set([...X, z]), others, opts);
        return { expr: r.expr, trace: [...trace, ...r.trace] };
      }
    }
    const joint = id(G, new Set([...Y, ...Z]), X, opts);
    if (!Z.size) return { expr: joint.expr, trace: [...trace, ...joint.trace] };
    const symbolOf = opts.symbol || ((v) => v);
    trace.push({ line: "IDC", depth: 0, text: `Identify P(${[...Y, ...Z].join(", ")} | do(${[...X].join(", ")})) and divide by its sum over ${[...Y].join(", ")}.` });
    return { expr: E().frac(joint.expr, E().sum([...Y].map(symbolOf), joint.expr)), trace: [...trace, ...joint.trace] };
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- do-calculus

  /**
   * Check one rule of the do-calculus on the DAG (latent variables allowed).
   *   rule 1: P(y | do(x), z, w) = P(y | do(x), w)        if Y ⊥⊥ Z | X, W in G_{X̄}
   *   rule 2: P(y | do(x), do(z), w) = P(y | do(x), z, w) if Y ⊥⊥ Z | X, W in G_{X̄ Z̲}
   *   rule 3: P(y | do(x), do(z), w) = P(y | do(x), w)    if Y ⊥⊥ Z | X, W in G_{X̄ Z(W)̄}
   * with Z(W) the nodes of Z that are not ancestors of W in G_{X̄}.
   */
  function doRule(graph, rule, { Y, X = [], Z, W = [] }) {
    Y = new Set(Y); X = new Set(X); Z = new Set(Z); W = new Set(W);
    const cut = (g, intoCut, outCut) => {
      const edges = g.edges.filter(([a, b]) => !intoCut.has(b) && !outCut.has(a));
      return new MG.Graph({ nodes: g.ids.map((id) => g.meta.get(id)), edges, latent: g.latent });
    };
    let H, label;
    if (rule === 1) {
      H = cut(graph, X, new Set());
      label = "G with edges into X removed";
    } else if (rule === 2) {
      H = cut(graph, X, Z);
      label = "G with edges into X and out of Z removed";
    } else {
      const gx = cut(graph, X, new Set());
      const anW = gx.ancestralClosure(W);
      const ZW = new Set([...Z].filter((z) => !anW.has(z)));
      H = cut(graph, new Set([...X, ...ZW]), new Set());
      label = `G with edges into X and into {${MG.sorted(ZW).join(", ")}} removed`;
    }
    const cond = new Set([...X, ...W]);
    const holds = MG.dsep.dSeparated(H, Y, Z, cond);
    return { rule, holds, graph: H, label, statement: new MG.Statement(Y, Z, cond) };
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- adjustment

  /** Minimal separators between node sets, by contracting each set in the moral graph. */
  function minimalSetSeparators(graph, X, Y, { allowed, included = [], limit = 200 } = {}) {
    X = new Set(X); Y = new Set(Y);
    const inc = new Set(included);
    const anc = graph.ancestralClosure(new Set([...X, ...Y, ...inc]));
    const moral = MG.separators.moralGraph(graph, anc);
    let adj = new Map();
    for (const [n, nb] of moral) if (!inc.has(n)) adj.set(n, new Set([...nb].filter((m) => !inc.has(m))));
    const forbidden = [...adj.keys()].filter((n) => !allowed.has(n) && !X.has(n) && !Y.has(n));
    adj = MG.separators.eliminate(adj, forbidden);
    const contract = (a, S, name) => {
      const out = new Map();
      const nb = new Set();
      for (const s of S) if (a.has(s)) for (const m of a.get(s)) if (!S.has(m)) nb.add(m);
      for (const [n, ns] of a) {
        if (S.has(n)) continue;
        const set = new Set([...ns].filter((m) => !S.has(m)));
        if (nb.has(n)) set.add(name);
        out.set(n, set);
      }
      out.set(name, nb);
      return out;
    };
    adj = contract(adj, X, "\u0000X");
    adj = contract(adj, Y, "\u0000Y");
    const { separators, truncated } = MG.separators.minimalVertexSeparators(adj, "\u0000X", "\u0000Y", limit);
    return { separators: separators.map((s) => new Set([...s, ...inc])), truncated };
  }

  function adjustment(graph, X, Y) {
    X = new Set(X); Y = new Set(Y);
    const gNoInX = new MG.Graph({ nodes: graph.ids.map((i) => graph.meta.get(i)), edges: graph.edges.filter(([, b]) => !X.has(b)), latent: graph.latent });
    const gNoOutX = new MG.Graph({ nodes: graph.ids.map((i) => graph.meta.get(i)), edges: graph.edges.filter(([a]) => !X.has(a)), latent: graph.latent });
    // proper causal paths: nodes that are descendants of X in G_{X̄} (excluding X) and ancestors of Y in G_{X̲}
    const deX = new Set();
    for (const x of X) for (const d of gNoInX.descendants(x)) if (!X.has(d)) deX.add(d);
    const anY = gNoOutX.ancestralClosure(Y);
    const pcp = new Set([...deX].filter((v) => anY.has(v)));
    const dpcp = new Set();
    for (const v of pcp) {
      dpcp.add(v);
      for (const d of graph.descendants(v)) dpcp.add(d);
    }
    const forbidden = new Set([...dpcp, ...X]);
    // proper back-door graph: remove the first edge of every proper causal path
    const pbd = new MG.Graph({ nodes: graph.ids.map((i) => graph.meta.get(i)), edges: graph.edges.filter(([a, b]) => !(X.has(a) && pcp.has(b))), latent: graph.latent });
    const allowed = new Set(graph.ids.filter((v) => !graph.latent.has(v) && !forbidden.has(v) && !Y.has(v)));
    const isValid = (Z) => [...Z].every((z) => allowed.has(z)) && MG.dsep.dSeparated(pbd, X, Y, Z);
    const canonical = new Set([...graph.ancestralClosure(new Set([...X, ...Y]))].filter((v) => allowed.has(v)));
    const exists = isValid(canonical);
    let minimal = [];
    let truncated = false;
    if (exists) {
      const r = minimalSetSeparators(pbd, X, Y, { allowed });
      minimal = r.separators.filter(isValid).sort(MG.compareSets);
      truncated = r.truncated;
    }
    let optimal = null;
    if (exists && !graph.latent.size) {
      // O-set (Henckel, Perković & Maathuis 2022): parents of the causal nodes, minus the forbidden set
      const cn = new Set([...pcp]);
      const O = new Set();
      for (const c of cn) for (const p of graph.parents(c)) if (!forbidden.has(p)) O.add(p);
      if (isValid(O)) optimal = O;
    }
    return { exists, canonical: exists ? canonical : null, minimal, truncated, optimal, forbidden, causalNodes: pcp, isValid, backdoorGraph: pbd };
  }

  /** Σ_z P(y | x, z) P(z): the adjustment formula. */
  function adjustmentFormula(X, Y, Z, column = (v) => v) {
    const ent = (v) => [column(v), v];
    const z = MG.sorted(Z);
    return E().sum(z, E().prod(E().P(MG.sorted(Y).map(ent), [...MG.sorted(X), ...z].map(ent)), z.length ? E().P(z.map(ent)) : E().ONE));
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- front-door and instruments

  function frontDoor(graph, x, y, { maxSize = 3 } = {}) {
    const obs = graph.ids.filter((v) => !graph.latent.has(v) && v !== x && v !== y);
    const directed = (from, to, blocked) => {
      // is there a directed path from -> to avoiding `blocked`?
      const stack = [from], seen = new Set([from]);
      while (stack.length) for (const c of graph.children(stack.pop())) {
        if (c === to) return true;
        if (!blocked.has(c) && !seen.has(c)) {
          seen.add(c);
          stack.push(c);
        }
      }
      return false;
    };
    const backdoorFree = (a, b, Z) => {
      // no open back-door path from a to b given Z: d-separation after removing edges out of a
      const g = new MG.Graph({ nodes: graph.ids.map((i) => graph.meta.get(i)), edges: graph.edges.filter(([p]) => p !== a), latent: graph.latent });
      return MG.dsep.dSeparated(g, [a], [b], Z);
    };
    const found = [];
    const subsets = [];
    const rec = (start, acc) => {
      if (acc.length) subsets.push(acc.slice());
      if (acc.length === maxSize) return;
      for (let i = start; i < obs.length; i++) {
        acc.push(obs[i]);
        rec(i + 1, acc);
        acc.pop();
      }
    };
    rec(0, []);
    for (const M of subsets.sort((a, b) => a.length - b.length)) {
      const Ms = new Set(M);
      if (found.some((f) => MG.subset(f, Ms))) continue;
      if (directed(x, y, Ms)) continue; // (i) M intercepts every directed path
      if (![...Ms].every((m) => backdoorFree(x, m, new Set()))) continue; // (ii)
      if (![...Ms].every((m) => backdoorFree(m, y, new Set([x])))) continue; // (iii)
      if (![...Ms].every((m) => graph.ancestralClosure([y]).has(m) && graph.descendants(x).has(m))) continue;
      found.push(Ms);
    }
    return found;
  }

  function frontDoorFormula(x, y, M) {
    const m = MG.sorted(M);
    const xp = `${x}'`;
    const inner = E().sum([xp], E().prod(E().P([[y, y]], [...m.map((v) => [v, v]), [x, xp]]), E().P([[x, xp]])));
    return E().sum(m, E().prod(E().P(m.map((v) => [v, v]), [[x, x]]), inner));
  }

  /** Conditional instruments Z for x -> y given W (W non-descendants of x and y). */
  function instruments(graph, x, y, { maxW = 2 } = {}) {
    const noOut = new MG.Graph({ nodes: graph.ids.map((i) => graph.meta.get(i)), edges: graph.edges.filter(([a]) => a !== x), latent: graph.latent });
    const desc = new Set([...graph.descendants(x), ...graph.descendants(y), x, y]);
    const cands = graph.ids.filter((v) => !graph.latent.has(v) && !desc.has(v));
    const out = [];
    for (const z of cands) {
      const pool = cands.filter((w) => w !== z);
      const tryW = (W) => MG.dsep.dSeparated(noOut, [z], [y], W) && !MG.dsep.dSeparated(graph, [z], [x], W);
      let found = null;
      const rec = (start, acc) => {
        if (found) return;
        if (tryW(acc)) return (found = acc.slice());
        if (acc.length === maxW) return;
        for (let i = start; i < pool.length; i++) {
          acc.push(pool[i]);
          rec(i + 1, acc);
          acc.pop();
        }
      };
      rec(0, []);
      if (found) out.push({ z, given: found });
    }
    return out;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- paths

  /** Causal (directed) paths from X to Y and back-door paths into X. */
  function pathsBetween(graph, x, y, { max = 200 } = {}) {
    const all = MG.dsep.paths(graph, x, y, [], { maxPaths: 2000, maxLength: 12 }).paths;
    const causal = all.filter((p) => p.forward.every(Boolean));
    const backdoor = all.filter((p) => !p.forward[0]);
    const other = all.filter((p) => p.forward[0] && !p.forward.every(Boolean));
    return { causal: causal.slice(0, max), backdoor: backdoor.slice(0, max), other: other.slice(0, max) };
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- one causal question

  /**
   * P(Y | do(X)[, Z]) in the graph. Without missing data: ID/IDC plus adjustment, front-door
   * and instruments. With partially observed variables (Section 3.5 of Mohan & Pearl 2021):
   * identify in the substantive model and recover each factor, or identify P(Y* | do(X, R = 0), Z*)
   * in the graph where partially observed variables are latent.
   */
  function effect(graph, X, Y, Z = []) {
    X = MG.sorted(new Set(X)); Y = MG.sorted(new Set(Y)); Z = MG.sorted(new Set(Z));
    graph.require([...X, ...Y, ...Z]);
    const both = [...X].filter((v) => Y.includes(v) || Z.includes(v));
    if (both.length) throw new Error(`${both.join(", ")} appears twice`);
    if (!X.length || !Y.length) throw new Error("choose at least one treatment and one outcome");
    for (const v of [...X, ...Y, ...Z]) if (graph.latent.has(v)) throw new Error(`${v} is latent`);
    const missing = graph.partiallyObserved.size > 0;
    const out = { X, Y, Z, missing };

    // the substantive model: observed and partially observed variables; indicators and proxies dropped
    const substantive = MG.ADMG.project(graph, graph.ids.filter((v) => !graph.latent.has(v) && !graph.indicators.has(v) && !graph.proxies.has(v)));
    try {
      const r = Z.length ? idc(substantive, Y, X, Z) : id(substantive, Y, X);
      out.identifiable = true;
      out.idExpr = r.expr;
      out.trace = r.trace;
    } catch (e) {
      if (!(e instanceof NotIdentifiable)) throw e;
      out.identifiable = false;
      out.hedge = e.hedge;
      out.trace = e.trace;
    }
    const subDag = graph.partiallyObserved.size ? null : graph;
    if (!Z.length && subDag) {
      out.adjustment = adjustment(subDag, X, Y);
      if (out.adjustment.exists) {
        const best = out.adjustment.optimal || out.adjustment.minimal[0] || out.adjustment.canonical;
        out.adjustmentExpr = adjustmentFormula(X, Y, best);
        out.adjustmentSet = best;
      }
      if (X.length === 1 && Y.length === 1) {
        out.frontDoor = frontDoor(subDag, X[0], Y[0]);
        out.instruments = instruments(subDag, X[0], Y[0]);
        out.paths = pathsBetween(subDag, X[0], Y[0]);
      }
    }
    if (out.idExpr) out.idExpr = MG.expr.present(out.idExpr);
    if (!missing) {
      out.recoverable = out.identifiable;
      out.expr = out.idExpr || null;
      out.method = out.identifiable ? "ID" : null;
      return out;
    }

    // Section 3.5: a query not identifiable in the substantive model is not recoverable
    if (!out.identifiable) {
      out.recoverable = false;
      out.method = "not identifiable";
      out.reason = "The effect is not identifiable even without missing data, so it cannot be recovered (Section 3.5).";
      return out;
    }
    // (a) recover every factor of the substantive estimand
    const viaFactors = recoverFactors(graph, out.idExpr);
    if (viaFactors) {
      out.recoverable = true;
      out.method = "identify, then recover each factor";
      out.expr = MG.expr.present(viaFactors.expr);
      out.factorResults = viaFactors.parts;
      return out;
    }
    // (b) identify P(Y* | do(X, R = 0), Z*) where partially observed variables are latent
    const viaR = interventionRoute(graph, Y, X, Z);
    if (viaR) {
      out.recoverable = true;
      out.method = "intervention on the indicators";
      out.expr = MG.expr.present(viaR.expr);
      out.trace = [...(out.trace || []), ...viaR.trace];
      out.interventionSet = viaR.R;
      return out;
    }
    out.recoverable = null;
    out.reason = "Identifiable without missing data, but neither recovering each factor nor intervening on the indicators recovers it.";
    return out;
  }

  /** Replace every P term of a substantive estimand by its recovered form, if all are recoverable. */
  function recoverFactors(graph, expr) {
    const parts = [];
    const memo = new Map();
    const go = (e) => {
      if (e.t === "P") {
        const target = e.vars.map(([c]) => c);
        const given = e.given.map(([c]) => c);
        // symbols in the substantive estimand are the variable names (or a renamed copy, like x')
        const k = JSON.stringify([e.vars, e.given]);
        if (!memo.has(k)) {
          const r = MG.missing.recover(graph, target, given);
          memo.set(k, r);
          parts.push(r);
        }
        const r = memo.get(k);
        if (r.recoverable !== true) throw new Error("factor not recoverable");
        return renameSymbols(r.expr, Object.fromEntries([...e.vars, ...e.given].map(([c, v]) => [c, v])));
      }
      if (e.t === "sum") return { ...e, body: go(e.body) };
      if (e.t === "prod") return { ...e, factors: e.factors.map(go) };
      if (e.t === "frac") return { ...e, num: go(e.num), den: go(e.den) };
      return e;
    };
    try {
      return { expr: go(expr), parts };
    } catch (err) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Rename free symbols (a recovered factor uses the variable names as symbols). Bound symbols
   * that would capture a new name are renamed first (alpha-renaming).
   */
  function renameSymbols(e, map) {
    const targets = new Set(Object.values(map).filter((v) => typeof v === "string"));
    let fresh = 0;
    const go = (x, ren) => {
      const val = (v) => (typeof v !== "string" ? v : ren.has(v) ? ren.get(v) : v);
      if (x.t === "P") {
        const f = (list) => list.map(([c, v]) => [c, val(v)]);
        return { ...x, vars: f(x.vars), given: f(x.given), do: f(x.do) };
      }
      if (x.t === "sum") {
        const inner = new Map(ren);
        const over = x.over.map((s) => {
          let name = s;
          if (targets.has(s)) name = `${s}~${++fresh}`;
          inner.set(s, name);
          return name;
        });
        return { ...x, over, body: go(x.body, inner) };
      }
      if (x.t === "prod") return { ...x, factors: x.factors.map((f) => go(f, ren)) };
      if (x.t === "frac") return { ...x, num: go(x.num, ren), den: go(x.den, ren) };
      return x;
    };
    return go(e, new Map(Object.entries(map)));
  }

  /**
   * Recovery by intervention (Section 3.3; Example 6, Example 10): R has no children among the
   * substantive variables, so P(Y | do(X), Z) = P(Y* | do(X), do(R = 0), Z*), which is an
   * identification problem in the graph where partially observed variables are latent.
   * Tries the indicators of the query's variables first, then all indicators.
   */
  function interventionRoute(graph, Y, X, Z, { only = null } = {}) {
    const pm = graph.partiallyObserved;
    if (X.some((x) => pm.has(x))) return null; // a partially observed treatment cannot be set in this graph
    const keep = graph.ids.filter((v) => !graph.latent.has(v) && !pm.has(v));
    const G = MG.ADMG.project(graph, keep);
    const col = (v) => (pm.has(v) ? v + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX : v);
    const needed = [...new Set([...Y, ...Z].filter((v) => pm.has(v)).map((v) => MG.R_PREFIX + v))];
    const all = MG.sorted(graph.indicators);
    const choices = only ? [only] : MG.setKey(new Set(needed)) === MG.setKey(new Set(all)) ? [needed] : [needed, all];
    for (const Rset of choices) {
      const constants = Object.fromEntries(Rset.map((r) => [r, 0]));
      const symbol = (v) => (v.endsWith(MG.PROXY_SUFFIX) && pm.has(v.slice(0, -MG.PROXY_SUFFIX.length)) ? v.slice(0, -MG.PROXY_SUFFIX.length) : v);
      try {
        const Ycols = Y.map(col), Zcols = Z.map(col);
        const r = idc(G, Ycols, [...X, ...Rset], Zcols, { constants, symbol });
        return { expr: r.expr, trace: [{ line: "§3.3", depth: 0, text: `Since no indicator causes a substantive variable, the query equals P(${Ycols.map(MG.expr.colName).join(", ")} | do(${[...X, ...Rset.map((r) => r + " = 0")].join(", ")})${Zcols.length ? ", " + Zcols.map(MG.expr.colName).join(", ") : ""}) in the graph where partially observed variables are latent.` }, ...r.trace], R: Rset };
      } catch (e) {
        if (!(e instanceof NotIdentifiable)) throw e;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  MG.causal = { id, idc, NotIdentifiable, doRule, adjustment, adjustmentFormula, minimalSetSeparators, frontDoor, frontDoorFormula, instruments, pathsBetween, effect, interventionRoute, recoverFactors };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/render/metrics.js ---- */
/* Text measurement with the standard Helvetica metrics (Adobe AFM, characters 32-126).
 *
 * The live view, the SVG and the PDF all size labels with these widths, so a figure has the
 * same geometry in every format. PDF uses the built-in Helvetica fonts, which need no embedding.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  // prettier-ignore
  const REGULAR = [278,278,355,556,556,889,667,222,333,333,389,584,278,333,278,278,556,556,556,556,556,556,556,556,556,556,278,278,584,584,584,556,1015,667,667,722,722,667,611,778,722,278,500,667,556,833,722,778,667,778,722,667,611,722,667,944,667,667,611,278,278,278,469,556,222,556,556,500,556,556,278,556,556,222,222,500,222,833,556,556,556,556,333,500,278,556,500,722,500,500,500,334,260,334,584];
  // prettier-ignore
  const BOLD = [278,333,474,556,556,889,722,278,333,333,389,584,278,333,278,278,556,556,556,556,556,556,556,556,556,556,333,333,584,584,584,611,975,722,722,722,722,667,611,778,722,278,556,722,611,833,722,778,667,778,722,667,611,722,667,944,667,667,611,333,278,333,584,556,278,556,611,556,611,556,333,611,611,278,278,556,278,889,611,611,611,611,389,556,333,611,556,778,556,556,500,389,280,389,584];

  function textWidth(text, size, bold = false) {
    const table = bold ? BOLD : REGULAR;
    let w = 0;
    for (const ch of String(text)) {
      const c = ch.codePointAt(0);
      w += c >= 32 && c <= 126 ? table[c - 32] : 556;
    }
    return (w * size) / 1000;
  }

  /** Greedy word wrap to a maximum width. */
  function wrap(text, size, maxWidth, bold = false) {
    const words = String(text).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    const lines = [];
    let line = "";
    for (const w of words) {
      const next = line ? line + " " + w : w;
      if (line && textWidth(next, size, bold) > maxWidth) {
        lines.push(line);
        line = w;
      } else line = next;
    }
    if (line) lines.push(line);
    return lines;
  }

  MG.metrics = { textWidth, wrap, FONT_FAMILY: "Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif" };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/render/geometry.js ---- */
/* Node shapes and edge geometry.
 *
 * Shapes are lists of path commands (["M",x,y], ["L",x,y], ["C",x1,y1,x2,y2,x,y], ["Z"]) relative
 * to the node centre, so the SVG writer, the PDF writer and the live view draw the same outline.
 * Each role has its own shape, so a role never depends on colour alone:
 *
 *   fully observed        rounded rectangle
 *   partially observed    rounded rectangle with a second, inner outline
 *   missingness indicator hexagon with pointed ends
 *   recorded proxy        stadium (fully rounded ends)
 *   latent                ellipse, dashed outline
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const LABEL_SIZE = 13;
  const HEIGHT = 28;
  const PAD_X = 11;
  const K = 0.5522847498; // cubic Bezier circle constant

  function nodeSize(id, role) {
    const w = Math.max(38, MG.metrics.textWidth(id, LABEL_SIZE) + 2 * PAD_X);
    if (role === "latent") return { w: w + 10, h: HEIGHT + 6 };
    if (role === "missingness indicator") return { w: w + 10, h: HEIGHT };
    return { w, h: HEIGHT };
  }

  function roundedRect(w, h, r) {
    const x = -w / 2, y = -h / 2;
    r = Math.min(r, w / 2, h / 2);
    const c = r * (1 - K);
    return [
      ["M", x + r, y],
      ["L", x + w - r, y],
      ["C", x + w - c, y, x + w, y + c, x + w, y + r],
      ["L", x + w, y + h - r],
      ["C", x + w, y + h - c, x + w - c, y + h, x + w - r, y + h],
      ["L", x + r, y + h],
      ["C", x + c, y + h, x, y + h - c, x, y + h - r],
      ["L", x, y + r],
      ["C", x, y + c, x + c, y, x + r, y],
      ["Z"],
    ];
  }

  function ellipse(rx, ry) {
    const kx = rx * K, ky = ry * K;
    return [
      ["M", rx, 0],
      ["C", rx, ky, kx, ry, 0, ry],
      ["C", -kx, ry, -rx, ky, -rx, 0],
      ["C", -rx, -ky, -kx, -ry, 0, -ry],
      ["C", kx, -ry, rx, -ky, rx, 0],
      ["Z"],
    ];
  }

  function hexagon(w, h) {
    const d = h / 2.6, x = w / 2, y = h / 2;
    return [["M", -x + d, -y], ["L", x - d, -y], ["L", x, 0], ["L", x - d, y], ["L", -x + d, y], ["L", -x, 0], ["Z"]];
  }

  /** Outline commands, plus an optional inner outline for partially observed nodes. */
  function nodeShape(role, w, h) {
    switch (role) {
      case "latent":
        return { outline: ellipse(w / 2, h / 2) };
      case "missingness indicator":
        return { outline: hexagon(w, h) };
      case "recorded proxy":
        return { outline: roundedRect(w, h, h / 2) };
      case "partially observed":
        return { outline: roundedRect(w, h, 7), inner: roundedRect(w - 6, h - 6, 4.5) };
      default:
        return { outline: roundedRect(w, h, 7) };
    }
  }

  /** Flatten an outline into a polygon for ray intersection. */
  function flatten(cmds, steps = 8) {
    const pts = [];
    let cx = 0, cy = 0, sx = 0, sy = 0;
    for (const c of cmds) {
      if (c[0] === "M") {
        cx = sx = c[1];
        cy = sy = c[2];
        pts.push([cx, cy]);
      } else if (c[0] === "L") {
        cx = c[1];
        cy = c[2];
        pts.push([cx, cy]);
      } else if (c[0] === "C") {
        const [, x1, y1, x2, y2, x, y] = c;
        for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
          const t = i / steps, u = 1 - t;
          pts.push([
            u * u * u * cx + 3 * u * u * t * x1 + 3 * u * t * t * x2 + t * t * t * x,
            u * u * u * cy + 3 * u * u * t * y1 + 3 * u * t * t * y2 + t * t * t * y,
          ]);
        }
        cx = x;
        cy = y;
      } else if (c[0] === "Z") {
        cx = sx;
        cy = sy;
      }
    }
    return pts;
  }

  /** Distance from the centre to the outline along direction (dx, dy). */
  function rayDistance(poly, dx, dy) {
    const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    dx /= len;
    dy /= len;
    let best = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < poly.length; i++) {
      const [ax, ay] = poly[i];
      const [bx, by] = poly[(i + 1) % poly.length];
      const ex = bx - ax, ey = by - ay;
      const den = dx * ey - dy * ex;
      if (Math.abs(den) < 1e-9) continue;
      const t = (ax * ey - ay * ex) / den;
      const s = (ax * dy - ay * dx) / den;
      if (t > 0 && s >= -1e-6 && s <= 1 + 1e-6 && t < best) best = t;
    }
    return best === Infinity ? 0 : best;
  }

  /** Layout of one node: size, shape and the polygon used to clip edges. */
  function nodeLayout(id, role) {
    const { w, h } = nodeSize(id, role);
    const shape = nodeShape(role, w, h);
    return { id, role, w, h, shape, poly: flatten(shape.outline) };
  }

  const ARROW_LENGTH = 9;
  const ARROW_HALF = 4.4;
  const GAP = 2;

  /**
   * An edge between two placed nodes: the line (start, end) and the arrowhead triangle.
   * a, b are {x, y, layout}.
   */
  function edgeGeometry(a, b) {
    const dx = b.x - a.x, dy = b.y - a.y;
    const len = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    const ux = dx / len, uy = dy / len;
    const da = rayDistance(a.layout.poly, ux, uy) + GAP;
    const db = rayDistance(b.layout.poly, -ux, -uy) + GAP;
    const sx = a.x + ux * da, sy = a.y + uy * da;
    const tx = b.x - ux * db, ty = b.y - uy * db;
    const bx = tx - ux * ARROW_LENGTH, by = ty - uy * ARROW_LENGTH;
    const px = -uy * ARROW_HALF, py = ux * ARROW_HALF;
    const head = [["M", tx, ty], ["L", bx + px, by + py], ["L", bx - px, by - py], ["Z"]];
    // the line stops inside the arrowhead so its end is hidden by the fill
    const ex = tx - ux * ARROW_LENGTH * 0.7, ey = ty - uy * ARROW_LENGTH * 0.7;
    return { x1: sx, y1: sy, x2: ex, y2: ey, tipX: tx, tipY: ty, head, visible: len > da + db + 1 };
  }

  function translate(cmds, x, y) {
    return cmds.map((c) => {
      if (c[0] === "Z") return c;
      const out = [c[0]];
      for (let i = 1; i < c.length; i += 2) out.push(c[i] + x, c[i + 1] + y);
      return out;
    });
  }

  function toSvgPath(cmds, digits = 2) {
    const f = (v) => +v.toFixed(digits);
    return cmds.map((c) => (c[0] === "Z" ? "Z" : c[0] + c.slice(1).map(f).join(" "))).join("");
  }

  MG.geometry = { LABEL_SIZE, HEIGHT, nodeLayout, nodeShape, edgeGeometry, translate, toSvgPath, rayDistance, flatten };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/layout.js ---- */
/* Automatic layout for graphs that come without coordinates.
 *
 * A compact layered (Sugiyama-style) layout:
 *   1. layers by longest path from the sources, so every edge points downward
 *   2. dummy nodes on long edges, then barycentre sweeps to reduce crossings
 *   3. x positions pulled towards the neighbours' median, keeping a minimum gap
 * Proxies are placed one layer below the lower of their two parents, so each sits under its
 * variable and indicator.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  function layered(ids, edges, { layerGap = 115, nodeGap = 34, roleOf = () => "fully observed" } = {}) {
    const parents = new Map(ids.map((n) => [n, []]));
    const children = new Map(ids.map((n) => [n, []]));
    for (const [a, b] of edges) {
      children.get(a).push(b);
      parents.get(b).push(a);
    }
    // 1. layers
    const order = topo(ids, parents, children);
    const layer = new Map();
    for (const n of order) layer.set(n, Math.max(-1, ...parents.get(n).map((p) => layer.get(p))) + 1);
    // pull sources down towards their children, so roots do not all crowd the top row
    for (const n of order.slice().reverse()) {
      const ch = children.get(n);
      if (ch.length && !parents.get(n).length) layer.set(n, Math.min(...ch.map((c) => layer.get(c))) - 1);
    }
    const nLayers = Math.max(0, ...layer.values()) + 1;

    // 2. dummy chains for edges that skip layers
    const rows = Array.from({ length: nLayers }, () => []);
    for (const n of order) rows[layer.get(n)].push(n);
    const up = new Map(ids.map((n) => [n, []]));
    const down = new Map(ids.map((n) => [n, []]));
    let dummy = 0;
    const isDummy = new Set();
    for (const [a, b] of edges) {
      let prev = a;
      for (let l = layer.get(a) + 1; l < layer.get(b); l++) {
        const d = `\u0000d${dummy++}`;
        isDummy.add(d);
        rows[l].push(d);
        up.set(d, [prev]);
        down.set(d, []);
        down.get(prev).push(d);
        prev = d;
      }
      down.get(prev).push(b);
      up.get(b).push(prev);
    }
    const pos = new Map();
    const reindex = () => rows.forEach((r) => r.forEach((n, i) => pos.set(n, i)));
    reindex();
    const bary = (n, nbrs) => {
      const list = nbrs.get(n);
      return list.length ? list.reduce((s, m) => s + pos.get(m), 0) / list.length : pos.get(n);
    };
    for (let it = 0; it < 12; it++) {
      const downward = it % 2 === 0;
      const seq = downward ? rows.slice(1) : rows.slice(0, -1).reverse();
      for (const r of seq) {
        const keyed = r.map((n) => [bary(n, downward ? up : down), pos.get(n), n]);
        keyed.sort((p, q) => p[0] - q[0] || p[1] - q[1]);
        r.splice(0, r.length, ...keyed.map((k) => k[2]));
        r.forEach((n, i) => pos.set(n, i));
      }
    }

    // 3. coordinates
    const width = (n) => (isDummy.has(n) ? 10 : MG.geometry ? MG.geometry.nodeLayout(n, roleOf(n)).w : 60);
    const x = new Map();
    for (const r of rows) {
      let cursor = 0;
      for (const n of r) {
        const w = width(n);
        x.set(n, cursor + w / 2);
        cursor += w + nodeGap;
      }
      const shift = cursor / 2;
      for (const n of r) x.set(n, x.get(n) - shift);
    }
    for (let it = 0; it < 24; it++) {
      for (const r of it % 2 ? rows : rows.slice().reverse()) {
        const target = r.map((n) => {
          const nb = [...up.get(n), ...down.get(n)].map((m) => x.get(m)).sort((p, q) => p - q);
          return nb.length ? (nb[(nb.length - 1) >> 1] + nb[nb.length >> 1]) / 2 : x.get(n);
        });
        r.forEach((n, i) => x.set(n, x.get(n) * 0.5 + target[i] * 0.5));
        // restore order and gaps, left to right then right to left
        for (let i = 1; i < r.length; i++) {
          const min = x.get(r[i - 1]) + (width(r[i - 1]) + width(r[i])) / 2 + nodeGap;
          if (x.get(r[i]) < min) x.set(r[i], min);
        }
        for (let i = r.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) {
          const max = x.get(r[i + 1]) - (width(r[i + 1]) + width(r[i])) / 2 - nodeGap;
          if (x.get(r[i]) > max) x.set(r[i], max);
        }
      }
    }
    const out = new Map();
    for (const n of ids) out.set(n, { x: +x.get(n).toFixed(1), y: layer.get(n) * layerGap });
    return out;
  }

  function topo(ids, parents, children) {
    const indeg = new Map(ids.map((n) => [n, parents.get(n).length]));
    const queue = ids.filter((n) => !indeg.get(n));
    const out = [];
    while (queue.length) {
      const n = queue.shift();
      out.push(n);
      for (const c of children.get(n)) {
        indeg.set(c, indeg.get(c) - 1);
        if (!indeg.get(c)) queue.push(c);
      }
    }
    if (out.length !== ids.length) throw new Error("the graph has a cycle");
    return out;
  }

  /**
   * The nearest free spot to (x, y): tries rings of candidate offsets until the node's box
   * (with a margin) overlaps no placed node. `placed` is a list of {id, x, y}.
   */
  function freeSpot(placed, x, y, { id = "", w = 70, h = 34, margin = 16, roleOf = () => "fully observed" } = {}) {
    const size = (n) => (MG.geometry ? MG.geometry.nodeLayout(n.id, roleOf(n.id)) : { w: 60, h: 30 });
    const mine = MG.geometry && id ? MG.geometry.nodeLayout(id, roleOf(id)) : { w, h };
    const clear = (cx, cy) => placed.every((n) => {
      if (!Number.isFinite(n.x)) return true;
      const o = size(n);
      return Math.abs(n.x - cx) >= (o.w + mine.w) / 2 + margin || Math.abs(n.y - cy) >= (o.h + mine.h) / 2 + margin;
    });
    if (clear(x, y)) return { x, y };
    for (let ring = 1; ring <= 12; ring++) {
      const r = ring * 46;
      const steps = 8 * ring;
      for (let k = 0; k < steps; k++) {
        const a = (k / steps) * 2 * Math.PI + Math.PI / 2; // start below
        const cx = x + Math.cos(a) * r * 1.4, cy = y + Math.sin(a) * r;
        if (clear(cx, cy)) return { x: +cx.toFixed(1), y: +cy.toFixed(1) };
      }
    }
    return { x, y };
  }

  MG.layout = { layered, freeSpot };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/parse.js ---- */
/* Read a user's graph from text: DAGitty, Graphviz (digraph), JSON, or a plain edge list.
 *
 *   MG.parse.graphText(text, {name}) -> {spec, format, warnings}
 *
 * spec is the model format the explorer uses everywhere:
 *   {name, nodes: [{id, latent, x?, y?, exposure?, outcome?, adjusted?, description?}], edges: [[a, b]]}
 *
 * A bidirected edge a <-> b (an unrecorded common cause) becomes a latent node L_a_b with
 * edges to a and b, as in the Python reader. Names that the statement syntax cannot carry
 * (spaces, commas, |) are rewritten with underscores, with a warning.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  class ParseError extends Error {}

  function graphText(text, { name = "" } = {}) {
    const t = String(text || "").replace(/^﻿/, "").trim();
    if (!t) throw new ParseError("the text is empty");
    let out;
    if (/^\s*[{[]/.test(t)) out = { ...json(t), format: "JSON" };
    else if (/^\s*(strict\s+)?(di)?graph\b[^{]*\{/i.test(t)) out = { ...graphviz(t), format: "Graphviz" };
    else if (/^\s*(dag|pdag|mag|pag)\s*(\w+\s*)?\{/i.test(t)) out = { ...dagitty(t), format: "DAGitty" };
    else out = { ...edgeList(t), format: "edge list" };
    out.spec.name = name || out.spec.name || "Imported graph";
    finish(out);
    return out;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- tokenizer shared by DAGitty and Graphviz

  function tokenize(body) {
    const tokens = [];
    // a name may contain "-" except where it starts an edge operator (A->B without spaces)
    const re = /\s+|\/\/[^\n]*|#[^\n]*|\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\/|"((?:[^"\\]|\\.)*)"|(<->|->|<-|--|\[|\]|\{|\}|;|,|=)|([^\s\[\]{};,="<>\-](?:[^\s\[\]{};,="<>\-]|-(?![>-]))*)/gy;
    let m;
    re.lastIndex = 0;
    while (re.lastIndex < body.length) {
      const start = re.lastIndex;
      m = re.exec(body);
      if (!m) throw new ParseError(`cannot read the text near: ${body.slice(start, start + 30)}`);
      if (m[1] !== undefined) tokens.push({ t: "name", v: m[1].replace(/\\"/g, '"'), quoted: true });
      else if (m[2]) tokens.push({ t: m[2] });
      else if (m[3]) tokens.push({ t: "name", v: m[3] });
    }
    return tokens;
  }

  /** Statements: node lists joined by edge operators, with an optional [attribute list]. */
  function statements(tokens) {
    const out = [];
    let i = 0;
    const readGroup = () => {
      if (tokens[i] && tokens[i].t === "{") {
        i++;
        const names = [];
        while (i < tokens.length && tokens[i].t !== "}") {
          if (tokens[i].t === "name") names.push(tokens[i].v);
          else if (tokens[i].t !== "," && tokens[i].t !== ";") throw new ParseError(`unexpected "${tokens[i].t}" inside { }`);
          i++;
        }
        if (tokens[i] && tokens[i].t === "}") i++;
        return names;
      }
      if (tokens[i] && tokens[i].t === "name") return [tokens[i++].v];
      return null;
    };
    while (i < tokens.length) {
      if (tokens[i].t === ";" || tokens[i].t === ",") {
        i++;
        continue;
      }
      const first = readGroup();
      if (!first) throw new ParseError(`unexpected "${tokens[i].t === "name" ? tokens[i].v : tokens[i].t}"`);
      if (tokens[i] && tokens[i].t === "=") {
        // a graph setting such as rankdir=LR or bb="..."; not a node
        i += 2;
        continue;
      }
      const parts = [first];
      const ops = [];
      while (tokens[i] && ["->", "<-", "<->", "--"].includes(tokens[i].t)) {
        ops.push(tokens[i++].t);
        const next = readGroup();
        if (!next) throw new ParseError(`an edge "${ops[ops.length - 1]}" has nothing after it`);
        parts.push(next);
      }
      let attrs = null;
      if (tokens[i] && tokens[i].t === "[") {
        i++;
        attrs = {};
        while (i < tokens.length && tokens[i].t !== "]") {
          if (tokens[i].t === "name") {
            const key = tokens[i++].v.toLowerCase();
            if (tokens[i] && tokens[i].t === "=") {
              i++;
              attrs[key] = tokens[i] ? tokens[i++].v : "";
            } else attrs[key] = true;
          } else i++;
        }
        i++;
      }
      out.push({ parts, ops, attrs });
    }
    return out;
  }

  function collect(stmts, { allowUndirected = false } = {}) {
    const nodes = new Map();
    const edges = [];
    const bidirected = [];
    const add = (id) => {
      if (!nodes.has(id)) nodes.set(id, { id });
      return nodes.get(id);
    };
    for (const s of stmts) {
      if (!s.ops.length) {
        for (const id of s.parts[0]) {
          const n = add(id);
          if (s.attrs) applyAttrs(n, s.attrs);
        }
        continue;
      }
      for (let k = 0; k < s.ops.length; k++) {
        const op = s.ops[k];
        for (const a of s.parts[k]) for (const b of s.parts[k + 1]) {
          add(a);
          add(b);
          if (op === "->") edges.push([a, b]);
          else if (op === "<-") edges.push([b, a]);
          else if (op === "<->") bidirected.push([a, b]);
          else if (!allowUndirected) throw new ParseError(`undirected edge ${a} -- ${b}: an m-graph needs every edge directed (use -> or <->)`);
          else edges.push([a, b]);
        }
      }
    }
    return { nodes, edges, bidirected };
  }

  function applyAttrs(n, attrs) {
    if (attrs.latent || attrs.unobserved) n.latent = true;
    if (attrs.exposure) n.exposure = true;
    if (attrs.outcome) n.outcome = true;
    if (attrs.adjusted) n.adjusted = true;
    if (typeof attrs.pos === "string") {
      const m = attrs.pos.match(/^\s*(-?[\d.eE+-]+)\s*,\s*(-?[\d.eE+-]+)\s*!?\s*$/);
      if (m) {
        n.x = parseFloat(m[1]);
        n.y = parseFloat(m[2]);
      }
    }
    if (typeof attrs.label === "string" && attrs.label) n.description = attrs.label;
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- formats

  function dagitty(t) {
    const open = t.indexOf("{"), close = t.lastIndexOf("}");
    if (open < 0 || close < open) throw new ParseError("DAGitty text needs { ... } around the graph");
    const kind = t.slice(0, open).trim().split(/\s+/)[0].toLowerCase();
    if (kind !== "dag") throw new ParseError(`"${kind}" graphs are not DAGs; only "dag { }" can be an m-graph`);
    const body = t.slice(open + 1, close);
    const { nodes, edges, bidirected } = collect(statements(tokenize(body)));
    // DAGitty labels may be written as "X: description"
    const rename = new Map();
    for (const id of nodes.keys()) {
      const m = id.match(/^([^:]+):\s*(.+)$/);
      if (m) rename.set(id, { id: m[1].trim(), description: m[2].trim() });
    }
    return build(nodes, edges, bidirected, rename, { positions: "dagitty" });
  }

  function graphviz(t) {
    const open = t.indexOf("{"), close = t.lastIndexOf("}");
    const head = t.slice(0, open).trim();
    if (!/digraph/i.test(head)) throw new ParseError("an undirected 'graph { }' cannot be an m-graph; use 'digraph { }'");
    const body = t.slice(open + 1, close);
    const stmts = statements(tokenize(body)).filter((s) => !(s.ops.length === 0 && ["graph", "node", "edge"].includes(s.parts[0][0])));
    const { nodes, edges, bidirected } = collect(stmts);
    return build(nodes, edges, bidirected, new Map(), { positions: "graphviz" });
  }

  function json(t) {
    let data;
    try {
      data = JSON.parse(t);
    } catch (e) {
      throw new ParseError(`not valid JSON: ${e.message}`);
    }
    if (Array.isArray(data)) data = { edges: data };
    const nodes = new Map();
    for (const n of data.nodes || []) {
      const node = typeof n === "string" ? { id: n } : { ...n, id: String(n.id ?? n.name) };
      if (!node.id || node.id === "undefined") throw new ParseError("every node needs an id");
      nodes.set(node.id, { id: node.id, latent: !!node.latent, x: num(node.x), y: num(node.y), description: node.description || node.label || undefined, exposure: !!node.exposure, outcome: !!node.outcome });
    }
    for (const l of data.latent || []) {
      if (!nodes.has(l)) nodes.set(l, { id: l });
      nodes.get(l).latent = true;
    }
    const edges = [], bidirected = [];
    for (const e of data.edges || []) {
      let a, b, type = "->";
      if (Array.isArray(e)) [a, b, type = "->"] = e;
      else {
        a = e.from ?? e.source;
        b = e.to ?? e.target;
        type = e.type || (e.bidirected ? "<->" : "->");
      }
      if (a === undefined || b === undefined) throw new ParseError("every edge needs two ends");
      a = String(a);
      b = String(b);
      for (const id of [a, b]) if (!nodes.has(id)) nodes.set(id, { id });
      if (type === "<->" || type === "bidirected") bidirected.push([a, b]);
      else edges.push([a, b]);
    }
    return build(nodes, edges, bidirected, new Map(), { positions: "json", name: data.name });
  }

  function edgeList(t) {
    const nodes = new Map();
    const edges = [], bidirected = [];
    const lines = t.split(/\r?\n/);
    let header = true;
    lines.forEach((raw, k) => {
      const line = raw.replace(/(#|\/\/).*$/, "").trim();
      if (!line) return;
      let m;
      if ((m = line.match(/^(.+?)\s*(<->|->|<-|--)\s*(.+?)\s*;?$/))) {
        const [, a, op, b] = m;
        const A = a.replace(/^"|"$/g, "").trim(), B = b.replace(/^"|"$/g, "").trim();
        for (const id of [A, B]) if (!nodes.has(id)) nodes.set(id, { id });
        if (op === "->") edges.push([A, B]);
        else if (op === "<-") edges.push([B, A]);
        else if (op === "<->") bidirected.push([A, B]);
        else throw new ParseError(`line ${k + 1}: undirected edge; use -> or <->`);
      } else if ((m = line.split(/\s*[,\t]\s*/)).length >= 2) {
        if (header && /^(from|source|parent|cause)$/i.test(m[0]) && /^(to|target|child|effect)$/i.test(m[1])) {
          header = false;
          return;
        }
        const [A, B] = m.map((s) => s.replace(/^"|"$/g, "").trim());
        if (!A || !B) throw new ParseError(`line ${k + 1}: expected "from, to"`);
        for (const id of [A, B]) if (!nodes.has(id)) nodes.set(id, { id });
        const kind = (m[2] || "").toLowerCase();
        if (kind === "<->" || kind === "bidirected") bidirected.push([A, B]);
        else edges.push([A, B]);
      } else if (/^[^\s,]+$/.test(line)) {
        if (!nodes.has(line)) nodes.set(line, { id: line });
      } else throw new ParseError(`line ${k + 1}: expected "A -> B", "A <-> B" or "A, B"`);
      header = false;
    });
    if (!nodes.size) throw new ParseError("no nodes or edges found");
    return build(nodes, edges, bidirected, new Map(), { positions: "none" });
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- assembling the spec

  function build(nodes, edges, bidirected, rename, { name } = {}) {
    const warnings = [];
    const idMap = new Map();
    const used = new Set();
    for (const id of nodes.keys()) {
      let clean = (rename.get(id) || { id }).id;
      const safe = clean.replace(/[\s,|]+/g, "_");
      if (safe !== clean) warnings.push(`renamed "${clean}" to ${safe} (names cannot contain spaces, commas or |)`);
      clean = safe;
      if (used.has(clean)) throw new ParseError(`two nodes are both called ${clean}`);
      used.add(clean);
      idMap.set(id, clean);
    }
    const outNodes = [];
    for (const [id, n] of nodes) {
      const r = rename.get(id);
      outNodes.push({ ...n, id: idMap.get(id), description: n.description || (r && r.description) || undefined });
    }
    const outEdges = edges.map(([a, b]) => [idMap.get(a), idMap.get(b)]);
    for (const [a0, b0] of bidirected) {
      const a = idMap.get(a0), b = idMap.get(b0);
      let l = `L_${a}_${b}`;
      for (let k = 2; used.has(l); k++) l = `L_${a}_${b}_${k}`;
      used.add(l);
      outNodes.push({ id: l, latent: true, description: `unrecorded common cause of ${a} and ${b}`, bidirected: [a, b] });
      outEdges.push([l, a], [l, b]);
    }
    const seen = new Set();
    const unique = [];
    for (const e of outEdges) {
      const k = e[0] + "\u0001" + e[1];
      if (e[0] === e[1]) throw new ParseError(`${e[0]} has an edge to itself`);
      if (!seen.has(k)) {
        seen.add(k);
        unique.push(e);
      } else warnings.push(`duplicate edge ${e[0]} -> ${e[1]} ignored`);
    }
    return { spec: { name, nodes: outNodes, edges: unique }, warnings };
  }

  /** Checks shared by every format: acyclic, proxies present for every indicator, positions. */
  function finish(out) {
    const spec = out.spec;
    const ids = new Set(spec.nodes.map((n) => n.id));
    // every R_X with X present needs the proxy X_star (the paper often leaves proxies out of the figure)
    for (const n of spec.nodes.slice()) {
      if (!n.id.startsWith(MG.R_PREFIX)) continue;
      const v = n.id.slice(MG.R_PREFIX.length);
      if (!ids.has(v)) {
        out.warnings.push(`${n.id} looks like a missingness indicator but there is no variable ${v}; it is treated as an ordinary node`);
        continue;
      }
      const star = v + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX;
      if (!ids.has(star)) {
        spec.nodes.push({ id: star, description: `recorded proxy of ${v} (added: ${v} when ${n.id} = 0, missing otherwise)`, added: true });
        spec.edges.push([v, star], [n.id, star]);
        ids.add(star);
        out.warnings.push(`added the proxy ${star} with edges ${v} -> ${star} and ${n.id} -> ${star}`);
      }
    }
    new MG.Graph({ nodes: spec.nodes, edges: spec.edges, latent: spec.nodes.filter((n) => n.latent).map((n) => n.id) }); // throws on a cycle
    placeNodes(spec);
  }

  /** Give every node page coordinates: rescale given positions, lay out the rest. */
  function placeNodes(spec) {
    const given = spec.nodes.filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n.x) && Number.isFinite(n.y));
    const missing = spec.nodes.filter((n) => !(Number.isFinite(n.x) && Number.isFinite(n.y)));
    // nodes added here (proxies, latent common causes) go next to the nodes they belong to
    const parentsOf = (id) => spec.edges.filter((e) => e[1] === id).map((e) => e[0]);
    const childrenOf = (id) => spec.edges.filter((e) => e[0] === id).map((e) => e[1]);
    const placeable = (n) => {
      const anchors = (n.latent ? childrenOf(n.id) : parentsOf(n.id)).map((id) => spec.nodes.find((m) => m.id === id));
      return anchors.length && anchors.every((m) => m && Number.isFinite(m.x));
    };
    if (given.length > 1 && missing.length && missing.every(placeable)) {
      rescale(given);
      for (const n of missing) {
        const anchors = (n.latent ? childrenOf(n.id) : parentsOf(n.id)).map((id) => spec.nodes.find((m) => m.id === id));
        const cx = anchors.reduce((s, m) => s + m.x, 0) / anchors.length;
        const cy = anchors.reduce((s, m) => s + m.y, 0) / anchors.length;
        const role = roleFinder(spec);
        const spot = MG.layout.freeSpot(spec.nodes.filter((m) => m !== n), cx + (n.latent ? 0 : 26), cy + (n.latent ? -62 : 78), { id: n.id, roleOf: role });
        n.x = spot.x;
        n.y = spot.y;
      }
      return;
    }
    if (given.length === spec.nodes.length && given.length > 1) {
      rescale(given);
      return;
    }
    if (given.length === spec.nodes.length && given.length === 1) {
      given[0].x = 0;
      given[0].y = 0;
      return;
    }
    const pos = MG.layout.layered(spec.nodes.map((n) => n.id), spec.edges, { roleOf: roleFinder(spec) });
    for (const n of spec.nodes) {
      const p = pos.get(n.id);
      n.x = p.x;
      n.y = p.y;
    }
  }

  /** Scale given coordinates so the median nearest-neighbour distance is about 120 px. */
  function rescale(given) {
    const d = given.map((a) => Math.min(...given.filter((b) => b !== a).map((b) => Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y))));
    d.sort((p, q) => p - q);
    const med = d[Math.floor(d.length / 2)] || 1;
    const s = 120 / med;
    for (const n of given) {
      n.x = +(n.x * s).toFixed(1);
      n.y = +(n.y * s).toFixed(1);
    }
  }

  function roleFinder(spec) {
    const g = new MG.Graph({ nodes: spec.nodes, edges: spec.edges, latent: spec.nodes.filter((n) => n.latent).map((n) => n.id) });
    return (id) => g.role(id);
  }

  function num(v) {
    return v === undefined || v === null || v === "" ? undefined : +v;
  }

  MG.parse = { graphText, ParseError, placeNodes };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/core/gallery.js ---- */

 * Each entry is DAGitty text plus what the paper (or the textbook) states about it. The tests
 * check the engine against `expect`; where the printed paper and its own derivation disagree,
 * the entry says so in `note` and `expect` follows the graph, not the printed claim.
 *
 * Positions follow the paper's figures (DAGitty units, y down). Proxies X_star are left out,
 * as in the paper's figures, and added on loading.
 */

    {
      key: "c-iv", group: "Causal effects", name: "Instrumental variable",
      description: "Z affects Y only through X; X and Y share an unmeasured cause. The effect is not identifiable nonparametrically, but Z is a valid instrument.",
      text: `dag { Z [pos="0,1"] X [exposure,pos="1,1"] Y [outcome,pos="2,1"] Z -> X X -> Y X <-> Y }`,
      expect: { causal: [{ x: ["X"], y: ["Y"], identifiable: false, instruments: ["Z"] }] },
    },
    {
      key: "c-napkin", group: "Causal effects", name: "Napkin",
      description: "Identifiable, but not by adjustment or the front-door: the ID algorithm finds a ratio formula.",
      text: `dag { W [pos="0,0"] Z [pos="1,0.6"] X [exposure,pos="2,1.2"] Y [outcome,pos="3,1.8"] W -> Z Z -> X X -> Y W <-> X W <-> Y }`,
      expect: { causal: [{ x: ["X"], y: ["Y"], identifiable: true, adjustment: null }] },
    },
    {
      key: "c-mediation", group: "Causal effects", name: "Mediation",
      description: "X affects Y directly and through M; C confounds M and Y.",
      text: `dag { X [exposure,pos="0,1"] M [pos="1,0"] C [pos="2,-0.6"] Y [outcome,pos="2,1"] X -> M M -> Y X -> Y C -> M C -> Y }`,
      expect: { causal: [{ x: ["X"], y: ["Y"], identifiable: true, adjustment: [[]] }] },
    },
  ];

  /** The gallery entry as a model spec, ready for the store. */
  function load(key) {
    const e = ENTRIES.find((x) => x.key === key);
    if (!e) throw new Error(`no gallery entry ${key}`);
    const { spec, warnings } = MG.parse.graphText(e.text, { name: e.name });
    for (const n of spec.nodes) if (n.added) n.description = n.description.replace(/ \(added:.*\)$/, "");
    return { ...spec, key: e.key, group: e.group, description: e.description, note: e.note || null, source: "gallery", warnings };
  }

  MG.gallery = { ENTRIES, load };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/render/palette.js ---- */
/* Colours for figures. The live view takes the same values from CSS tokens (app.css); figures
 * are always drawn on white so they drop into a paper or slide unchanged.
 *
 * Role hues are from the Okabe-Ito colour-blind-safe set; every role also has its own shape.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  MG.palette = {
    figure: {
      ground: "#FFFFFF",
      ink: "#1B2322",
      muted: "#5B6763",
      faint: "#8E9894",
      edge: "#5F6B70",
      open: "#C8510A",
      focus: "#1F5FA8",
      conditionedFill: "#1B2322",
      conditionedText: "#FFFFFF",
      implied: "#1E7A4C",
      notImplied: "#B23A0E",
      roles: {
        "fully observed": { fill: "#E2EDF7", stroke: "#0072B2" },
        "partially observed": { fill: "#FBEBCB", stroke: "#A86F00" },
        "missingness indicator": { fill: "#F5E1EC", stroke: "#A24C7C" },
        "recorded proxy": { fill: "#DAF0E8", stroke: "#007A57" },
        latent: { fill: "#FFFFFF", stroke: "#7D8783", dash: [4, 3] },
      },
    },
  };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/render/scene.js ---- */
/* Figure scenes: a flat list of drawing primitives that the SVG and PDF writers both consume.
 *
 *   {kind: "path", d, fill, stroke, width, dash, opacity}
 *   {kind: "text", x, y, text, size, bold, fill, anchor, opacity}   y is the baseline
 *
 * buildScene takes plain data (graph, positions, highlight) and has no DOM dependency, so the
 * same code runs in the browser and in Node for figure export from Python.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const G = () => MG.geometry;
  const edgeKey = (a, b) => a + "\u0001" + b;

  /**
   * @param {object} o
   * @param {MG.Graph} o.graph
   * @param {Map<string,{x:number,y:number}>} o.positions
   * @param {Set<string>} [o.hiddenRoles]
   * @param {object} [o.highlight]  {x, y, z: Set; openEdges, focusEdges: Set of edge keys; blockers: Set; dim: bool}
   * @param {boolean} [o.legend]
   * @param {string} [o.title]
   * @param {string[]} [o.caption]  lines of caption text
   */
  function buildScene({ graph, positions, hiddenRoles = new Set(), highlight = null, legend = true, title = null, caption = [], spacing = 1, palette = MG.palette.figure }) {
    const pal = palette;
    const items = [];
    const visible = graph.ids.filter((id) => !hiddenRoles.has(graph.role(id)) && positions.has(id));
    const layouts = new Map(visible.map((id) => [id, G().nodeLayout(id, graph.role(id))]));
    const factor = spacing === "auto" ? compactFactor(visible, positions, layouts) : +spacing || 1;
    if (factor !== 1) positions = new Map(visible.map((id) => [id, { x: positions.get(id).x * factor, y: positions.get(id).y * factor }]));

    // bounds of the drawn graph
    let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
    for (const id of visible) {
      const p = positions.get(id), l = layouts.get(id);
      minX = Math.min(minX, p.x - l.w / 2 - 12);
      maxX = Math.max(maxX, p.x + l.w / 2 + 12);
      minY = Math.min(minY, p.y - l.h / 2 - 14);
      maxY = Math.max(maxY, p.y + l.h / 2 + 8);
    }
    if (!visible.length) minX = minY = 0, maxX = maxY = 100;

    const M = 28;
    const graphW = maxX - minX;
    const titleH = title ? 30 : 0;
    const legendItems = legend ? legendEntries(graph, hiddenRoles) : [];
    const width = Math.max(graphW + 2 * M, legend ? 420 : 0);
    const textWidth = width - 2 * M;
    const capLines = [];
    for (const line of caption || []) for (const l of MG.metrics.wrap(line, 11.5, textWidth)) capLines.push(l);
    const legendRows = layoutLegend(legendItems, textWidth);
    const ox = M - minX + (width - 2 * M - graphW) / 2;
    const oy = M + titleH - minY;
    const graphBottom = maxY + oy;
    let y = graphBottom + 18;
    const capY = y;
    y += capLines.length ? capLines.length * 16 + 8 : 0;
    const legendY = y;
    y += legendRows.length ? legendRows.length * 24 + 4 : 0;
    const height = y + M - 14;

    const pos = (id) => ({ x: positions.get(id).x + ox, y: positions.get(id).y + oy });
    const hl = highlight || {};
    const inStatement = (id) => (hl.x && hl.x.has(id)) || (hl.y && hl.y.has(id)) || (hl.z && hl.z.has(id));
    const onOpen = new Set();
    for (const k of hl.openEdges || []) k.split("\u0001").forEach((n) => onOpen.add(n));
    for (const k of hl.focusEdges || []) k.split("\u0001").forEach((n) => onOpen.add(n));
    const dimNode = (id) => hl.dim && !inStatement(id) && !onOpen.has(id);

    items.push({ kind: "path", d: rect(0, 0, width, height), fill: pal.ground, stroke: null });
    if (title) items.push({ kind: "text", x: M, y: M + 12, text: title, size: 15, bold: true, fill: pal.ink, anchor: "start" });

    // edges: normal first, then highlighted ones on top
    const edges = graph.edges.filter(([a, b]) => layouts.has(a) && layouts.has(b));
    const ordered = edges.slice().sort((e1, e2) => rank(e1) - rank(e2));
    function rank([a, b]) {
      const k = edgeKey(a, b);
      return (hl.focusEdges && hl.focusEdges.has(k) ? 2 : 0) + (hl.openEdges && hl.openEdges.has(k) ? 1 : 0);
    }
    for (const [a, b] of ordered) {
      const k = edgeKey(a, b);
      const geo = G().edgeGeometry({ ...pos(a), layout: layouts.get(a) }, { ...pos(b), layout: layouts.get(b) });
      if (!geo.visible) continue;
      const focus = hl.focusEdges && hl.focusEdges.has(k);
      const open = hl.openEdges && hl.openEdges.has(k);
      const color = focus ? pal.focus : open ? pal.open : pal.edge;
      const dim = hl.dim && !open && !focus;
      const opacity = dim ? 0.22 : 1;
      items.push({ kind: "path", d: [["M", geo.x1, geo.y1], ["L", geo.x2, geo.y2]], fill: null, stroke: color, width: open || focus ? 2.6 : 1.3, opacity });
      items.push({ kind: "path", d: geo.head, fill: color, stroke: null, opacity });
    }

    // nodes
    for (const id of visible) {
      const l = layouts.get(id);
      const p = pos(id);
      const style = pal.roles[l.role];
      const isZ = hl.z && hl.z.has(id);
      const isXY = (hl.x && hl.x.has(id)) || (hl.y && hl.y.has(id));
      const opacity = dimNode(id) ? 0.3 : 1;
      items.push({
        kind: "path",
        d: G().translate(l.shape.outline, p.x, p.y),
        fill: isZ ? pal.conditionedFill : style.fill,
        stroke: isXY ? pal.ink : isZ ? pal.conditionedFill : style.stroke,
        width: isXY ? 2.6 : 1.5,
        dash: l.role === "latent" && !isZ ? style.dash : null,
        opacity,
      });
      if (l.shape.inner)
        items.push({ kind: "path", d: G().translate(l.shape.inner, p.x, p.y), fill: null, stroke: isZ ? pal.conditionedText : style.stroke, width: 1, opacity });
      items.push({ kind: "text", x: p.x, y: p.y + 4.6, text: id, size: G().LABEL_SIZE, bold: false, fill: isZ ? pal.conditionedText : pal.ink, anchor: "middle", opacity });
      if (hl.blockers && hl.blockers.has(id)) {
        items.push({ kind: "path", d: G().translate(roundedOutline(l.w + 8, l.h + 8), p.x, p.y), fill: null, stroke: pal.focus, width: 1.4, dash: [3, 2.5], opacity });
      }
      const badge = hl.x && hl.x.has(id) ? "X" : hl.y && hl.y.has(id) ? "Y" : hl.z && hl.z.has(id) ? "Z" : null;
      if (badge) {
        const bx = p.x + l.w / 2 - 2, by = p.y - l.h / 2 - 2;
        items.push({ kind: "path", d: G().translate(circle(7.5), bx, by), fill: badge === "Z" ? pal.ground : pal.ink, stroke: pal.ink, width: 1.2 });
        items.push({ kind: "text", x: bx, y: by + 3.5, text: badge, size: 9.5, bold: true, fill: badge === "Z" ? pal.ink : pal.ground, anchor: "middle" });
      }
    }

    // caption
    capLines.forEach((line, i) =>
      items.push({ kind: "text", x: M, y: capY + 11 + i * 16, text: line, size: 11.5, bold: i === 0, fill: i === 0 ? pal.ink : pal.muted, anchor: "start" })
    );

    // legend
    legendRows.forEach((row, r) => {
      for (const e of row) {
        const cy = legendY + 10 + r * 24;
        const sample = sampleShape(e, pal);
        for (const s of sample(M + e.x + 13, cy)) items.push(s);
        items.push({ kind: "text", x: M + e.x + 30, y: cy + 4, text: e.label, size: 11, bold: false, fill: pal.muted, anchor: "start" });
      }
    });

    return { width: Math.ceil(width), height: Math.ceil(height), items };
  }

  /**
   * The smallest scale of the layout (node sizes unchanged) at which no two nodes overlap,
   * leaving a gap of at least 14 px, between 0.4 and 1.
   */
  function compactFactor(ids, positions, layouts) {
    const fits = (f) => {
      for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
        const a = positions.get(ids[i]), la = layouts.get(ids[i]);
        for (let j = i + 1; j < ids.length; j++) {
          const b = positions.get(ids[j]), lb = layouts.get(ids[j]);
          const dx = Math.abs(a.x - b.x) * f, dy = Math.abs(a.y - b.y) * f;
          if (dx < (la.w + lb.w) / 2 + 14 && dy < (la.h + lb.h) / 2 + 14) return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    };
    if (!fits(1)) return 1;
    let lo = 0.4, hi = 1;
    if (fits(lo)) return lo;
    for (let k = 0; k < 18; k++) {
      const mid = (lo + hi) / 2;
      if (fits(mid)) hi = mid;
      else lo = mid;
    }
    return Math.round(hi * 1000) / 1000;
  }

  function legendEntries(graph, hiddenRoles) {
    const roles = MG.ROLES.filter((r) => !hiddenRoles.has(r) && graph.ids.some((id) => graph.role(id) === r));
    return roles.map((r) => ({ type: "role", role: r, label: r }));
  }

  function layoutLegend(entries, maxWidth) {
    const rows = [];
    let row = [], x = 0;
    for (const e of entries) {
      const w = 34 + MG.metrics.textWidth(e.label, 11) + 18;
      if (row.length && x + w > maxWidth) {
        rows.push(row);
        row = [];
        x = 0;
      }
      row.push({ ...e, x });
      x += w;
    }
    if (row.length) rows.push(row);
    return rows;
  }

  function sampleShape(entry, pal) {
    return (cx, cy) => {
      const style = pal.roles[entry.role];
      const shape = G().nodeShape(entry.role, 24, 14);
      const out = [{ kind: "path", d: G().translate(shape.outline, cx, cy), fill: style.fill, stroke: style.stroke, width: 1.3, dash: style.dash || null }];
      if (shape.inner) out.push({ kind: "path", d: G().translate(G().nodeShape(entry.role, 19, 9).outline, cx, cy), fill: null, stroke: style.stroke, width: 0.9 });
      return out;
    };
  }

  function rect(x, y, w, h) {
    return [["M", x, y], ["L", x + w, y], ["L", x + w, y + h], ["L", x, y + h], ["Z"]];
  }

  function circle(r) {
    const k = r * 0.5522847498;
    return [["M", r, 0], ["C", r, k, k, r, 0, r], ["C", -k, r, -r, k, -r, 0], ["C", -r, -k, -k, -r, 0, -r], ["C", k, -r, r, -k, r, 0], ["Z"]];
  }

  function roundedOutline(w, h) {
    return G().nodeShape("fully observed", w, h).outline;
  }

  /** "X _||_ Y | Z" in words, for figure captions (the PDF fonts have no ⊥ glyph). */
  function inWords(text) {
    return String(text).replace(" _||_ ", " independent of ").replace(" | ", " given ");
  }

  /** Caption lines for a figure of one statement; shared by the page and the Python export. */
  function statementCaption(graph, st, { implied, openCount = 0, truncated = false } = {}) {
    const recorded = MG.Statement.recordedForm(graph, st);
    const lines = [`${inWords(st.toString())}: ${implied ? "implied" : "NOT implied"} by ${graph.name}.`];
    lines.push(recorded ? `Testable in recorded data as: ${inWords(recorded)}.` : `Not testable: ${MG.Statement.whyNotTestable(graph, st)}.`);
    lines.push(implied ? "Every path is blocked. X and Y outlined; conditioned nodes (Z) filled."
      : `${openCount}${truncated ? "+" : ""} open path${openCount === 1 ? "" : "s"} in orange. X and Y outlined; conditioned nodes (Z) filled.`);
    return lines;
  }

  MG.scene = { buildScene, edgeKey, compactFactor, statementCaption, inWords };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/render/svg_writer.js ---- */
/* Scene -> standalone SVG text. */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  function toSvg(scene, { title = "" } = {}) {
    const esc = MG.escapeXml;
    const f = (v) => +(+v).toFixed(2);
    const out = [];
    out.push(
      `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${scene.width}" height="${scene.height}" viewBox="0 0 ${scene.width} ${scene.height}" font-family="${MG.metrics.FONT_FAMILY}">`
    );
    if (title) out.push(`<title>${esc(title)}</title>`);
    for (const it of scene.items) {
      const op = it.opacity !== undefined && it.opacity < 1 ? ` opacity="${f(it.opacity)}"` : "";
      if (it.kind === "path") {
        const fill = it.fill ? ` fill="${it.fill}"` : ` fill="none"`;
        const stroke = it.stroke ? ` stroke="${it.stroke}" stroke-width="${f(it.width || 1)}" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-linecap="round"` : "";
        const dash = it.dash ? ` stroke-dasharray="${it.dash.join(" ")}"` : "";
        out.push(`<path d="${MG.geometry.toSvgPath(it.d)}"${fill}${stroke}${dash}${op}/>`);
      } else if (it.kind === "text") {
        const anchor = it.anchor && it.anchor !== "start" ? ` text-anchor="${it.anchor}"` : "";
        const weight = it.bold ? ` font-weight="bold"` : "";
        out.push(`<text x="${f(it.x)}" y="${f(it.y)}" font-size="${f(it.size)}" fill="${it.fill}"${anchor}${weight}${op}>${esc(it.text)}</text>`);
      }
    }
    out.push("</svg>");
    return out.join("\n");
  }

  MG.svgWriter = { toSvg };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/render/pdf_writer.js ---- */
/* Scene -> vector PDF (PDF 1.4, one page, built-in Helvetica, no dependencies).
 *
 * Returns a "binary string" (one char per byte, all < 256); MG.pdfWriter.toBytes converts it to
 * a Uint8Array for download. Text uses WinAnsi encoding; characters outside it are replaced.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const WINANSI_EXTRA = { "–": "\x96", "—": "\x97", "‘": "\x91", "’": "\x92", "“": "\x93", "”": "\x94", "•": "\x95", "·": "\xB7", "×": "\xD7", "…": "\x85" };
  const ASCII_SWAP = { "→": "->", "←": "<-", "⊥⊥": "_||_", "⫫": "_||_", "⊥": "_|_", "≤": "<=", "≥": ">=", "✓": "", "✗": "x" };

  function encodeText(s) {
    let t = String(s);
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(ASCII_SWAP)) t = t.split(k).join(v);
    let out = "";
    for (const ch of t) {
      const c = ch.codePointAt(0);
      let b;
      if (c >= 32 && c <= 126) b = ch;
      else if (WINANSI_EXTRA[ch]) b = WINANSI_EXTRA[ch];
      else if (c >= 0xa0 && c <= 0xff) b = String.fromCharCode(c);
      else b = "?";
      if (b === "(" || b === ")" || b === "\\") b = "\\" + b;
      out += b;
    }
    return out;
  }

  function rgb(hex) {
    const h = hex.replace("#", "");
    const n = parseInt(h.length === 3 ? h.split("").map((c) => c + c).join("") : h, 16);
    return [(n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255].map((v) => +(v / 255).toFixed(4)).join(" ");
  }

  const num = (v) => +(+v).toFixed(2);

  function toPdf(scene, { title = "", producer = "amr-identifiability" } = {}) {
    const H = scene.height;
    const gstates = new Map();
    const gs = (opacity) => {
