      const o = +(opacity === undefined ? 1 : opacity).toFixed(3);
      if (o >= 1) return "";
      if (!gstates.has(o)) gstates.set(o, `GS${gstates.size}`);
      return `/${gstates.get(o)} gs `;
    };
    const ops = [];
    for (const it of scene.items) {
      if (it.kind === "path") {
        let s = "q " + gs(it.opacity);
        if (it.fill) s += `${rgb(it.fill)} rg `;
        if (it.stroke) s += `${rgb(it.stroke)} RG ${num(it.width || 1)} w 1 j 1 J `;
        s += it.dash ? `[${it.dash.map(num).join(" ")}] 0 d ` : "";
        for (const c of it.d) {
          if (c[0] === "M") s += `${num(c[1])} ${num(H - c[2])} m `;
          else if (c[0] === "L") s += `${num(c[1])} ${num(H - c[2])} l `;
          else if (c[0] === "C") s += `${num(c[1])} ${num(H - c[2])} ${num(c[3])} ${num(H - c[4])} ${num(c[5])} ${num(H - c[6])} c `;
          else if (c[0] === "Z") s += "h ";
        }
        s += it.fill && it.stroke ? "B" : it.fill ? "f" : "S";
        ops.push(s + " Q");
      } else if (it.kind === "text") {
        const w = MG.metrics.textWidth(it.text, it.size, it.bold);
        const x = it.anchor === "middle" ? it.x - w / 2 : it.anchor === "end" ? it.x - w : it.x;
        ops.push(`q ${gs(it.opacity)}${rgb(it.fill)} rg BT /${it.bold ? "F2" : "F1"} ${num(it.size)} Tf ${num(x)} ${num(H - it.y)} Td (${encodeText(it.text)}) Tj ET Q`);
      }
    }
    const content = ops.join("\n");
    const ext = [...gstates].map(([o, name]) => `/${name} << /ca ${o} /CA ${o} >>`).join(" ");
    const objects = [
      "<< /Type /Catalog /Pages 2 0 R >>",
      "<< /Type /Pages /Kids [3 0 R] /Count 1 >>",
      `<< /Type /Page /Parent 2 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 ${scene.width} ${scene.height}] /Resources << /Font << /F1 4 0 R /F2 5 0 R >> /ExtGState << ${ext} >> >> /Contents 6 0 R >>`,
      "<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica /Encoding /WinAnsiEncoding >>",
      "<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica-Bold /Encoding /WinAnsiEncoding >>",
      `<< /Length ${content.length} >>\nstream\n${content}\nendstream`,
      `<< /Title (${encodeText(title)}) /Producer (${encodeText(producer)}) >>`,
    ];
    let pdf = "%PDF-1.4\n%\xE2\xE3\xCF\xD3\n";
    const offsets = [];
    objects.forEach((body, i) => {
      offsets.push(pdf.length);
      pdf += `${i + 1} 0 obj\n${body}\nendobj\n`;
    });
    const xref = pdf.length;
    pdf += `xref\n0 ${objects.length + 1}\n0000000000 65535 f \n`;
    for (const o of offsets) pdf += String(o).padStart(10, "0") + " 00000 n \n";
    pdf += `trailer\n<< /Size ${objects.length + 1} /Root 1 0 R /Info ${objects.length} 0 R >>\nstartxref\n${xref}\n%%EOF\n`;
    return pdf;
  }

  function toBytes(binary) {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i) & 255;
    return bytes;
  }

  MG.pdfWriter = { toPdf, toBytes, encodeText };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/dom.js ---- */
/* DOM helpers: element builder, icons, downloads, clipboard, toasts. */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const ui = (MG.ui = MG.ui || {});
  const SVG_NS = "http://www.w3.org/2000/svg";

  /** h("div", {class: "x", onClick: fn, dataset: {...}, style: {...}}, ...children) */
  function h(tag, props, ...children) {
    const el = document.createElement(tag);
    applyProps(el, props);
    append(el, children);
    return el;
  }

  function s(tag, attrs, ...children) {
    const el = document.createElementNS(SVG_NS, tag);
    if (attrs) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs)) if (v !== null && v !== undefined && v !== false) el.setAttribute(k, v);
    append(el, children);
    return el;
  }

  function applyProps(el, props) {
    if (!props) return;
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(props)) {
      if (v === null || v === undefined || v === false) continue;
      if (k === "class") el.className = Array.isArray(v) ? v.filter(Boolean).join(" ") : v;
      else if (k === "style" && typeof v === "object") Object.assign(el.style, v);
      else if (k === "dataset") Object.assign(el.dataset, v);
      else if (k === "html") el.innerHTML = v;
      else if (k === "value") el.value = v;
      else if (k.startsWith("on") && typeof v === "function") el.addEventListener(k.slice(2).toLowerCase(), v);
      else if (k in el && typeof v !== "string") el[k] = v;
      else el.setAttribute(k, v === true ? "" : v);
    }
  }

  function append(el, children) {
    for (const c of children.flat(Infinity)) {
      if (c === null || c === undefined || c === false) continue;
      el.appendChild(c instanceof Node ? c : document.createTextNode(String(c)));
    }
  }

  const ICONS = {
    plus: '<path d="M8 3v10M3 8h10"/>',
    minus: '<path d="M3 8h10"/>',
    fit: '<path d="M2.5 6V2.5H6M10 2.5h3.5V6M13.5 10v3.5H10M6 13.5H2.5V10"/>',
    layout: '<circle cx="4" cy="4" r="1.8"/><circle cx="12" cy="4" r="1.8"/><circle cx="8" cy="12" r="1.8"/><path d="M5.3 5.3l1.6 4.9M10.7 5.3L9.1 10.2"/>',
    undo: '<path d="M5.5 3.5L2.5 6.5l3 3"/><path d="M2.5 6.5h7a4 4 0 010 8H7"/>',
    redo: '<path d="M10.5 3.5l3 3-3 3"/><path d="M13.5 6.5h-7a4 4 0 000 8H9"/>',
    edit: '<path d="M10.5 2.5l3 3-8 8h-3v-3z"/><path d="M9 4l3 3"/>',
    download: '<path d="M8 2.5v8M4.5 7L8 10.5 11.5 7"/><path d="M2.5 12.5v1h11v-1"/>',
    sun: '<circle cx="8" cy="8" r="3"/><path d="M8 1.5v1.5M8 13v1.5M1.5 8H3M13 8h1.5M3.4 3.4l1 1M11.6 11.6l1 1M3.4 12.6l1-1M11.6 4.4l1-1"/>',
    moon: '<path d="M13 9.5A5.5 5.5 0 016.5 3a5.5 5.5 0 106.5 6.5z"/>',
    auto: '<circle cx="8" cy="8" r="5.5"/><path d="M8 2.5v11" /><path d="M8 2.5a5.5 5.5 0 010 11z" fill="currentColor" stroke="none"/>',
    help: '<circle cx="8" cy="8" r="6"/><path d="M6.2 6.3a1.9 1.9 0 013.6.7c0 1.3-1.8 1.6-1.8 2.7"/><path d="M8 11.6v.1"/>',
    close: '<path d="M4 4l8 8M12 4l-8 8"/>',
    copy: '<rect x="5" y="5" width="8.5" height="8.5" rx="1.5"/><path d="M3 10.5V3.5A1 1 0 014 2.5h7"/>',
    check: '<path d="M3 8.5l3 3 7-7"/>',
    cross: '<path d="M4 4l8 8M12 4l-8 8"/>',
    arrow: '<path d="M3 8h10M9 4l4 4-4 4"/>',
    target: '<circle cx="8" cy="8" r="5.5"/><circle cx="8" cy="8" r="2"/>',
    search: '<circle cx="7" cy="7" r="4.5"/><path d="M10.5 10.5L14 14"/>',
    query: '<path d="M2.5 4h11M2.5 8h7M2.5 12h4"/><circle cx="12" cy="11" r="2"/><path d="M13.5 12.5L15 14"/>',
  };

  function icon(name) {
    const el = document.createElementNS(SVG_NS, "svg");
    el.setAttribute("viewBox", "0 0 16 16");
    el.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
    el.innerHTML = ICONS[name] || "";
    return el;
  }

  function iconButton(name, label, props = {}) {
    const { text, class: cls, ...rest } = props;
    const classes = ["icon-btn", text ? "labelled" : null, cls].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    return h("button", { type: "button", title: label, "aria-label": label, ...rest, class: classes }, icon(name), text ? h("span", null, text) : null);
  }

  function download(filename, data, mime) {
    const blob = data instanceof Blob ? data : new Blob([data], { type: mime });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = h("a", { href: url, download: filename, style: { display: "none" } });
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    setTimeout(() => {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      a.remove();
    }, 1500);
  }

  async function copyText(text) {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
    } catch (e) {
      const ta = h("textarea", { style: { position: "fixed", opacity: "0" } });
      ta.value = text;
      document.body.appendChild(ta);
      ta.select();
      document.execCommand("copy");
      ta.remove();
    }
    toast("Copied");
  }

  function toast(message, kind = "") {
    const box = document.getElementById("toasts");
    if (!box) return;
    const el = h("div", { class: ["toast", kind] }, message);
    box.appendChild(el);
    setTimeout(() => {
      el.style.transition = "opacity 200ms";
      el.style.opacity = "0";
      setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 220);
    }, kind === "error" ? 4200 : 1800);
  }

  function debounce(fn, ms) {
    let t = null;
    return (...args) => {
      clearTimeout(t);
      t = setTimeout(() => fn(...args), ms);
    };
  }

  /** A small inline SVG showing the shape and colour of a role (legend, inspector). */
  const ROLE_CLASS = {
    "fully observed": "full",
    "partially observed": "part",
    "missingness indicator": "ind",
    "recorded proxy": "proxy",
    latent: "lat",
  };

  function roleGlyph(role, w = 24, h2 = 15) {
    const shape = MG.geometry.nodeShape(role, w, h2);
    const el = s("svg", { viewBox: `${-w / 2 - 2} ${-h2 / 2 - 2} ${w + 4} ${h2 + 4}`, width: w + 4, height: h2 + 4, class: "glyph", "aria-hidden": "true" });
    const cls = ROLE_CLASS[role];
    const style = (fill, stroke, dash) => `fill:var(--r-${fill}-fill);stroke:var(--r-${stroke});stroke-width:1.4${dash ? ";stroke-dasharray:3 2" : ""}`;
    el.appendChild(s("path", { d: MG.geometry.toSvgPath(shape.outline), style: style(cls, cls, role === "latent") }));
    if (shape.inner) el.appendChild(s("path", { d: MG.geometry.toSvgPath(MG.geometry.nodeShape(role, w - 5, h2 - 5).outline), style: `fill:none;stroke:var(--r-${cls});stroke-width:0.9` }));
    return el;
  }

  /** Statement text with the conditioning part muted. */
  function statementText(st) {
    const f = (set) => MG.sorted(set).join(", ");
    const head = `${f(st.x)} ⊥⊥ ${f(st.y)}`;
    if (!st.z.size) return h("span", { class: "stmt" }, head, h("span", { class: "given" }, "  (marginal)"));
    return h("span", { class: "stmt" }, head, h("span", { class: "given" }, " | " + f(st.z)));
  }

  Object.assign(ui, { h, s, icon, iconButton, download, copyText, toast, debounce, roleGlyph, statementText, ROLE_CLASS });
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/store.js ---- */
/* MG.ui.Store: application state, derived data (graph, catalogue, analysis) and edit history.
 *
 * Graphs. The page ships with built-in models (data.models). Any graph the user opens, imports,
 * loads from the gallery or edits is kept as a full spec in state.custom[key]:
 *   {key, name, group?, description?, note?, source, edited?, nodes: [{id, latent, x, y, ...}], edges: [[a, b]]}
 * An edited built-in model has a custom spec under its own key; resetting removes it.
 * Specs are replaced, never mutated, so a spec object identifies one version of a graph.
 *
 * Persistent state (saved into an exported HTML file):
 *   modelKey, custom, userOrder, layout, node, statement, tab, filters, hiddenRoles, theme,
 *   recovery, panelWidth, view
 * Transient state (never saved): hover, pick slot, edit mode, focused path.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const DEFAULTS = {
    modelKey: null,
    custom: {},
    userOrder: [],
    layout: {},
    node: null,
    statement: null,
    tab: "node",
    filters: { search: "", node: "", testableOnly: false, showNever: false, hideProxies: false },
    hiddenRoles: [],
    theme: "auto",
    missingQuery: null,
    causalQuery: null,
    panelWidth: 430,
    view: null,
  };

  const SAVED_KEYS = Object.keys(DEFAULTS);
  const GRAPH_KEYS = ["modelKey", "custom"];

  class Store {
    constructor(data, saved = {}) {
      this.data = data;
      this.builtins = new Map(data.models.map((m) => [m.key, m]));
      saved = migrate(saved || {}, this.builtins);
      this.state = {
        ...structuredClone(DEFAULTS),
        ...structuredClone(saved),
        filters: { ...DEFAULTS.filters, ...(saved.filters || {}) },
      };
      const first = data.defaultModel && this.builtins.has(data.defaultModel) ? data.defaultModel : data.models.length ? data.models[0].key : null;
      this._loadImports(data.imports || []);
      if (!this.spec(this.state.modelKey)) this.state.modelKey = first || this.state.userOrder[0] || this._loadFirstGallery();
      this.transient = { hoverNode: null, hoverEdge: null, focusPath: null, pick: null, editMode: false, editSource: null, edgeKind: "directed", pathTab: "open", emphasis: null };
      this._listeners = new Set();
      this._graphs = new WeakMap();
      this._catalogues = new WeakMap();
      this._analysis = null;
      this.history = { past: [], future: [] };
    }

    /** Graphs given as text (DAGitty, edge list, ...) by the page's author. */
    _loadImports(imports) {
      imports.forEach((imp, i) => {
        const key = imp.key || `import-${i + 1}`;
        if (this.state.custom[key]) return;
        try {
          const { spec } = MG.parse.graphText(imp.text, { name: imp.name || `Graph ${i + 1}` });
          this.state.custom = { ...this.state.custom, [key]: { ...spec, key, source: "user" } };
          this.state.userOrder = [...this.state.userOrder, key];
          if (i === 0 && !this.state.modelKey) this.state.modelKey = key;
        } catch (e) {
          console.error(`could not read graph ${imp.name || key}: ${e.message}`);
        }
      });
    }

    _loadFirstGallery() {
      const spec = MG.gallery.load(MG.gallery.ENTRIES[0].key);
      this.state.custom = { ...this.state.custom, [spec.key]: spec };
      this.state.userOrder = [...this.state.userOrder, spec.key];
      return spec.key;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- events

    on(fn) {
      this._listeners.add(fn);
      return () => this._listeners.delete(fn);
    }

    emit(changed) {
      for (const fn of this._listeners) fn(changed, this);
    }

    set(patch) {
      const changed = new Set(Object.keys(patch));
      Object.assign(this.state, patch);
      if (GRAPH_KEYS.some((k) => changed.has(k)) || changed.has("statement")) this._analysis = null;
      if (changed.has("custom")) changed.add("edits"); // panels listen for "edits" to mean "the graph changed"
      this.emit(changed);
    }

    setFilters(patch) {
      this.set({ filters: { ...this.state.filters, ...patch } });
    }

    setTransient(patch) {
      Object.assign(this.transient, patch);
      this.emit(new Set(["transient", ...Object.keys(patch).map((k) => "t:" + k)]));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- models and graphs

    /** The spec of a graph: the user's version if there is one, else the built-in model. */
    spec(key = this.state.modelKey) {
      return (key && (this.state.custom[key] || this.builtins.get(key))) || null;
    }

    get model() {
      return this.spec();
    }

    /** Every graph that can be shown: built-ins first, then gallery and user graphs. */
    listModels() {
      const out = this.data.models.map((m) => ({ ...this.spec(m.key), key: m.key, builtin: true, edited: !!this.state.custom[m.key] }));
      for (const k of this.state.userOrder) if (this.state.custom[k] && !this.builtins.has(k)) out.push({ ...this.state.custom[k], builtin: false });
      return out;
    }

    isBuiltin(key = this.state.modelKey) {
      return this.builtins.has(key);
    }

    /** A built-in or gallery graph that differs from its original. */
    isEdited(key = this.state.modelKey) {
      const spec = this.state.custom[key];
      return !!spec && (this.builtins.has(key) || (spec.source === "gallery" && !!spec.edited));
    }

    graph(key = this.state.modelKey) {
      const spec = this.spec(key);
      if (!spec) throw new Error(`no graph ${key}`);
      if (!this._graphs.has(spec)) {
        const name = this.isEdited(key) && !/\(edited\)$/.test(spec.name) ? `${spec.name} (edited)` : spec.name;
        this._graphs.set(spec, MG.Graph.fromPayload({ ...spec, key, name }));
      }
      return this._graphs.get(spec);
    }

    catalogue(graph = this.graph()) {
      if (!this._catalogues.has(graph)) this._catalogues.set(graph, new MG.Catalogue(graph));
      return this._catalogues.get(graph);
    }

    positions(key = this.state.modelKey) {
      const spec = this.spec(key);
      const over = this.state.layout[key] || {};
      return new Map(spec.nodes.map((n) => [n.id, over[n.id] ? { x: over[n.id][0], y: over[n.id][1] } : { x: n.x, y: n.y }]));
    }

    moveNode(id, x, y, { silent = false } = {}) {
      const key = this.state.modelKey;
      const layout = { ...this.state.layout, [key]: { ...(this.state.layout[key] || {}), [id]: [Math.round(x * 10) / 10, Math.round(y * 10) / 10] } };
      if (silent) this.state.layout = layout;
      else this.set({ layout });
    }

    resetLayout() {
      const layout = { ...this.state.layout };
      delete layout[this.state.modelKey];
      this.set({ layout });
    }

    /** Lay the current graph out again automatically (keeps the result as the graph's layout). */
    autoLayout() {
      const spec = cloneSpec(this.spec());
      const pos = MG.layout.layered(spec.nodes.map((n) => n.id), spec.edges, { roleOf: (id) => this.graph().role(id) });
      const layout = { ...this.state.layout, [this.state.modelKey]: Object.fromEntries([...pos].map(([id, p]) => [id, [p.x, p.y]])) };
      this.set({ layout });
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- statement analysis

    analysis() {
      if (this._analysis) return this._analysis;
      const text = this.state.statement;
      if (!text) return (this._analysis = null);
      const graph = this.graph();
      const out = { text, statement: null, error: null };
      try {
        out.statement = MG.Statement.parse(text).check(graph);
      } catch (e) {
        out.error = e.message;
        return (this._analysis = out);
      }
      const st = out.statement;
      out.implied = MG.dsep.implies(graph, st);
      out.testable = MG.Statement.testable(graph, st);
      out.recorded = MG.Statement.recordedForm(graph, st);
      out.whyNot = MG.Statement.whyNotTestable(graph, st);
      out.open = { paths: [], truncated: false };
      if (!out.implied) {
        for (const a of MG.sorted(st.x)) for (const b of MG.sorted(st.y)) {
          const r = MG.dsep.paths(graph, a, b, st.z, { onlyOpen: true, maxPaths: 200 - out.open.paths.length });
          out.open.paths.push(...r.paths);
          out.open.truncated = out.open.truncated || r.truncated;
        }
        out.open.paths.sort((p, q) => p.path.length - q.path.length);
      }
      out.openEdges = edgeSet(out.open.paths);
      out._blocked = null;
      out.blocked = () => {
        if (!out._blocked) {
          const res = { paths: [], truncated: false };
          const cap = 400;
          for (const a of MG.sorted(st.x)) for (const b of MG.sorted(st.y)) {
            // iterative deepening, so the shortest blocked paths are always listed first
            let complete = [];
            for (let L = 1; L <= 9; L++) {
              const r = MG.dsep.paths(graph, a, b, st.z, { maxPaths: 50000, maxLength: L });
              const blocked = r.paths.filter((p) => !p.open);
              if (blocked.length + res.paths.length > cap || r.truncated) {
                const extra = blocked.filter((p) => p.path.length - 1 === L);
                complete = complete.concat(extra.slice(0, Math.max(0, cap - res.paths.length - complete.length)));
                res.truncated = true;
                break;
              }
              complete = blocked;
              if (L === 9) res.truncated = res.truncated || false;
            }
            res.paths.push(...complete);
          }
          res.paths.sort((p, q) => p.path.length - q.path.length);
          out._blocked = res;
        }
        return out._blocked;
      };
      if (st.x.size === 1 && st.y.size === 1) out.pair = this.catalogue(graph).pair([...st.x][0], [...st.y][0]);
      return (this._analysis = out);
    }

    /** The statement in every open graph that has all of its nodes, for the comparison grid. */
    compare() {
      const a = this.analysis();
      if (!a || !a.statement) return [];
      const out = [];
      for (const m of this.listModels()) {
        const g = this.graph(m.key);
        let implied = null, error = null;
        try {
          a.statement.check(g);
          implied = MG.dsep.implies(g, a.statement);
        } catch (e) {
          error = e.message;
        }
        if (implied === null && !m.builtin) continue;
        out.push({ key: m.key, name: g.name, family: m.family, upstream: m.upstream, implied, error });
      }
      return out;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- opening graphs

    switchModel(key) {
      if (this.spec(key) && key !== this.state.modelKey) this.set({ modelKey: key });
    }

    /** Open a gallery entry (loading it the first time). */
    openGallery(key) {
      if (!this.state.custom[key]) {
        const spec = MG.gallery.load(key);
        this.set({ custom: { ...this.state.custom, [key]: spec }, userOrder: [...this.state.userOrder.filter((k) => k !== key), key], modelKey: key });
      } else this.set({ modelKey: key });
      return key;
    }

    /** Add a graph the user imported or drew; returns its key. */
    addUserGraph(spec) {
      let n = 1;
      while (this.state.custom[`user-${n}`]) n++;
      const key = `user-${n}`;
      const names = new Set(this.listModels().map((m) => m.name));
      let name = spec.name || `Graph ${n}`;
      for (let k = 2; names.has(name); k++) name = `${spec.name || "Graph"} (${k})`;
      const full = { ...cloneSpec(spec), key, name, source: spec.source || "user" };
      new MG.Graph({ nodes: full.nodes, edges: full.edges, latent: full.nodes.filter((x) => x.latent).map((x) => x.id) });
      this.set({ custom: { ...this.state.custom, [key]: full }, userOrder: [...this.state.userOrder, key], modelKey: key, node: null });
      return key;
    }

    duplicate(key = this.state.modelKey) {
      const spec = this.spec(key);
      return this.addUserGraph({ ...cloneSpec(spec), nodes: spec.nodes.map((n) => ({ ...n, ...(this.positions(key).get(n.id) || {}) })), name: `${spec.name} (copy)`, source: "user" });
    }

    removeModel(key = this.state.modelKey) {
      if (this.builtins.has(key)) return false;
      const custom = { ...this.state.custom };
      delete custom[key];
      const userOrder = this.state.userOrder.filter((k) => k !== key);
      const patch = { custom, userOrder };
      if (this.state.modelKey === key) patch.modelKey = this.data.models.length ? this.data.models[0].key : userOrder[0] || null;
      this.history = { past: this.history.past.filter((h) => h.key !== key), future: this.history.future.filter((h) => h.key !== key) };
      if (!patch.modelKey) {
        Object.assign(this.state, patch);
        patch.modelKey = this._loadFirstGallery();
        patch.custom = this.state.custom;
        patch.userOrder = this.state.userOrder;
      }
      this.set(patch);
      return true;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- edits with undo

    /** Apply fn to a copy of the current spec; validate; record it for undo. */
    _edit(fn) {
      const key = this.state.modelKey;
      const before = this.state.custom[key];
      const next = cloneSpec(this.spec(key));
      next.key = key;
      // node moves made by dragging become part of the edited graph
      const over = this.state.layout[key] || {};
      for (const n of next.nodes) if (over[n.id]) [n.x, n.y] = over[n.id];
      const result = fn(next);
      if (result === false) return false;
      if (!this.builtins.has(key)) next.edited = true;
      try {
        new MG.Graph({ nodes: next.nodes, edges: next.edges, latent: next.nodes.filter((n) => n.latent).map((n) => n.id) });
      } catch (e) {
        MG.ui.toast(e.message, "error");
        return false;
      }
      this.history.past.push({ key, before, after: next });
      this.history.future = [];
      const layout = { ...this.state.layout };
      delete layout[key];
      this.set({ custom: { ...this.state.custom, [key]: next }, layout });
      return true;
    }

    addEdge(a, b) {
      const g = this.graph();
      if (a === b) return false;
      if (g.hasEdge(a, b)) return MG.ui.toast(`${a} → ${b} is already in the graph`), false;
      if (g.wouldCreateCycle(a, b)) return MG.ui.toast(`${a} → ${b} would create a cycle`, "error"), false;
      return this._edit((s) => void s.edges.push([a, b]));
    }

    removeEdge(a, b) {
      return this._edit((s) => {
        const src = s.nodes.find((n) => n.id === a);
        if (src && src.latent && src.bidirected) return void removeNode(s, a); // a <-> b goes as a whole
        s.edges = s.edges.filter(([p, q]) => !(p === a && q === b));
        // a latent node made for a bidirected edge goes when its last edge goes
        for (const n of s.nodes.filter((x) => x.latent && x.bidirected)) if (!s.edges.some((e) => e[0] === n.id)) removeNode(s, n.id);
      });
    }

    /** a <-> b: an unrecorded common cause, added as a latent node with edges to both. */
    addBidirected(a, b) {
      if (a === b) return false;
      return this._edit((s) => {
        let id = `L_${a}_${b}`;
        for (let k = 2; s.nodes.some((n) => n.id === id); k++) id = `L_${a}_${b}_${k}`;
        const pa = s.nodes.find((n) => n.id === a), pb = s.nodes.find((n) => n.id === b);
        const at = MG.layout.freeSpot(s.nodes, (pa.x + pb.x) / 2, Math.min(pa.y, pb.y) - 60, { id, roleOf: () => "latent" });
        s.nodes.push({ id, latent: true, bidirected: [a, b], description: `unrecorded common cause of ${a} and ${b}`, x: at.x, y: at.y });
        s.edges.push([id, a], [id, b]);
      });
    }

    toggleLatent(id) {
      return this._edit((s) => {
        const n = s.nodes.find((x) => x.id === id);
        n.latent = !n.latent;
      });
    }

    addNode(id, x, y, props = {}) {
      const check = nameProblem(id, this.graph());
      if (check) return MG.ui.toast(check, "error"), false;
      return this._edit((s) => void s.nodes.push({ id, latent: false, x, y, ...props }));
    }

    renameNode(from, to) {
      if (from === to) return false;
      const g = this.graph();
      const companions = g.partiallyObserved.has(from) ? [[MG.R_PREFIX + from, MG.R_PREFIX + to], [from + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX, to + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX]] : [];
      const all = [[from, to], ...companions];
      for (const [, t] of all) {
        const check = nameProblem(t, g, all.map(([f]) => f));
        if (check) return MG.ui.toast(check, "error"), false;
      }
      const map = new Map(all);
      const ok = this._edit((s) => {
        for (const n of s.nodes) if (map.has(n.id)) n.id = map.get(n.id);
        s.edges = s.edges.map(([a, b]) => [map.get(a) || a, map.get(b) || b]);
      });
      if (ok && this.state.node === from) this.set({ node: to });
      return ok;
    }

    deleteNode(id) {
      const g = this.graph();
      const extra = g.partiallyObserved.has(id) ? [MG.R_PREFIX + id, id + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX] : [];
      const ok = this._edit((s) => {
        for (const n of [id, ...extra]) removeNode(s, n);
      });
      if (ok && this.state.node === id) this.set({ node: null });
      return ok;
    }

    /** Mark a variable as partially observed: add R_X and the proxy X_star (Equation 1). */
    makePartial(id) {
      const g = this.graph();
      if (g.partiallyObserved.has(id) || g.latent.has(id)) return false;
      const r = MG.R_PREFIX + id, star = id + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX;
      for (const n of [r, star]) if (g.has(n)) return MG.ui.toast(`${n} already exists`, "error"), false;
      return this._edit((s) => {
        const p = s.nodes.find((n) => n.id === id);
        const at1 = MG.layout.freeSpot(s.nodes, p.x + 80, p.y + 70, { id: r, roleOf: () => "missingness indicator" });
        s.nodes.push({ id: r, x: at1.x, y: at1.y, description: `missingness indicator of ${id} (1 = missing)` });
        const at2 = MG.layout.freeSpot(s.nodes, (p.x + at1.x) / 2, Math.max(p.y, at1.y) + 70, { id: star, roleOf: () => "recorded proxy" });
        s.nodes.push({ id: star, x: at2.x, y: at2.y, description: `recorded ${id}: equal to ${id} when ${r} = 0, missing otherwise` });
        s.edges.push([id, star], [r, star]);
      });
    }

    /** Make a partially observed variable fully observed: remove R_X, X_star and their edges. */
    makeFull(id) {
      if (!this.graph().partiallyObserved.has(id)) return false;
      return this._edit((s) => {
        removeNode(s, MG.R_PREFIX + id);
        removeNode(s, id + MG.PROXY_SUFFIX);
      });
    }

    setNodeFlag(id, flag, value) {
      return this._edit((s) => {
        const n = s.nodes.find((x) => x.id === id);
        n[flag] = value;
      });
    }

    /** Back to the built-in or gallery version of this graph. */
    resetModel() {
      const key = this.state.modelKey;
      if (!this.isEdited(key)) return false;
      const before = this.state.custom[key];
      const custom = { ...this.state.custom };
      let after;
      if (this.builtins.has(key)) delete custom[key];
      else if (before.source === "gallery") custom[key] = after = MG.gallery.load(key);
      else return false;
      this.history.past.push({ key, before, after });
      this.history.future = [];
      this.set({ custom });
      return true;
    }

    _restore(key, spec) {
      const custom = { ...this.state.custom };
      if (spec) custom[key] = spec;
      else delete custom[key];
      const userOrder = spec && !this.builtins.has(key) && !this.state.userOrder.includes(key) ? [...this.state.userOrder, key] : this.state.userOrder;
      this.set({ custom, userOrder, modelKey: key });
    }

    undo() {
      const step = this.history.past.pop();
      if (!step) return;
      this.history.future.push(step);
      this._restore(step.key, step.before);
    }

    redo() {
      const step = this.history.future.pop();
      if (!step) return;
      this.history.past.push(step);
      this._restore(step.key, step.after);
    }

    serialize() {
      const out = {};
      for (const k of SAVED_KEYS) out[k] = this.state[k];
      return out;
    }
  }

  function cloneSpec(spec) {
    return { ...spec, nodes: spec.nodes.map((n) => ({ ...n })), edges: spec.edges.map((e) => e.slice()) };
  }

  function removeNode(spec, id) {
    spec.nodes = spec.nodes.filter((n) => n.id !== id);
    spec.edges = spec.edges.filter(([a, b]) => a !== id && b !== id);
  }

  /** Why a name cannot be used, or null. */
  function nameProblem(id, graph, freed = []) {
    if (!id) return "a node needs a name";
    if (/[\s,|]/.test(id)) return "names cannot contain spaces, commas or |";
    if (/_\|\|_|⊥/.test(id)) return "that name looks like an independence statement";
    if (graph.has(id) && !freed.includes(id)) return `${id} already exists`;
    return null;
  }

  /** Older saved states stored edge edits instead of full specs. */
  function migrate(saved, builtins) {
    if (!saved.edits) return saved;
    const custom = { ...(saved.custom || {}) };
    for (const [key, e] of Object.entries(saved.edits)) {
      const base = builtins.get(key);
      if (!base || !(e.add.length || e.remove.length || e.latent.length || e.unlatent.length)) continue;
      const g = MG.Graph.fromPayload(base).with(e);
      custom[key] = { ...base, nodes: base.nodes.map((n) => ({ ...n, latent: g.latent.has(n.id) })), edges: g.edges };
    }
    const out = { ...saved, custom };
    delete out.edits;
    return out;
  }

  /** Directed edge keys used by a list of paths. */
  function edgeSet(paths) {
    const out = new Set();
    for (const p of paths)
      for (let i = 0; i < p.path.length - 1; i++) {
        const [u, v] = p.forward[i] ? [p.path[i], p.path[i + 1]] : [p.path[i + 1], p.path[i]];
        out.add(MG.scene.edgeKey(u, v));
      }
    return out;
  }

  MG.ui.Store = Store;
  MG.ui.cloneSpec = cloneSpec;
  MG.ui.nameProblem = nameProblem;
  MG.ui.edgeSet = edgeSet;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/math.js ---- */
/* Typeset probability expressions (MG.expr trees) as HTML: stacked fractions, Σ with the summed
 * variables underneath, proxies as X*, indicators as R with a subscript. */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { h } = MG.ui;
  const STAR = MG.PROXY_SUFFIX;

  /** A variable or column name: R_X -> R with subscript X; X_star -> X*; Yj1 -> Yj1. */
  function name(c, { lower = false } = {}) {
    let star = false;
    if (c.endsWith(STAR)) {
      star = true;
      c = c.slice(0, -STAR.length);
    }
    const primes = (c.match(/′+$/) || [""])[0];
    c = c.slice(0, c.length - primes.length).replace(/~\d+$/, "");
    const base = lower ? c.toLowerCase() : c;
    const m = base.match(/^([Rr])_(.+)$/);
    const parts = m ? [m[1], h("sub", null, m[2])] : [base];
    return h("span", { class: "mv" }, ...parts, star ? h("sup", null, "*") : null, primes || null);
  }

  /** An entry inside P(...): "Y*", "R_X = 0", or "X = x′" when the symbol differs. */
  function entry([c, v]) {
    if (typeof v === "number") return h("span", { class: "me" }, name(c), " = ", String(v));
    const base = c.endsWith(STAR) ? c.slice(0, -STAR.length) : c;
    if (v === base) return name(c);
    return h("span", { class: "me" }, name(c), " = ", name(v, { lower: true }));
  }

  function join(items) {
    const out = [];
    items.forEach((it, i) => {
      if (i) out.push(h("span", { class: "mc" }, ", "));
      out.push(it);
    });
    return out;
  }

  function render(e) {
    switch (e.t) {
      case "one":
        return h("span", null, "1");
      case "P": {
        const right = [];
        if (e.do.length) right.push(h("span", null, "do(", ...join(e.do.map(entry)), ")"));
        right.push(...e.given.map(entry));
        return h("span", { class: "mp" }, h("span", { class: "mP" }, "P"), "(", ...join(e.vars.map(entry)), right.length ? h("span", { class: "mbar" }, " | ") : null, ...join(right), ")");
      }
      case "sum":
        return h("span", { class: "ms" },
          h("span", { class: "msig" }, h("span", { class: "msym" }, "Σ"), h("span", { class: "msub" }, ...join(e.over.map((s) => name(s, { lower: true }))))),
          wrapSum(e.body));
      case "prod":
        return h("span", { class: "mprod" }, ...e.factors.map((f) => (f.t === "sum" ? h("span", { class: "mgroup" }, "[", render(f), "]") : render(f))));
      case "frac":
        return h("span", { class: "mfrac" }, h("span", { class: "mnum" }, render(e.num)), h("span", { class: "mden" }, render(e.den)));
    }
    return h("span", null, "?");
  }

  function wrapSum(body) {
    return render(body);
  }

  /** A typeset formula with copy buttons for text and LaTeX. */
  function formula(e, { lhs = null } = {}) {
    const box = h("div", { class: "formula-box" },
      h("div", { class: "math" }, lhs ? [h("span", { class: "mlhs" }, lhs), h("span", { class: "meq" }, " = ")] : null, render(e)),
      h("div", { class: "formula-tools" },
        h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => MG.ui.copyText(MG.expr.toText(e)) }, MG.ui.icon("copy"), "Text"),
        h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => MG.ui.copyText(MG.expr.toLatex(e)) }, MG.ui.icon("copy"), "LaTeX"),
        h("details", { class: "estimator" }, h("summary", null, "Estimator from counts"),
          h("p", { class: "muted" }, "Replace each probability by its sample frequency (Remark 1): # counts records with those values; N is the number of records."),
          h("div", { class: "mono", style: { overflowWrap: "anywhere" } }, MG.expr.estimatorText(e)))));
    return box;
  }

  /** A query such as P(Y | do(X), Z) as typeset text. */
  function query(target, given = [], doSet = []) {
    return render(MG.expr.P(target, [...given], [...doSet]));
  }

  MG.ui.math = { render, formula, name, query };
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/graph_view.js ---- */
/* MG.ui.GraphView: the live, interactive graph.
 *
 * Draws with the same geometry as the figure exporter (MG.geometry) and takes colours from
 * CSS tokens, so it follows the theme. Interaction:
 *   drag background / two-finger scroll   pan          wheel, pinch, ctrl+scroll   zoom
 *   drag a node                            move it      double-click background     fit
 *   click a node                           select       shift+click                 toggle in Z
 * Node clicks are passed to onNodeClick so the app decides what they mean (select, pick a
 * query slot, or draw an edge in edit mode).
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { s, h } = MG.ui;
  const G = MG.geometry;
  const edgeKey = MG.scene.edgeKey;
  const ROLE_CLASS = MG.ui.ROLE_CLASS;

  class GraphView {
    constructor(root, store, { onNodeClick, onEdgeClick, onBackgroundClick, onBackgroundDblClick } = {}) {
      this.root = root;
      this.store = store;
      this.onNodeClick = onNodeClick || (() => {});
      this.onEdgeClick = onEdgeClick || (() => {});
      this.onBackgroundClick = onBackgroundClick || (() => {});
      this.onBackgroundDblClick = onBackgroundDblClick || null;
      this.view = { k: 1, tx: 0, ty: 0 };
      this.nodeEls = new Map();
      this.edgeEls = new Map();
      this._graph = null;
      this._hiddenSig = null;
      this._pointers = new Map();
      this._raf = null;
      this._anim = null;

      this.svg = s("svg", { class: "graph", role: "img", "aria-label": "m-graph" });
      const defs = s("defs");
      this.pattern = s("pattern", { id: "mg-dots", width: 22, height: 22, patternUnits: "userSpaceOnUse" }, s("circle", { cx: 1, cy: 1, r: 1, style: "fill:var(--canvas-dot)" }));
      defs.appendChild(this.pattern);
      this.bg = s("rect", { class: "bg", x: 0, y: 0, width: "100%", height: "100%" });
      this.viewport = s("g", { class: "viewport" });
      this.edgeLayer = s("g", { class: "edges" });
      this.nodeLayer = s("g", { class: "nodes" });
      this.ghost = s("path", { class: "ghost", d: "" });
      this.viewport.append(this.edgeLayer, this.ghost, this.nodeLayer);
      this.svg.append(defs, this.bg, this.viewport);
      root.prepend(this.svg);
      this.tooltip = root.querySelector("#tooltip");

      this._bind();
      store.on((changed) => this._onChange(changed));
      new ResizeObserver(() => this._onResize()).observe(root);
      this.sync();
      if (store.state.view && store.state.view.key === store.state.modelKey) {
        this._userView = true;
        this.setView(store.state.view, false);
      }
      else requestAnimationFrame(() => this.fit(false));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- building

    sync() {
      const graph = this.store.graph();
      const hidden = new Set(this.store.state.hiddenRoles);
      if (this.store.state.filters.hideProxies) hidden.add("recorded proxy");
      const sig = [...hidden].sort().join("|");
      if (graph !== this._graph || sig !== this._hiddenSig) {
        this._build(graph, hidden);
        this._graph = graph;
        this._hiddenSig = sig;
      }
      this.updatePositions();
      this.updateClasses();
    }

    _build(graph, hidden) {
      this.edgeLayer.replaceChildren();
      this.nodeLayer.replaceChildren();
      this.nodeEls.clear();
      this.edgeEls.clear();
      this.visible = new Set(graph.ids.filter((id) => !hidden.has(graph.role(id))));
      for (const id of graph.ids) {
        if (!this.visible.has(id)) continue;
        const role = graph.role(id);
        const layout = G.nodeLayout(id, role);
        const g = s("g", { class: `node role-${ROLE_CLASS[role]}`, "data-id": id, tabindex: -1 });
        const halo = s("path", { class: "halo", d: G.toSvgPath(G.nodeShape("fully observed", layout.w + 9, layout.h + 9).outline) });
        const blocker = s("path", { class: "blocker", d: G.toSvgPath(G.nodeShape("fully observed", layout.w + 9, layout.h + 9).outline) });
        const shape = s("path", { class: "shape", d: G.toSvgPath(layout.shape.outline) });
        g.append(halo, blocker, shape);
        if (layout.shape.inner) g.appendChild(s("path", { class: "inner", d: G.toSvgPath(layout.shape.inner) }));
        const label = s("text", { x: 0, y: 4.6, "text-anchor": "middle" }, id);
        g.appendChild(label);
        const badge = s("g", { class: "badge", transform: `translate(${layout.w / 2 - 2},${-layout.h / 2 - 2})`, style: "display:none" }, s("circle", { r: 7.5 }), s("text", { y: 3.5 }, ""));
        g.appendChild(badge);
        this.nodeLayer.appendChild(g);
        this.nodeEls.set(id, { g, layout, badge, badgeText: badge.querySelector("text") });
      }
      for (const [a, b] of graph.edges) {
        if (!this.visible.has(a) || !this.visible.has(b)) continue;
        const g = s("g", { class: "edge", "data-a": a, "data-b": b });
        const hit = s("path", { class: "hit" });
        const line = s("path", { class: "line" });
        const head = s("path", { class: "head" });
        g.append(line, head, hit);
        this.edgeLayer.appendChild(g);
        this.edgeEls.set(edgeKey(a, b), { g, line, head, hit, a, b });
      }
      this.svg.setAttribute("aria-label", `${graph.name}: ${graph.ids.length} nodes, ${graph.edgeCount} edges`);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- drawing

    updatePositions() {
      const pos = this.store.positions();
      this._pos = pos;
      for (const [id, el] of this.nodeEls) {
        const p = pos.get(id);
        el.g.setAttribute("transform", `translate(${p.x},${p.y})`);
      }
      for (const el of this.edgeEls.values()) this._drawEdge(el);
    }

    _drawEdge(el) {
      const a = this._pos.get(el.a), b = this._pos.get(el.b);
      const geo = G.edgeGeometry({ ...a, layout: this.nodeEls.get(el.a).layout }, { ...b, layout: this.nodeEls.get(el.b).layout });
      const d = `M${geo.x1.toFixed(1)} ${geo.y1.toFixed(1)}L${geo.x2.toFixed(1)} ${geo.y2.toFixed(1)}`;
      el.line.setAttribute("d", d);
      el.hit.setAttribute("d", `M${geo.x1.toFixed(1)} ${geo.y1.toFixed(1)}L${geo.tipX.toFixed(1)} ${geo.tipY.toFixed(1)}`);
      el.head.setAttribute("d", G.toSvgPath(geo.head, 1));
      el.g.style.display = geo.visible ? "" : "none";
    }

    updateClasses() {
      const st = this.store.state;
      const tr = this.store.transient;
      const graph = this._graph;
      const a = this.store.analysis();
      const emph = tr.emphasis && !(a && a.statement) ? tr.emphasis : null;
      const valid = !!(a && a.statement);
      const S = valid ? a.statement : emph;
      const focus = tr.focusPath;
      const focusEdges = focus ? MG.ui.edgeSet([focus]) : new Set();
      const focusNodes = new Set(focus ? focus.path : []);
      const openNodes = new Set();
      if (valid) for (const k of a.openEdges) k.split("\u0001").forEach((n) => openNodes.add(n));
      const hover = !valid && !focus ? tr.hoverNode : null;
      const hoverNb = hover && graph.has(hover) ? graph.neighbours(hover) : null;

      for (const [id, el] of this.nodeEls) {
        const c = el.g.classList;
        const inX = S && S.x.has(id), inY = S && S.y.has(id), inZ = S && S.z.has(id);
        c.toggle("is-selected", st.node === id);
        c.toggle("is-xy", !!(inX || inY));
        c.toggle("is-z", !!inZ);
        const dim = focus ? !focusNodes.has(id) && !inX && !inY && !inZ : valid ? !inX && !inY && !inZ && !openNodes.has(id) : false;
        el.g.classList.toggle("is-do", !!(emph && inX));
        c.toggle("is-dim", dim);
        c.toggle("is-faded", !!(hoverNb && id !== hover && !hoverNb.has(id)));
        c.toggle("is-blocker", !!(focus && focus.blocker === id));
        c.toggle("is-pick-source", tr.editSource === id);
        const badge = inX ? (emph ? "do" : "X") : inY ? "Y" : inZ ? "Z" : null;
        el.badge.style.display = badge ? "" : "none";
        if (badge) {
          el.badgeText.textContent = badge;
          el.badge.classList.toggle("badge-z", badge === "Z");
        }
      }
      for (const [k, el] of this.edgeEls) {
        const c = el.g.classList;
        const open = valid && a.openEdges.has(k);
        const isFocus = focusEdges.has(k);
        c.toggle("is-open", !!open && !focus);
        c.toggle("is-focus", isFocus);
        c.toggle("is-dim", focus ? !isFocus : valid ? !open : false);
        c.toggle("is-neighbour", !!(hover && (el.a === hover || el.b === hover)));
        c.toggle("is-faded", !!(hover && el.a !== hover && el.b !== hover));
      }
      this.svg.classList.toggle("is-editing", tr.editMode);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- view transform

    setView(v, animate = true) {
      const target = { k: MG.clamp(v.k, 0.12, 4), tx: v.tx, ty: v.ty };
      if (!animate || matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches) {
        cancelAnimationFrame(this._anim);
        this.view = target;
        this._applyView();
        return;
      }
      const from = { ...this.view };
      const t0 = performance.now();
      const dur = 320;
      cancelAnimationFrame(this._anim);
      const tick = (now) => {
        const t = Math.min(1, (now - t0) / dur);
        const e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
        this.view = { k: from.k + (target.k - from.k) * e, tx: from.tx + (target.tx - from.tx) * e, ty: from.ty + (target.ty - from.ty) * e };
        this._applyView();
        if (t < 1) this._anim = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
      };
      this._anim = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
    }

    _applyView() {
      const { k, tx, ty } = this.view;
      const t = `translate(${tx.toFixed(2)},${ty.toFixed(2)}) scale(${k.toFixed(4)})`;
      this.viewport.setAttribute("transform", t);
      this.pattern.setAttribute("patternTransform", t);
      this.store.state.view = { ...this.view, key: this.store.state.modelKey };
      if (this.onViewChange) this.onViewChange(this.view);
    }

    bounds() {
      let x0 = Infinity, y0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y1 = -Infinity;
      for (const [id, el] of this.nodeEls) {
        const p = this._pos.get(id);
        x0 = Math.min(x0, p.x - el.layout.w / 2);
        x1 = Math.max(x1, p.x + el.layout.w / 2);
        y0 = Math.min(y0, p.y - el.layout.h / 2 - 10);
        y1 = Math.max(y1, p.y + el.layout.h / 2);
      }
      return x0 === Infinity ? { x0: -200, y0: -150, x1: 200, y1: 150 } : { x0, y0, x1, y1 };
    }

    fit(animate = true, nodes = null) {
      if (animate) this._userView = !!(nodes && nodes.length);
      const W = this.svg.clientWidth, H = this.svg.clientHeight;
      if (!W || !H) return;
      let b = this.bounds();
      if (nodes && nodes.length) {
        const pts = nodes.filter((n) => this._pos.has(n)).map((n) => this._pos.get(n));
        if (pts.length) b = { x0: Math.min(...pts.map((p) => p.x)) - 60, x1: Math.max(...pts.map((p) => p.x)) + 60, y0: Math.min(...pts.map((p) => p.y)) - 50, y1: Math.max(...pts.map((p) => p.y)) + 50 };
      }
      const padL = 32, padR = 70, padT = 40, padB = 70;
      const k = Math.min((W - padL - padR) / (b.x1 - b.x0), (H - padT - padB) / (b.y1 - b.y0), 1.6);
      const tx = padL + (W - padL - padR - (b.x1 - b.x0) * k) / 2 - b.x0 * k;
      const ty = padT + (H - padT - padB - (b.y1 - b.y0) * k) / 2 - b.y0 * k;
      this.setView({ k, tx, ty }, animate);
    }

    zoomBy(factor, cx, cy, animate = true) {
      this._userView = true;
      const W = this.svg.clientWidth, H = this.svg.clientHeight;
      cx = cx === undefined ? W / 2 : cx;
      cy = cy === undefined ? H / 2 : cy;
      const k = MG.clamp(this.view.k * factor, 0.12, 4);
      const f = k / this.view.k;
      this.setView({ k, tx: cx - (cx - this.view.tx) * f, ty: cy - (cy - this.view.ty) * f }, animate);
    }

    centerOn(id) {
      const p = this._pos && this._pos.get(id);
      if (!p) return;
      const W = this.svg.clientWidth, H = this.svg.clientHeight;
      const x = p.x * this.view.k + this.view.tx, y = p.y * this.view.k + this.view.ty;
      if (x > 60 && x < W - 80 && y > 60 && y < H - 80) return; // already in view
      this.setView({ k: this.view.k, tx: W / 2 - p.x * this.view.k, ty: H / 2 - p.y * this.view.k });
    }

    toGraph(clientX, clientY) {
      const r = this.svg.getBoundingClientRect();
      return { x: (clientX - r.left - this.view.tx) / this.view.k, y: (clientY - r.top - this.view.ty) / this.view.k };
    }

    _onResize() {
      // keep the whole graph in view until the user pans or zooms
      if (!this._userView) this.fit(false);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- events

    _onChange(changed) {
      const structural = changed.has("modelKey") || changed.has("edits") || changed.has("hiddenRoles") || changed.has("filters");
      if (structural) {
        const modelChanged = changed.has("modelKey");
        this.sync();
        if (modelChanged) requestAnimationFrame(() => this.fit(true));
        return;
      }
      if (changed.has("layout")) this.updatePositions();
      this.updateClasses();
    }

    _bind() {
      const svg = this.svg;
      svg.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => this._down(e));
      svg.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => this._move(e));
      svg.addEventListener("pointerup", (e) => this._up(e));
      svg.addEventListener("pointercancel", (e) => this._up(e, true));
      svg.addEventListener("pointerleave", () => this._hideTooltip());
      svg.addEventListener("wheel", (e) => this._wheel(e), { passive: false });
      svg.addEventListener("dblclick", (e) => {
        if (e.target.closest(".node") || e.target.closest(".edge")) return;
        if (this.store.transient.editMode && this.onBackgroundDblClick) this.onBackgroundDblClick(this.toGraph(e.clientX, e.clientY), e);
        else this.fit(true);
      });
      svg.addEventListener("pointerover", (e) => {
        const n = e.target.closest(".node");
        const ed = e.target.closest(".edge");
        if (n) this.store.setTransient({ hoverNode: n.dataset.id });
        else if (ed) this.store.setTransient({ hoverEdge: edgeKey(ed.dataset.a, ed.dataset.b) });
      });
      svg.addEventListener("pointerout", (e) => {
        const n = e.target.closest(".node");
        if (n && !n.contains(e.relatedTarget)) this.store.setTransient({ hoverNode: null });
        const ed = e.target.closest(".edge");
        if (ed && !ed.contains(e.relatedTarget)) this.store.setTransient({ hoverEdge: null });
      });
    }

    _down(e) {
      if (e.button !== 0 && e.pointerType === "mouse") return;
      this.svg.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      this._pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
      const nodeEl = e.target.closest(".node");
      const edgeEl = e.target.closest(".edge");
      if (this._pointers.size === 2) {
        const [p, q] = [...this._pointers.values()];
        this._gesture = { type: "pinch", dist: Math.hypot(p.x - q.x, p.y - q.y), view: { ...this.view }, cx: (p.x + q.x) / 2, cy: (p.y + q.y) / 2 };
        return;
      }
      if (nodeEl) {
        const id = nodeEl.dataset.id;
        const p = this._pos.get(id);
        this._gesture = { type: "node", id, sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, x: p.x, y: p.y, moved: false, shift: e.shiftKey };
      } else if (edgeEl) {
        this._gesture = { type: "edge", a: edgeEl.dataset.a, b: edgeEl.dataset.b, sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, moved: false, view: { ...this.view } };
      } else {
        this._gesture = { type: "pan", sx: e.clientX, sy: e.clientY, view: { ...this.view }, moved: false };
      }
    }

    _move(e) {
      if (this._pointers.has(e.pointerId)) this._pointers.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
      const g = this._gesture;
      const tr = this.store.transient;
      if (tr.editMode && tr.editSource && this._pos) {
        const src = this._pos.get(tr.editSource);
        const pt = this.toGraph(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        if (src) this.ghost.setAttribute("d", `M${src.x} ${src.y}L${pt.x} ${pt.y}`);
      }
      if (!g) return this._tooltipFor(e);
      if (g.type === "pinch" && this._pointers.size === 2) {
        const [p, q] = [...this._pointers.values()];
        const dist = Math.hypot(p.x - q.x, p.y - q.y);
        const r = this.svg.getBoundingClientRect();
        const k = MG.clamp(g.view.k * (dist / g.dist), 0.12, 4);
        const f = k / g.view.k;
        const cx = g.cx - r.left, cy = g.cy - r.top;
        const mx = (p.x + q.x) / 2 - g.cx, my = (p.y + q.y) / 2 - g.cy;
        this._userView = true;
        this.view = { k, tx: cx - (cx - g.view.tx) * f + mx, ty: cy - (cy - g.view.ty) * f + my };
        this._applyView();
        return;
      }
      const dx = e.clientX - g.sx, dy = e.clientY - g.sy;
      if (!g.moved && Math.hypot(dx, dy) < 4) return;
      g.moved = true;
      this._hideTooltip();
      if (g.type === "node") {
        const nx = g.x + dx / this.view.k, ny = g.y + dy / this.view.k;
        this.store.moveNode(g.id, nx, ny, { silent: true });
        this._schedulePositions();
      } else {
        if (g.type === "pan" || g.type === "edge") {
          this.svg.classList.add("is-panning");
          this._userView = true;
          this.view = { k: g.view.k, tx: g.view.tx + dx, ty: g.view.ty + dy };
          this._applyView();
        }
      }
    }

    _up(e, cancelled = false) {
      this._pointers.delete(e.pointerId);
      const g = this._gesture;
      this.svg.classList.remove("is-panning");
      if (!g) return;
      if (g.type === "pinch") {
        if (this._pointers.size === 0) this._gesture = null;
        return;
      }
      this._gesture = null;
      if (cancelled) return;
      if (g.type === "node") {
        if (g.moved) this.store.set({ layout: { ...this.store.state.layout } });
        else this.onNodeClick(g.id, e);
      } else if (g.type === "edge" && !g.moved) {
        this.onEdgeClick(g.a, g.b, e);
      } else if (g.type === "pan" && !g.moved) {
        this.onBackgroundClick(e);
      }
    }

    _schedulePositions() {
      if (this._raf) return;
      this._raf = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        this._raf = null;
        this.updatePositions();
      });
    }

    _wheel(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      this._userView = true;
      const r = this.svg.getBoundingClientRect();
      const cx = e.clientX - r.left, cy = e.clientY - r.top;
      const mouseWheel = e.deltaMode === 1 || (e.deltaX === 0 && Math.abs(e.deltaY) >= 50 && Number.isInteger(e.deltaY));
      if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || mouseWheel) {
        const scale = e.deltaMode === 1 ? 0.05 : e.ctrlKey ? 0.012 : 0.0022;
        const factor = Math.exp(-e.deltaY * scale);
        const k = MG.clamp(this.view.k * factor, 0.12, 4);
        const f = k / this.view.k;
        cancelAnimationFrame(this._anim);
        this.view = { k, tx: cx - (cx - this.view.tx) * f, ty: cy - (cy - this.view.ty) * f };
      } else {
        this.view = { ...this.view, tx: this.view.tx - e.deltaX, ty: this.view.ty - e.deltaY };
      }
      this._applyView();
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- tooltip

    _tooltipFor(e) {
      const n = e.target.closest && e.target.closest(".node");
      const ed = e.target.closest && e.target.closest(".edge");
      if (!n && !ed) return this._hideTooltip();
      const graph = this._graph;
      let html;
      if (n) {
        const id = n.dataset.id;
        const meta = graph.meta.get(id) || {};
        html = h("div", null,
          h("strong", null, id), " ", h("span", { class: "tt-role" }, graph.role(id)),
          meta.description ? h("div", { class: "tt-desc" }, meta.description) : null,
          h("div", { class: "tt-meta" }, `${graph.parents(id).size} parents · ${graph.children(id).size} children`));
      } else {
        const tr = this.store.transient;
        html = h("div", null, h("strong", null, `${ed.dataset.a} → ${ed.dataset.b}`), tr.editMode ? h("div", { class: "tt-desc" }, "Click to delete this arrow") : null);
      }
      const r = this.root.getBoundingClientRect();
      this.tooltip.replaceChildren(html);
      this.tooltip.hidden = false;
      const tw = this.tooltip.offsetWidth, th = this.tooltip.offsetHeight;
      let x = e.clientX - r.left + 14, y = e.clientY - r.top + 16;
      if (x + tw > r.width - 8) x = e.clientX - r.left - tw - 14;
      if (y + th > r.height - 8) y = e.clientY - r.top - th - 12;
      this.tooltip.style.left = x + "px";
      this.tooltip.style.top = y + "px";
    }

    _hideTooltip() {
      if (this.tooltip) this.tooltip.hidden = true;
    }

    clearGhost() {
      this.ghost.setAttribute("d", "");
    }
  }

  MG.ui.GraphView = GraphView;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/panels/inspector.js ---- */
/* Node tab: everything about one node, including every independence it takes part in.
 * With no node selected it shows the model overview (roles, structure, self-masking). */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { h, icon, roleGlyph, statementText } = MG.ui;

  class InspectorPanel {
    constructor(store, app) {
      this.store = store;
      this.app = app;
      this.testableOnly = false;
    }

    get title() {
      return "Node";
    }

    shouldRender(changed) {
      return ["node", "modelKey", "edits", "statement", "filters"].some((k) => changed.has(k));
    }

    render(body) {
      const id = this.store.state.node;
      const graph = this.store.graph();
      body.replaceChildren(id && graph.has(id) ? this._node(graph, id) : this._overview(graph));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- overview

    _overview(graph) {
      const roles = MG.ROLES.map((r) => ({ role: r, nodes: MG.sorted(graph.ids.filter((n) => graph.role(n) === r)) })).filter((r) => r.nodes.length);
      const problems = graph.validate();
      const masking = graph.selfMasking();
      const mech = graph.indicators.size ? MG.missing.classify(graph) : null;
      const t2 = graph.indicators.size ? MG.missing.theorem2(graph) : null;
      const cat = this.store.catalogue(graph);
      const sum = cat.summary();
      const total = graph.ids.length;
      const spec = this.store.model;
      if (!graph.ids.length)
        return h("div", { class: "empty" }, h("p", null, h("b", null, "An empty graph. "), "Turn on Edit (E) and double-click the canvas to add nodes, or use Import a DAG in the graph menu."),
          h("button", { class: "btn small primary", onClick: () => this.app.toggleEdit(true) }, icon("edit"), "Start editing"));
      return h("div", null,
        spec.description || spec.note ? h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
          spec.group ? h("div", { class: "eyebrow" }, spec.group) : null,
          spec.description ? h("p", { style: { margin: "0 0 6px" } }, spec.description) : null,
          spec.note ? h("div", { class: "problem" }, h("b", null, "Note on the paper: "), spec.note) : null) : null,
        h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
          h("h3", null, graph.name, h("span", { class: "aside" }, `${graph.ids.length} nodes · ${graph.edgeCount} edges`)),
          h("div", { class: "role-bar", "aria-hidden": "true" }, roles.map((r) => h("span", { style: { width: `${(100 * r.nodes.length) / total}%`, background: `var(--r-${MG.ui.ROLE_CLASS[r.role]})` } }))),
          h("dl", { class: "kv" }, roles.map((r) => [
            h("dt", null, h("span", { style: { display: "inline-flex", gap: "6px", alignItems: "center" } }, roleGlyph(r.role, 18, 11), r.role)),
            h("dd", null, h("div", { class: "chip-row" }, r.nodes.map((n) => this._chip(n)))),
          ]))),
        h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
          h("h3", null, "Independences", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "between recorded nodes")),
          h("div", { class: "stat-grid" },
            stat(sum.sets, "minimal separating sets"),
            stat(sum.testable, "testable in recorded data"),
            stat(sum.separable, "pairs that can be separated"),
            stat(sum.inseparable + sum.adjacent, "pairs never independent")),
          h("div", { class: "form-row" }, h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => this.app.showTab("conditions") }, "Browse all", icon("arrow")))),
        h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
          h("h3", null, "Missing-data structure"),
          problems.length ? problems.map((p) => h("div", { class: "problem" }, p)) : h("div", { class: "problem ok" }, "Valid m-graph: every proxy has exactly its variable and indicator as parents."),
          Object.keys(masking).length
            ? Object.entries(masking).map(([v, why]) => h("div", { class: "problem" }, h("b", { class: "mono" }, v), " self-masking: ", why))
            : h("div", { class: "problem ok" }, "No self-masking: no result drives its own missingness, directly or through latent nodes."),
          mech ? h("div", { class: mech.mechanism === "MNAR" ? "problem" : "problem ok" }, h("b", null, mech.mechanism + ": "), mech.text) : null,
          t2 ? h("div", { class: t2.recoverable === true ? "problem ok" : "problem" },
            t2.applies ? (t2.recoverable ? "The joint distribution is recoverable (Theorem 2)." : `The joint distribution is not recoverable (Theorem 2): ${t2.violations.map((v) => v.text).join("; ")}.`)
              : "Theorem 2 does not apply: some missingness indicators share an edge or an unrecorded cause.") : null,
          mech ? h("div", { class: "form-row" }, h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => this.app.showTab("missing") }, "Missing data", icon("arrow")), h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => this.app.showTab("causal") }, "Causal effect", icon("arrow"))) : h("div", { class: "form-row" }, h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => this.app.showTab("causal") }, "Causal effect", icon("arrow")))),
        h("div", { class: "empty" }, h("p", null, "Click a node to see its parents, children and every independence it takes part in."))
      );
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- one node

    _node(graph, id) {
      const role = graph.role(id);
      const meta = graph.meta.get(id) || {};
      const cat = graph.recorded.has(id) ? this.store.catalogue(graph) : null;
      const head = h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("div", { class: "node-head" },
          roleGlyph(role, 30, 18),
          h("div", null, h("h2", null, id), h("div", { class: "desc" }, meta.description || role), h("div", { style: { marginTop: "6px" } }, h("span", { class: "tag role" }, role))),
          h("div", { class: "actions-row" },
            MG.ui.iconButton("query", "Use in a query", { onClick: () => this.app.queryFrom(id) }),
            MG.ui.iconButton("target", "Centre on graph", { onClick: () => this.app.view.centerOn(id) }),
            MG.ui.iconButton("close", "Clear selection", { onClick: () => this.store.set({ node: null }) }))),
        h("dl", { class: "kv", style: { marginTop: "12px" } },
          h("dt", null, "Parents"), h("dd", null, this._chips(graph.parents(id))),
          h("dt", null, "Children"), h("dd", null, this._chips(graph.children(id))),
          h("dt", null, "Ancestors"), h("dd", { class: "muted" }, String(graph.ancestors(id).size)),
          h("dt", null, "Descendants"), h("dd", { class: "muted" }, String(graph.descendants(id).size))),
      );
      const sections = [head, this._changeNode(graph, id)];
      const lm = MG.localMarkov(graph, id);
      if (lm) sections.push(h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("h3", null, "Local Markov", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "independent of its non-descendants given its parents")), this._stmtRow(graph, lm, { tag: h("span", { class: "tag local" }, "local") })));
      if (!cat) {
        sections.push(h("div", { class: "empty" }, h("p", null, "Latent nodes cannot be conditioned on or tested, so pairwise independences are listed for recorded nodes only.")));
        return h("div", null, sections);
      }
      const pairs = cat.forNode(id);
      const sep = pairs.filter((p) => (this.testableOnly ? p.testable.length : p.separators.length));
      const never = pairs.filter((p) => !p.separators.length);
      const nSets = sep.reduce((n, p) => n + (this.testableOnly ? p.testable.length : p.separators.length + p.testable.filter((z) => !p.separators.some((s) => MG.setEq(s, z))).length), 0);
      const toggle = h("label", { class: "toggle" }, h("input", { type: "checkbox", checked: this.testableOnly, onChange: (e) => { this.testableOnly = e.target.checked; this.render(this.app.body); } }), "Testable only");
      sections.push(h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("h3", null, "Independent of", h("span", { class: "aside" }, `${MG.plural(sep.length, "node")} · ${MG.plural(nSets, "statement")}`)),
        h("div", { class: "filters", style: { marginBottom: "6px" } }, toggle),
        sep.length ? sep.map((p) => this._partner(graph, id, p)) : h("p", { class: "muted" }, this.testableOnly ? "No testable statements involve this node." : "No recorded set separates this node from any other.")));
      if (never.length && !this.testableOnly)
        sections.push(h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
          h("h3", null, "Never independent of", h("span", { class: "aside" }, MG.plural(never.length, "node"))),
          h("div", { class: "chip-row" }, never.map((p) => this._chip(p.other(id), p.adjacent ? "adjacent" : "no recorded separator")))));
      return h("div", null, sections);
    }

    /** Rename, change the role, or delete the node. Every change can be undone. */
    _changeNode(graph, id) {
      const store = this.store;
      const role = graph.role(id);
      const meta = graph.meta.get(id) || {};
      const name = h("input", { class: "input mono grow", value: id, "aria-label": "Node name" });
      const rename = () => {
        const to = name.value.trim();
        if (to && to !== id) store.renameNode(id, to);
      };
      name.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => e.key === "Enter" && rename());
      const toggle = (label, checked, onChange, title) =>
        h("label", { class: "toggle", title }, h("input", { type: "checkbox", checked, onChange: (e) => onChange(e.target.checked) }), label);
      const rows = [h("div", { class: "form-row", style: { marginTop: 0 } }, name, h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: rename }, "Rename"))];
      const flags = [];
      if (meta.bidirected) {
        flags.push(h("span", { class: "muted", style: { fontSize: "12px" } }, `Unrecorded common cause of ${meta.bidirected.join(" and ")}.`));
      } else {
        if (role === "fully observed" || role === "partially observed")
          flags.push(toggle("Partially observed", role === "partially observed", (on) => (on ? store.makePartial(id) : store.makeFull(id)), "Adds or removes its missingness indicator R_X and recorded proxy X_star"));
        if (role === "fully observed" || role === "latent")
          flags.push(toggle("Unmeasured (latent)", role === "latent", () => store.toggleLatent(id), "A latent variable is never recorded and cannot be conditioned on"));
        if (role !== "latent" && role !== "missingness indicator" && role !== "recorded proxy") {
          flags.push(toggle("Exposure", !!meta.exposure, (on) => store.setNodeFlag(id, "exposure", on), "Default treatment in the Causal effect tab"));
          flags.push(toggle("Outcome", !!meta.outcome, (on) => store.setNodeFlag(id, "outcome", on), "Default outcome in the Causal effect tab"));
        }
      }
      if (flags.length) rows.push(h("div", { class: "filters", style: { marginTop: "10px" } }, flags));
      rows.push(h("div", { class: "form-row" },
        h("button", { class: "btn small danger", onClick: () => store.deleteNode(id) }, icon("close"), meta.bidirected ? "Remove this common cause" : role === "partially observed" ? "Delete node, its indicator and proxy" : "Delete node"),
        h("span", { class: "faint", style: { fontSize: "11.5px" } }, "⌘Z undoes any change")));
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("h3", null, "Change this node"), rows);
    }

    _partner(graph, id, p) {
      const other = p.other(id);
      const rows = [];
      const sets = this.testableOnly ? [] : p.separators;
      for (const z of sets) {
        const st = new MG.Statement([id], [other], z);
        rows.push(this._stmtRow(graph, st, { given: true, testable: p.isTestableSet(z) }));
      }
      for (const z of p.testable) {
        if (!this.testableOnly && p.separators.some((s) => MG.setEq(s, z))) continue;
        rows.push(this._stmtRow(graph, new MG.Statement([id], [other], z), { given: true, testable: true }));
      }
      return h("div", { class: "partner" },
        h("div", { class: "partner-head" }, this._chip(other), h("span", { class: "count" }, MG.plural(rows.length, "set"))),
        rows);
    }

    _stmtRow(graph, st, { tag = null, given = false, testable = false } = {}) {
      const active = this.store.state.statement === st.toString();
      let recorded = testable ? MG.Statement.recordedForm(graph, st) : null;
      if (recorded === st.toString()) recorded = null;
      const text = given ? h("span", { class: "stmt" }, h("span", { class: "given" }, "given "), st.z.size ? MG.sorted(st.z).join(", ") : "nothing (marginal)") : statementText(st);
      return h("div", { class: ["stmt-row", active ? "is-active" : null], tabindex: 0, onClick: () => this.app.showStatement(st.toString(), { stay: true }), onKeydown: (e) => e.key === "Enter" && this.app.showStatement(st.toString(), { stay: true }) },
        h("div", null, text, recorded ? h("div", { class: "sub" }, recorded) : null),
        h("div", { class: "row-tools" },
          tag || (testable ? h("span", { class: "tag testable" }, "testable") : null),
          h("button", { class: "row-btn", title: "Open in Query", "aria-label": "Open in Query", onClick: (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); this.app.showStatement(st.toString()); } }, icon("arrow"))));
    }

    _chips(nodes) {
      const list = MG.sorted(nodes);
      return list.length ? h("div", { class: "chip-row" }, list.map((n) => this._chip(n))) : h("span", { class: "faint" }, "none");
    }

    _chip(n, title) {
      const role = this.store.graph().role(n);
      return h("button", { class: "chip", title: title || role, onClick: () => this.app.selectNode(n) }, h("span", { class: "dot", style: { background: `var(--r-${MG.ui.ROLE_CLASS[role]})` } }), n);
    }
  }

  function stat(value, label) {
    return h("div", { class: "stat" }, h("b", null, value.toLocaleString("en-US")), h("span", null, label));
  }

  MG.ui.InspectorPanel = InspectorPanel;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/panels/conditions.js ---- */
/* Conditions tab: every minimal independence between recorded nodes, with filters.
 * The list is virtualised (only visible rows are in the DOM). */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { h, icon, statementText, debounce } = MG.ui;

  const H_GROUP = 32, H_ROW = 34, H_ROW2 = 52, H_NEVER = 30;

  class ConditionsPanel {
    constructor(store, app) {
      this.store = store;
      this.app = app;
      this._entries = [];
      this._onScroll = () => this._paint();
    }

    get title() {
      return "Conditions";
    }

    count() {
      return this.store.catalogue().summary().sets;
    }

    shouldRender(changed) {
      return ["modelKey", "edits", "filters"].some((k) => changed.has(k)) || changed.has("statement");
    }

    render(body) {
      const store = this.store;
      const graph = store.graph();
      const cat = store.catalogue(graph);
      const f = store.state.filters;
      const sum = cat.summary();
      this.body = body;
      const prevScroll = this._keepScroll ? body.scrollTop : 0;
      const hadFocus = this.search && document.activeElement === this.search;
      const caret = hadFocus ? this.search.selectionStart : null;

      const search = h("input", { class: "input mono grow", type: "search", placeholder: "Filter: node names or text", value: f.search, "aria-label": "Filter statements" });
      this.search = search;
      search.addEventListener("input", debounce((e) => { this._keepScroll = false; store.setFilters({ search: e.target.value }); }, 120));
      const nodeSel = h("select", { class: "select", "aria-label": "Only pairs involving" },
        h("option", { value: "" }, "All nodes"),
        cat.nodes.map((n) => h("option", { value: n, selected: f.node === n }, n)));
      nodeSel.addEventListener("change", (e) => store.setFilters({ node: e.target.value }));
      const tog = (key, label) => h("label", { class: "toggle" }, h("input", { type: "checkbox", checked: !!f[key], onChange: (e) => store.setFilters({ [key]: e.target.checked }) }), label);

      this._entries = this._build(cat, f);
      const nRows = this._entries.filter((e) => e.type === "row").length;
      const nPairs = this._entries.filter((e) => e.type === "group").length;

      this.list = h("div", { class: "vlist", role: "list" });
      this.inner = h("div", { class: "vlist-inner" });
      this.list.appendChild(this.inner);

      body.replaceChildren(
        h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
          h("h3", null, "All conditions", h("span", { class: "aside" }, graph.name)),
          h("div", { class: "stat-grid" },
            stat(sum.sets, "minimal separating sets"),
            stat(sum.testable, "testable forms"),
            stat(sum.separable, `of ${sum.pairs} pairs separable`),
            stat(sum.adjacent + sum.inseparable, "pairs never independent")),
          h("p", { class: "muted", style: { margin: "10px 0 0", fontSize: "12px" } },
            "Each statement is a minimal set of recorded nodes that d-separates the pair. Testable forms add the indicators needed to check the statement in recorded data.")),
        h("div", { class: "panel-section list-head" },
          h("div", { class: "filters" }, search, nodeSel),
          h("div", { class: "filters", style: { marginTop: "8px" } }, tog("testableOnly", "Testable only"), tog("showNever", "Show never-independent pairs"), tog("hideProxies", "Hide proxies")),
          h("div", { class: "filters", style: { marginTop: "8px", justifyContent: "space-between" } },
            h("span", { class: "muted", style: { fontSize: "12px" } }, `${MG.plural(nRows, "statement")} in ${MG.plural(nPairs, "pair")}`),
            h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => this._csv(cat) }, icon("download"), "CSV"))),
        nRows || nPairs ? this.list : h("div", { class: "empty" }, h("p", null, "Nothing matches these filters."))
      );
      this._layout();
      body.removeEventListener("scroll", this._onScroll);
      body.addEventListener("scroll", this._onScroll, { passive: true });
      body.scrollTop = prevScroll;
      this._keepScroll = true;
      if (hadFocus) {
        search.focus();
        search.setSelectionRange(caret, caret);
      }
      this._paint();
    }

    _build(cat, f) {
      const graph = cat.graph;
      const terms = f.search.trim().toLowerCase().split(/[\s,|]+/).filter(Boolean);
      const out = [];
      for (const p of cat.pairs) {
        if (f.node && p.x !== f.node && p.y !== f.node) continue;
        if (f.hideProxies && (graph.proxies.has(p.x) || graph.proxies.has(p.y))) continue;
        let rows = [];
        if (f.testableOnly) rows = p.testable.map((z) => ({ z, testable: true }));
        else {
          rows = p.separators.map((z) => ({ z, testable: p.isTestableSet(z) }));
          for (const z of p.testable) if (!p.separators.some((s) => MG.setEq(s, z))) rows.push({ z, testable: true, extra: true });
        }
        const x = f.node && p.y === f.node ? p.y : p.x;
        const y = x === p.x ? p.y : p.x;
        rows = rows.map((r) => {
          const st = new MG.Statement([x], [y], r.z);
          const recorded = r.testable ? MG.Statement.recordedForm(graph, st) : null;
          return { ...r, st, recorded, showRecorded: !!recorded && recorded !== st.toString() };
        });
        if (terms.length) rows = rows.filter((r) => matches(terms, r.st.toString() + " " + (r.recorded || "")));
        if (!rows.length) {
          if (f.showNever && !p.separators.length && !terms.length) out.push({ type: "never", pair: p, x, y });
          continue;
        }
        out.push({ type: "group", pair: p, x, y, n: rows.length });
        for (const r of rows) out.push({ type: "row", pair: p, ...r });
      }
      return out;
    }

    _layout() {
      let y = 0;
      for (const e of this._entries) {
        e.top = y;
        e.h = e.type === "group" ? H_GROUP : e.type === "never" ? H_NEVER : e.showRecorded ? H_ROW2 : H_ROW;
        y += e.h;
      }
      this.total = y + 12;
      this.list.style.height = this.total + "px";
    }

    _paint() {
      if (!this.list || !this.list.isConnected) return;
      const body = this.body;
      const top = body.scrollTop - this.list.offsetTop;
      const view = body.clientHeight;
      const lo = top - 300, hi = top + view + 300;
      let i = lowerBound(this._entries, lo);
      const nodes = [];
      for (; i < this._entries.length && this._entries[i].top < hi; i++) nodes.push(this._entry(this._entries[i]));
      this.inner.replaceChildren(...nodes);
    }

    _entry(e) {
      const style = { position: "absolute", top: e.top + "px", left: "8px", right: "8px", height: e.h + "px" };
      if (e.type === "group")
        return h("div", { class: "group-label", style },
          h("button", { class: "chip", onClick: () => this.app.selectNode(e.x) }, e.x), "and",
          h("button", { class: "chip", onClick: () => this.app.selectNode(e.y) }, e.y),
          h("span", { class: "faint", style: { marginLeft: "auto", fontWeight: 500 } }, MG.plural(e.n, "set")));
      if (e.type === "never")
        return h("div", { class: "group-label", style },
          h("span", { class: "mono" }, `${e.x} · ${e.y}`), h("span", { class: "tag never" }, e.pair.adjacent ? "adjacent" : "never separable"));
      const text = e.st.toString();
      const active = this.store.state.statement === text;
      return h("div", { class: ["stmt-row", active ? "is-active" : null], style, role: "listitem", tabindex: 0, title: e.recorded ? `${text}\nrecorded: ${e.recorded}` : text,
          onClick: () => this.app.showStatement(text, { stay: true }),
          onKeydown: (ev) => ev.key === "Enter" && this.app.showStatement(text, { stay: true }) },
        h("div", { style: { minWidth: 0 } }, statementText(e.st), e.showRecorded ? h("div", { class: "sub" }, e.recorded) : null),
        h("div", { class: "row-tools" },
          e.testable ? h("span", { class: "tag testable" }, e.extra ? "testable form" : "testable") : null,
          h("button", { class: "row-btn", title: "Copy", "aria-label": "Copy statement", onClick: (ev) => { ev.stopPropagation(); MG.ui.copyText(text); } }, icon("copy")),
          h("button", { class: "row-btn", title: "Open in Query", "aria-label": "Open in Query", onClick: (ev) => { ev.stopPropagation(); this.app.showStatement(text); } }, icon("arrow"))));
    }

    _csv(cat) {
      const lines = ["x,y,given,statement,testable,recorded_form"];
      for (const e of this._entries) if (e.type === "row")
        lines.push([[...e.st.x][0], [...e.st.y][0], MG.sorted(e.st.z).join(", "), e.st.toString(), e.testable ? "yes" : "no", e.recorded || ""].map(csvCell).join(","));
      MG.ui.download(`${slug(cat.graph.name)}-conditions.csv`, lines.join("\n") + "\n", "text/csv");
      MG.ui.toast(`Saved ${MG.plural(lines.length - 1, "statement")}`);
    }
  }

  function matches(terms, text) {
    const t = text.toLowerCase();
    return terms.every((term) => t.includes(term));
  }

  function lowerBound(entries, y) {
    let lo = 0, hi = entries.length;
    while (lo < hi) {
      const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1;
      if (entries[mid].top + entries[mid].h < y) lo = mid + 1;
      else hi = mid;
    }
    return lo;
  }

  function csvCell(v) {
    const s = String(v);
    return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? `"${s.replace(/"/g, '""')}"` : s;
  }

  function slug(s) {
    return String(s).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-").replace(/^-|-$/g, "") || "graph";
  }

  function stat(value, label) {
