    return h("div", { class: "stat" }, h("b", null, value.toLocaleString("en-US")), h("span", null, label));
  }

  MG.ui.ConditionsPanel = ConditionsPanel;
  MG.ui.slug = slug;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/panels/query.js ---- */
/* Query tab: check any statement X _||_ Y | Z, see why it holds or fails, and compare models. */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { h, icon, debounce } = MG.ui;

  const SLOT_LABEL = { x: "X", y: "Y", z: "Z" };

  class QueryPanel {
    constructor(store, app) {
      this.store = store;
      this.app = app;
      this._typing = false;
    }

    get title() {
      return "Query";
    }

    shouldRender(changed) {
      return ["statement", "modelKey", "edits"].some((k) => changed.has(k)) || changed.has("t:pick") || changed.has("t:pathTab");
    }

    render(body) {
      this.body = body;
      if (!this.input || !this.input.isConnected) {
        this.input = h("input", { class: "input mono", style: { width: "100%" }, spellcheck: false, autocomplete: "off", placeholder: "Yk _||_ R_Yk | S, W, T", "aria-label": "Independence statement" });
        this.input.addEventListener("input", debounce((e) => {
          this._typing = true;
          this.store.set({ statement: e.target.value.trim() || null });
          this._typing = false;
        }, 160));
        this.details = h("div");
        body.replaceChildren(
          h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
            h("h3", null, "Statement", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "X _||_ Y | Z   ·   ⊥⊥ also works")),
            this.input,
            this.slots = h("div", { class: "slots", style: { marginTop: "10px" } })),
          this.details);
      }
      if (document.activeElement !== this.input) this.input.value = this.store.state.statement || "";
      this._slots();
      this._details();
    }

    _current() {
      const a = this.store.analysis();
      if (a && a.statement) return a.statement;
      if (a && a.text) {
        try {
          return MG.Statement.parse(a.text);
        } catch (e) {
          return null;
        }
      }
      return null;
    }

    _slots() {
      const st = this._current() || new MG.Statement([], [], []);
      const pick = this.store.transient.pick;
      const row = (slot) => {
        const members = MG.sorted(st[slot]);
        return h("div", { class: "slot" },
          h("span", { class: ["slot-label", slot === "z" ? "z" : null] }, SLOT_LABEL[slot]),
          h("div", { class: "chip-row" },
            members.length ? members.map((n) => h("button", { class: "chip", title: `Remove ${n}`, onClick: () => this.app.toggleInSlot(slot, n) }, n, h("span", { class: "x" }, "×")))
              : h("span", { class: "placeholder" }, slot === "z" ? "nothing conditioned on" : "empty")),
          h("button", { class: "btn small pick", "aria-pressed": String(pick === slot), title: `Click nodes on the graph to add them to ${SLOT_LABEL[slot]}`, onClick: () => this.store.setTransient({ pick: pick === slot ? null : slot }) },
            pick === slot ? "Picking…" : "Pick"));
      };
      this.slots.replaceChildren(row("x"), row("y"), row("z"));
    }

    _details() {
      const a = this.store.analysis();
      const graph = this.store.graph();
      if (!a) return this.details.replaceChildren(this._examples(graph));
      if (a.error)
        return this.details.replaceChildren(h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("div", { class: "verdict err" }, h("b", null, "Can't check this yet"), h("span", { class: "why" }, a.error))));
      const st = a.statement;
      const nOpen = a.open.paths.length;
      const verdict = h("div", { class: ["verdict", a.implied ? "yes" : "no"] },
        h("span", { class: "mark" }, icon(a.implied ? "check" : "cross")),
        h("b", null, a.implied ? "Implied by the graph" : "Not implied by the graph"),
        h("span", { class: "why" }, a.implied ? `Every path between ${list(st.x)} and ${list(st.y)} is blocked given ${st.z.size ? list(st.z) : "nothing"}.`
          : `${MG.plural(nOpen, "open path")}${a.open.truncated ? " (first 200 shown)" : ""} connect${nOpen === 1 ? "s" : ""} them; shown in orange on the graph.`));
      const test = h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("h3", null, "Testability"),
        a.testable
          ? h("div", null, h("div", { class: "stmt-row", style: { cursor: "default", padding: "4px 0" } },
              h("div", null, h("div", { class: "mono" }, a.recorded), h("div", { class: "muted", style: { fontSize: "12px", marginTop: "3px" } }, "Checkable on recorded data: proxies stand in for the variables, among records where they are observed.")),
              h("div", { class: "row-tools" }, h("span", { class: "tag testable" }, "testable"), h("button", { class: "row-btn", style: { opacity: 1 }, title: "Copy", "aria-label": "Copy recorded form", onClick: () => MG.ui.copyText(a.recorded) }, icon("copy")))))
          : h("div", { class: "muted" }, h("span", { class: "tag never" }, "not testable"), " ", a.whyNot, "."));
      const sections = [h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, verdict, h("div", { class: "form-row" },
        h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => MG.ui.copyText(st.toString()) }, icon("copy"), "Copy"),
        h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => this.app.view.fit(true, [...st.x, ...st.y, ...st.z]) }, icon("fit"), "Zoom to statement"),
        h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => this.app.exporter.open("svg") }, icon("download"), "Export figure"),
        h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => this.store.set({ statement: null }) }, "Clear"))), test];
      sections.push(this._compare());
      if (a.pair) sections.push(this._separators(graph, a));
      sections.push(this._paths(a));
      this.details.replaceChildren(...sections);
    }

    _compare() {
      const rows = this.store.compare();
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("h3", null, "Across models", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "click to switch")),
        h("div", { class: "compare" }, rows.map((r) =>
          h("button", { class: r.key === this.store.state.modelKey ? "is-current" : null, title: r.error || r.name, onClick: () => this.app.switchModel(r.key) },
            h("span", null, h("b", null, r.family || r.name), " ", h("span", { class: "faint" }, r.upstream === 1 ? "single" : r.upstream ? `${r.upstream} up` : "")),
            h("span", { class: ["res", r.implied === null ? "na" : r.implied ? "yes" : "no"] }, r.implied === null ? "n/a" : r.implied ? "implied" : "not")))));
    }

    _separators(graph, a) {
      const p = a.pair;
      const [x] = [...a.statement.x], [y] = [...a.statement.y];
      const setChip = (z, testable) => {
        const st = new MG.Statement([x], [y], z);
        const active = MG.setEq(z, a.statement.z);
        return h("button", { class: "chip", style: active ? { borderColor: "var(--accent)", background: "var(--accent-soft)" } : null, title: testable ? MG.Statement.recordedForm(graph, st) : st.toString(), onClick: () => this.app.showStatement(st.toString(), { stay: true }) },
          z.size ? MG.sorted(z).join(", ") : "∅ (marginal)", testable ? h("span", { class: "tag testable", style: { marginLeft: "4px", height: "16px" } }, "T") : null);
      };
      const extra = p.testable.filter((z) => !p.separators.some((s) => MG.setEq(s, z)));
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("h3", null, `Minimal sets separating ${x} and ${y}`),
        p.separators.length ? h("div", { class: "chip-row" }, p.separators.map((z) => setChip(z, p.isTestableSet(z)))) : h("p", { class: "muted", style: { margin: 0 } }, p.reason + "."),
        extra.length ? [h("div", { class: "muted", style: { fontSize: "12px", margin: "10px 0 5px" } }, "Testable forms (indicators added):"), h("div", { class: "chip-row" }, extra.map((z) => setChip(z, true)))] : null);
    }

    _paths(a) {
      const tab = this.store.transient.pathTab;
      const open = a.open;
      const blocked = tab === "blocked" ? a.blocked() : a._blocked;
      const list = tab === "open" ? open : blocked;
      const rows = list.paths.slice(0, 300).map((p) =>
        h("div", { class: ["path-row", p.open ? "open" : null],
            onMouseenter: () => this.store.setTransient({ focusPath: p }),
            onMouseleave: () => this.store.setTransient({ focusPath: null }) },
          h("span", { class: "len" }, String(p.path.length - 1)),
          h("span", { class: "p" }, MG.dsep.formatPath(p)),
          h("span", { class: "r" }, p.open ? "open" : p.reason)));
      const sub = h("div", { class: "subtabs" },
        h("button", { "aria-pressed": String(tab === "open"), onClick: () => this.store.setTransient({ pathTab: "open" }) }, `Open ${open.paths.length}${open.truncated ? "+" : ""}`),
        h("button", { "aria-pressed": String(tab === "blocked"), onClick: () => this.store.setTransient({ pathTab: "blocked" }) }, blocked ? `Blocked ${blocked.paths.length}${blocked.truncated ? "+" : ""}` : "Blocked"));
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("h3", null, "Paths", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "hover to trace on the graph")),
        sub,
        h("div", { style: { marginTop: "8px" } }, rows.length ? rows : h("p", { class: "muted", style: { margin: "4px 0" } }, tab === "open" ? "No open paths: the statement holds." : "No blocked paths up to length 9.")),
        tab === "blocked" && blocked && blocked.truncated ? h("p", { class: "faint", style: { fontSize: "12px" } }, "Blocked paths are listed up to length 9 and 400 paths.") : null);
    }

    _examples(graph) {
      const up = graph.ids.filter((n) => /^Yj\d*$/.test(n)).sort();
      const ex = [];
      if (graph.has("Yk") && up.length) ex.push(`Yk _||_ R_Yk | ${up.join(", ")}, S, W, T`);
      if (up.length) ex.push(`${up[0]} _||_ R_${up[0]} | S, W, T`);
      if (up.length > 1) ex.push(`${up[0]} _||_ R_${up[1]} | S, W, T, R_${up[0]}`);
      if (graph.has("Yk")) ex.push("Yk _||_ R_Yk | S, W, T");
      return h("div", { class: "empty" },
        h("p", null, "Type a statement above, use Pick to choose nodes on the graph, or start from one of these:"),
        h("div", { style: { display: "grid", gap: "4px" } }, ex.filter((t) => { try { MG.Statement.parse(t).check(graph); return true; } catch (e) { return false; } })
          .map((t) => h("button", { class: "chip", style: { justifyContent: "flex-start", height: "auto", padding: "6px 10px", borderRadius: "7px" }, onClick: () => this.app.showStatement(t) }, t))));
    }
  }

  function list(set) {
    return MG.sorted(set).join(", ");
  }

  MG.ui.QueryPanel = QueryPanel;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/panels/missing.js ---- */
/* Missing data tab (Mohan & Pearl 2021): the mechanism, recovering any distribution with its
 * derivation, and what the graph lets data test. */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { h, icon } = MG.ui;
  const M = () => MG.ui.math;

  const METHOD = {
    direct: { label: "directly estimable", cite: "every variable is fully observed" },
    "Corollary 2(a)": { label: "complete cases", cite: "Corollary 2(a)" },
    "Corollary 2(b)": { label: "available cases", cite: "Corollary 2(b)" },
    "Theorem 1": { label: "ordered factorisation", cite: "Theorem 1, Definition 2" },
    "R-factorisation": { label: "R-factorisation", cite: "Section 3.2, Theorem 2" },
    intervention: { label: "intervention on the indicators", cite: "Section 3.3, ID algorithm" },
    ratio: { label: "ratio of recoverable distributions", cite: "P(T | S) = P(T, S) / P(S)" },
    "Theorem 3": { label: "not recoverable", cite: "Theorem 3" },
  };

  class MissingPanel {
    constructor(store, app) {
      this.store = store;
      this.app = app;
      this.query = { target: [], given: [] };
      this._key = null;
    }

    get title() {
      return "Missing data";
    }

    shouldRender(changed) {
      return ["modelKey", "edits", "statement"].some((k) => changed.has(k));
    }

    render(body) {
      const graph = this.store.graph();
      if (this._key !== this.store.state.modelKey + graph.ids.join()) {
        this._key = this.store.state.modelKey + graph.ids.join();
        const Vm = MG.sorted(graph.partiallyObserved);
        const q = this.store.state.missingQuery;
        const ok = q && [...q.target, ...q.given].every((v) => graph.has(v));
        this.query = ok ? { target: q.target.slice(), given: q.given.slice() } : { target: Vm.length ? [Vm[Vm.length - 1]] : [], given: [] };
      }
      if (!graph.indicators.size) {
        body.replaceChildren(h("div", { class: "empty" },
          h("p", null, h("b", null, "No partially observed variables. "), "Every variable in this graph is either fully observed or latent, so there is no missing data to analyse."),
          h("p", null, "To model missingness, select a variable in the Node tab and switch on ", h("b", null, "Partially observed"), ": its indicator R_X (1 = missing) and recorded proxy X* are added. Then draw arrows into R_X from whatever causes the missingness."),
          h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => this.app.openGallery("mp-fig1c") }, "See an example: Fig. 1(c)")));
        return;
      }
      body.replaceChildren(this._mechanism(graph), this._recover(graph), this._joint(graph), this._testability(graph), this._learn());
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- mechanism

    _mechanism(graph) {
      const c = MG.missing.classify(graph);
      const tone = c.mechanism === "MNAR" ? "no" : "yes";
      const witness = c.mechanism === "MCAR" ? null : c.mechanism === "v-MAR" ? c.mcarWitness : c.vmarWitness;
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("h3", null, "Missingness mechanism", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "Section 2.2")),
        h("div", { class: ["verdict", tone] },
          h("span", { class: "mark" }, icon(tone === "yes" ? "check" : "cross")),
          h("b", null, c.mechanism),
          h("span", { class: "why" }, c.text)),
        h("div", { class: "kv", style: { marginTop: "10px" } },
          h("dt", null, "MCAR"), h("dd", null, this._stmt(c.mcarStatement, c.mcar)),
          h("dt", null, "v-MAR"), h("dd", null, this._stmt(c.vmarStatement, c.vmar))),
        witness ? h("div", { class: "path-row open", onMouseenter: () => this.store.setTransient({ focusPath: witness }), onMouseleave: () => this.store.setTransient({ focusPath: null }) },
          h("span", { class: "p" }, MG.dsep.formatPath(witness)), h("span", { class: "r" }, c.mechanism === "v-MAR" ? "why not MCAR" : "why not v-MAR")) : null);
    }

    _stmt(st, holds) {
      return h("span", { class: "chip-row", style: { alignItems: "center" } },
        h("span", { class: ["tag", holds ? "testable" : "never"] }, holds ? "holds" : "fails"),
        h("button", { class: "linkish mono", title: "Show on the graph", onClick: () => this.app.showStatement(st.toString(), { stay: true }) }, st.pretty()));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- recover a query

    _recover(graph) {
      const V = MG.sorted([...graph.fullyObserved, ...graph.partiallyObserved]);
      const Vm = MG.sorted(graph.partiallyObserved);
      const q = this.query;
      const set = (patch) => {
        this.query = { ...this.query, ...patch };
        this.store.state.missingQuery = this.query;
        this.render(this.app.body);
      };
      const toggle = (slot, v) => {
        const other = slot === "target" ? "given" : "target";
        const list = q[slot].includes(v) ? q[slot].filter((x) => x !== v) : [...q[slot], v];
        set({ [slot]: MG.sorted(list), [other]: q[other].filter((x) => x !== v) });
      };
      const chips = (slot) => h("div", { class: "chip-row" }, V.map((v) =>
        h("button", { class: ["chip", q[slot].includes(v) ? "on" : null], "aria-pressed": String(q[slot].includes(v)), onClick: () => toggle(slot, v) },
          h("span", { class: "dot", style: { background: `var(--r-${MG.ui.ROLE_CLASS[graph.role(v)]})` } }), v)));
      const presets = h("div", { class: "filters" },
        h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => set({ target: V, given: [] }) }, "Joint P(V)"),
        ...Vm.slice(0, 6).map((v) => h("button", { class: "btn small", onClick: () => set({ target: [v], given: [] }) }, `P(${v})`)));
      let result;
      if (!q.target.length) result = h("p", { class: "muted" }, "Choose at least one variable to recover.");
      else {
        try {
          result = this._result(graph, MG.missing.recover(graph, q.target, q.given));
        } catch (e) {
          result = h("div", { class: "verdict err" }, h("b", null, "Can't ask this"), h("span", { class: "why" }, e.message));
        }
      }
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("h3", null, "Recover a distribution", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "Section 3")),
        h("p", { class: "muted small" }, "Can this distribution be estimated consistently from the incomplete data, as if nothing were missing?"),
        h("div", { class: "slot-label-row" }, "Distribution of"), chips("target"),
        h("div", { class: "slot-label-row" }, "given"), chips("given"),
        presets,
        h("div", { style: { marginTop: "12px" } }, result));
    }

    _result(graph, r) {
      const meta = METHOD[r.method] || { label: r.method, cite: "" };
      const q = () => M().query(r.target, r.given); // a fresh node for each place it appears
      if (r.recoverable === false) {
        return h("div", null,
          h("div", { class: "verdict no" }, h("span", { class: "mark" }, icon("cross")), h("b", null, ["Not recoverable: ", q()]), h("span", { class: "why" }, r.reason)),
          this._proofPath(graph, r));
      }
      if (r.recoverable !== true) {
        return h("div", { class: "verdict err" }, h("b", null, ["Undetermined: ", q()]), h("span", { class: "why" }, r.reason));
      }
      return h("div", null,
        h("div", { class: "verdict yes" }, h("span", { class: "mark" }, icon("check")), h("b", null, ["Recoverable: ", q()]), h("span", { class: "why" }, `By ${meta.label} (${meta.cite}). ${r.reason}`)),
        M().formula(r.expr, { lhs: q() }),
        this._derivation(graph, r));
    }

    _proofPath(graph, r) {
      if (!r.proof || !r.proof.path) return null;
      return h("p", { class: "muted small" }, "The path ", h("span", { class: "mono" }, r.proof.path.join(" ⋯ ")), " joins the variable to its own indicator through colliders that the query conditions on.");
    }

    /** The steps behind the answer, each independence clickable. */
    _derivation(graph, r) {
      const steps = [];
      const add = (title, statements, note) => steps.push({ title, statements: statements.filter(Boolean), note });
      if (r.method === "Corollary 2(a)") add("All records that are complete", [r.corollary2.complete.statement], "The target is independent of every missingness indicator given the conditioning set.");
      if (r.method === "Corollary 2(b)") add("Records where the query's variables are observed", [r.corollary2.available.statement], "The target is independent of the indicators of the query's variables given the conditioning set.");
      if (r.factorisation) {
        const f = r.factorisation;
        if (f.helpers.length) add(`Sum over ${f.helpers.join(", ")}`, [], "Theorem 1 allows a sum of ordered factorisations.");
        for (const s of f.steps) {
          const given = s.given.length ? ` | ${s.given.join(", ")}` : "";
          add(`Factor P(${s.variable}${given})`, [s.validity, s.recoverability],
            s.sub ? `Recovered on its own by ${METHOD[s.sub.method] ? METHOD[s.sub.method].label : s.sub.method}.` : s.recoverability ? "The first independence makes it the right factor; the second lets it be computed where the needed variables are observed." : "The first independence makes it the right factor; its variables are fully observed.");
        }
      }
      if (r.rfactorisation) {
        add("P(V) = P(V*, R = 0) / P(R = 0 | V)", [], "Then P(R = 0 | V) is a product over the indicators, each simplified as follows.");
        for (const f of r.rfactorisation.factors) add(`P(${f.indicator} = 0 | ${[...f.given, ...f.zeros.map((z) => z + " = 0")].join(", ") || "∅"})`, [f.drop, f.add], null);
      }
      if (r.trace && r.trace.length) for (const t of r.trace) add(`ID line ${t.line}`, [], t.text);
      if (r.parts) add("Parts", [], r.parts.map((p) => `${p.query}: ${p.method}`).join("; "));
      if (!steps.length) return null;
      return h("details", { class: "derivation", open: steps.length <= 6 },
        h("summary", null, `Derivation (${steps.length} step${steps.length === 1 ? "" : "s"})`),
        h("ol", { class: "steps" }, steps.map((s) => h("li", null,
          h("div", { class: "step-title" }, s.title),
          s.statements.map((st) => h("button", { class: "linkish mono", title: "Show this independence on the graph", onClick: () => this.app.showStatement(st.toString(), { stay: true }) }, "uses ", st.pretty())),
          s.note ? h("div", { class: "muted small" }, s.note) : null))));
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Theorem 2

    _joint(graph) {
      const t2 = MG.missing.theorem2(graph);
      let body;
      if (!t2.applies)
        body = h("p", { class: "muted small", style: { margin: 0 } }, `Theorem 2 assumes no edges between indicators; this graph has ${t2.edgesBetweenIndicators.map(([a, b]) => `${a}–${b}`).join(", ")} (an arrow or a shared unrecorded cause). Its verdict does not apply here; the methods above still do.`);
      else if (!t2.violations.length) body = h("div", { class: "problem ok" }, "The joint distribution is recoverable: no variable is a neighbour of its own indicator or joined to it by a path of colliders.");
      else body = h("div", null, t2.violations.map((v) => h("div", { class: "problem" }, `Condition ${v.condition} fails: ${v.text}.`)), h("p", { class: "muted small" }, "So the joint distribution P(V) is not recoverable."));
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("h3", null, "The joint distribution", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "Theorem 2")), body);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Section 4

    _testability(graph) {
      const t = MG.missing.testability(graph, this.store.catalogue(graph));
      const nTestable = this.store.catalogue(graph).summary().testable;
      const row = (st, tag) => h("div", { class: "stmt-row", onClick: () => this.app.showStatement(st.toString(), { stay: true }) },
        h("div", null, MG.ui.statementText(st), MG.Statement.testable(graph, st) ? h("div", { class: "sub" }, MG.Statement.recordedForm(graph, st)) : null), tag);
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("h3", null, "What data can test", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "Section 4")),
        h("p", { class: "muted small" }, `The graph implies ${nTestable} testable independences (Theorem 5); see Conditions with "Testable only".`),
        t.untestable.length ? [h("div", { class: "slot-label-row" }, "Claims no data can refute"), ...t.untestable.map((u) => row(u.statement, h("span", { class: "tag never" }, u.theorem4 ? "Theorem 4" : "untestable")))] : null,
        h("div", { class: "slot-label-row" }, "Is the mechanism itself testable?"),
        h("div", { class: ["problem", t.vmar.testable ? "ok" : ""].join(" ") }, t.vmar.text),
        h("div", { class: ["problem", t.mcar.testable ? "ok" : ""].join(" ") }, t.mcar.text),
        t.vmar.testable ? h("details", null, h("summary", null, "Tests implied by v-MAR (Example 13)"), t.vmar.tests.map((s) => row(s, MG.dsep.implies(graph, s) ? h("span", { class: "tag testable" }, "graph agrees") : h("span", { class: "tag never" }, "graph disagrees")))) : null);
    }

    _learn() {
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("h3", null, "How to read this"),
        h("details", null, h("summary", null, "m-graphs"), h("p", { class: "small" }, "Each partially observed variable X has an indicator R_X (1 = missing) and a recorded proxy X*, equal to X when R_X = 0 and missing otherwise (Equation 1). Arrows into R_X say what causes X to be missing.")),
        h("details", null, h("summary", null, "Recoverable"), h("p", { class: "small" }, "A quantity is recoverable if it can be estimated consistently from the incomplete data for every strictly positive observed-data distribution the graph allows (Definition 1). The answer depends on the graph, not on the data.")),
        h("details", null, h("summary", null, "Undetermined"), h("p", { class: "small" }, "No criterion of the paper recovers the query and none proves it impossible. That is not a proof either way; the paper's Section 3.4 describes recoveries that use features of the data beyond the graph.")),
        h("details", null, h("summary", null, "Checking these answers"), h("p", { class: "small" }, "Every formula here was produced from d-separations the graph implies. The test suite also evaluates such formulas on random models of each graph and compares them with the truth.")));
    }
  }

  MG.ui.MissingPanel = MissingPanel;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/panels/causal.js ---- */
/* Causal effect tab: P(Y | do(X)) or P(Y | do(X), Z). Identification by the ID algorithm with its
 * derivation, adjustment sets, front-door and instruments, causal and back-door paths, and
 * Section 3.5 of Mohan & Pearl (2021) when variables are partially observed. */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { h, icon } = MG.ui;
  const M = () => MG.ui.math;

  class CausalPanel {
    constructor(store, app) {
      this.store = store;
      this.app = app;
      this.q = { X: [], Y: [], Z: [] };
      this._key = null;
    }

    get title() {
      return "Causal effect";
    }

    shouldRender(changed) {
      return ["modelKey", "edits"].some((k) => changed.has(k)) || changed.has("t:pick");
    }

    _defaults(graph) {
      const ids = graph.ids.filter((v) => !graph.latent.has(v) && !graph.indicators.has(v) && !graph.proxies.has(v));
      const meta = (v) => graph.meta.get(v) || {};
      let X = ids.filter((v) => meta(v).exposure), Y = ids.filter((v) => meta(v).outcome);
      if (!X.length || !Y.length) {
        // a pair with a directed path, preferring a root and a sink
        const order = MG.ADMG.project(graph).topological().filter((v) => ids.includes(v));
        for (const a of order) {
          const b = [...order].reverse().find((c) => c !== a && graph.descendants(a).has(c));
          if (b) {
            if (!X.length) X = [a];
            if (!Y.length) Y = [b];
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      return { X, Y: Y.filter((v) => !X.includes(v)), Z: [] };
    }

    render(body) {
      const graph = this.store.graph();
      const k = this.store.state.modelKey + graph.ids.join();
      if (this._key !== k) {
        this._key = k;
        const saved = this.store.state.causalQuery;
        this.q = saved && [...saved.X, ...saved.Y, ...saved.Z].every((v) => graph.has(v)) ? saved : this._defaults(graph);
      }
      body.replaceChildren(this._builder(graph), ...this._results(graph));
      this._emphasise();
    }

    _emphasise() {
      const { X, Y, Z } = this.q;
      const z = this._shown || new Set(Z);
      this.store.setTransient({ emphasis: X.length || Y.length ? { x: new Set(X), y: new Set(Y), z } : null });
    }

    _set(patch) {
      this._shown = null;
      this.q = { ...this.q, ...patch };
      this.store.state.causalQuery = this.q;
      this.render(this.app.body);
    }

    _builder(graph) {
      const ids = MG.sorted(graph.ids.filter((v) => !graph.latent.has(v) && !graph.indicators.has(v) && !graph.proxies.has(v)));
      const pick = this.store.transient.pick;
      const slot = (key, label, cls) => {
        const members = this.q[key];
        return h("div", { class: "slot" },
          h("span", { class: ["slot-label", cls] }, label),
          h("div", { class: "chip-row" }, members.length ? members.map((n) => h("button", { class: "chip", onClick: () => this._set({ [key]: members.filter((m) => m !== n) }) }, n, h("span", { class: "x" }, "×"))) : h("span", { class: "placeholder" }, key === "Z" ? "nothing (optional)" : "choose")),
          h("select", { class: "select", "aria-label": `Add to ${label}`, onChange: (e) => {
              const v = e.target.value;
              if (!v) return;
              const others = Object.fromEntries(["X", "Y", "Z"].filter((s) => s !== key).map((s) => [s, this.q[s].filter((m) => m !== v)]));
              this._set({ ...others, [key]: MG.sorted([...members, v]) });
            } }, h("option", { value: "" }, "add…"), ids.filter((v) => !members.includes(v)).map((v) => h("option", { value: v }, v))));
      };
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" },
        h("h3", null, "Causal question", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "do-calculus, ID algorithm")),
        h("div", { class: "slots" }, slot("X", "do", "do"), slot("Y", "Y", ""), slot("Z", "Z", "z")),
        h("p", { class: "muted small", style: { margin: "8px 0 0" } }, "do = treatments set by intervention, Y = outcomes, Z = optional observed conditions. Mark Exposure and Outcome on nodes in the Node tab to make them the default."));
    }

    _results(graph) {
      const { X, Y, Z } = this.q;
      if (!X.length || !Y.length) return [h("div", { class: "empty" }, h("p", null, "Choose a treatment and an outcome."))];
      let r;
      try {
        r = MG.causal.effect(graph, X, Y, Z);
      } catch (e) {
        return [h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("div", { class: "verdict err" }, h("b", null, "Can't ask this"), h("span", { class: "why" }, e.message)))];
      }
      const q = () => M().query(Y, Z, X); // a fresh node for each place it appears
      const out = [];
      // verdict
      if (r.recoverable === true) {
        const how = r.missing ? `Identifiable, and recoverable from the incomplete data by ${r.method} (Mohan & Pearl 2021, Section 3.5).` : "Identifiable: the ID algorithm expresses it in observational probabilities.";
        out.push(h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("div", { class: "verdict yes" }, h("span", { class: "mark" }, icon("check")), h("b", null, ["Identifiable: ", q()]), h("span", { class: "why" }, how)), M().formula(r.expr, { lhs: q() })));
      } else if (r.identifiable === false) {
        out.push(h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("div", { class: "verdict no" }, h("span", { class: "mark" }, icon("cross")), h("b", null, ["Not identifiable: ", q()]),
          h("span", { class: "why" }, `No formula in observational probabilities exists. The ID algorithm found a hedge: {${MG.sorted(r.hedge.F).join(", ")}} and {${MG.sorted(r.hedge.Fprime).join(", ")}} are each joined by unrecorded common causes${r.missing ? "; so it cannot be recovered under missingness either (Section 3.5)" : ""}.`)),
          h("button", { class: "btn small", style: { marginTop: "8px" }, onClick: () => this.app.view.fit(true, [...r.hedge.F]) }, "Show the hedge")));
      } else {
        out.push(h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("div", { class: "verdict err" }, h("b", null, ["Identifiable, recovery undetermined: ", q()]), h("span", { class: "why" }, r.reason || "")),
          r.idExpr ? [h("p", { class: "muted small" }, "Without missing data it would be:"), M().formula(r.idExpr, { lhs: q() })] : null));
      }
      // ID derivation
      if (r.trace && r.trace.length)
        out.push(h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("details", { class: "derivation" }, h("summary", null, `How the ID algorithm decides (${r.trace.length} step${r.trace.length === 1 ? "" : "s"})`),
          h("ol", { class: "steps" }, r.trace.map((t) => h("li", { style: { marginLeft: `${Math.min(t.depth, 6) * 10}px` } }, h("div", { class: "step-title" }, typeof t.line === "number" ? `Line ${t.line}` : t.line), h("div", { class: "small" }, t.text)))))));
      if (r.missing && r.factorResults)
        out.push(h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("h3", null, "Each factor, recovered"), r.factorResults.map((p) => h("div", { class: "stmt-row", style: { cursor: "default" } }, h("div", null, h("span", { class: "mono" }, p.query), h("div", { class: "muted small" }, p.method))))));
      // adjustment, front-door, instruments
      if (r.adjustment) out.push(this._adjustment(graph, r));
      if (r.frontDoor && r.frontDoor.length) out.push(this._frontDoor(r));
      if (r.instruments && r.instruments.length) out.push(this._instruments(r));
      if (r.paths) out.push(this._paths(r));
      if (r.missing) out.push(h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("p", { class: "muted small", style: { margin: 0 } }, "Adjustment sets and paths are listed for graphs without partially observed variables. Here the effect is first identified in the substantive model (indicators and proxies removed), then recovered from the incomplete data.")));
      return out;
    }

    _adjustment(graph, r) {
      const a = r.adjustment;
      const { X, Y } = this.q;
      const body = [];
      if (!a.exists) body.push(h("p", { class: "muted small", style: { margin: 0 } }, "No set of observed variables satisfies the adjustment criterion: some non-causal path cannot be blocked without blocking or distorting the effect."));
      else {
        body.push(h("p", { class: "muted small", style: { marginTop: 0 } }, "Each set blocks every non-causal path and contains no descendant of a mediator (generalised adjustment criterion). Click one to see the formula."));
        body.push(h("div", { class: "chip-row" }, a.minimal.map((Z) => h("button", { class: "chip", onClick: () => this._showSet(Z) }, Z.size ? MG.sorted(Z).join(", ") : "∅ (no adjustment needed)"))));
        if (a.optimal) body.push(h("div", { class: "problem ok" }, h("b", null, "Optimal set: "), MG.sorted(a.optimal).join(", ") || "∅", " — the valid set with the smallest asymptotic variance (Henckel et al. 2022)."));
        if (this._shown) body.push(h("div", { style: { marginTop: "10px" } }, M().formula(MG.causal.adjustmentFormula(X, Y, this._shown), { lhs: M().query(Y, [], X) })));
      }
      const custom = h("input", { class: "input mono grow", placeholder: "Check your own set, e.g. A, B", "aria-label": "Adjustment set to check" });
      const verdict = h("span", { class: "small" });
      custom.addEventListener("input", () => {
        const Z = custom.value.split(/[,\s]+/).filter(Boolean);
        if (!Z.length) return (verdict.textContent = "");
        const unknown = Z.filter((z) => !graph.has(z));
        if (unknown.length) return (verdict.textContent = `unknown: ${unknown.join(", ")}`);
        const bad = Z.filter((z) => a.forbidden.has(z) || graph.latent.has(z));
        verdict.textContent = bad.length ? `not valid: ${bad.join(", ")} ${bad.some((b) => graph.latent.has(b)) ? "is latent or " : ""}is a descendant of a mediator or a treatment` : a.isValid(new Set(Z)) ? "valid adjustment set" : "not valid: a non-causal path stays open";
        verdict.style.color = a.isValid(new Set(Z)) ? "var(--implied)" : "var(--not)";
      });
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("h3", null, "Adjustment sets", h("span", { class: "aside" }, a.exists ? `${a.minimal.length} minimal` : "none")), body, h("div", { class: "form-row" }, custom, verdict));
    }

    /** Show an adjustment set's formula, with the set drawn as conditioned on the graph. */
    _showSet(Z) {
      this._shown = new Set(Z);
      this.render(this.app.body);
    }

    _frontDoor(r) {
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("h3", null, "Front-door", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "Pearl 1995")),
        h("p", { class: "muted small", style: { marginTop: 0 } }, "These sets carry the whole effect, have no open back-door path from the treatment, and their back-door paths to the outcome are blocked by the treatment."),
        r.frontDoor.map((Mset) => M().formula(MG.expr.present(MG.causal.frontDoorFormula(r.X[0], r.Y[0], Mset)), { lhs: `via {${MG.sorted(Mset).join(", ")}}` })));
    }

    _instruments(r) {
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("h3", null, "Instrumental variables"),
        h("p", { class: "muted small", style: { marginTop: 0 } }, "An instrument affects the outcome only through the treatment. Instruments identify the effect only with extra assumptions (for example linear effects, or bounds); the graph alone does not."),
        h("div", { class: "chip-row" }, r.instruments.map((iv) => h("span", { class: "chip static" }, iv.z, iv.given.length ? ` given ${iv.given.join(", ")}` : ""))));
    }

    _paths(r) {
      const row = (p, kind) => h("div", { class: ["path-row", kind === "causal" ? "open" : null], onMouseenter: () => this.store.setTransient({ focusPath: p }), onMouseleave: () => this.store.setTransient({ focusPath: null }) },
        h("span", { class: "len" }, String(p.path.length - 1)), h("span", { class: "p" }, MG.dsep.formatPath(p)), h("span", { class: "r" }, kind));
      const { causal, backdoor } = r.paths;
      return h("div", { class: "panel-section" }, h("h3", null, "Paths", h("span", { class: "aside" }, "hover to trace")),
        causal.length ? causal.slice(0, 40).map((p) => row(p, "causal")) : h("p", { class: "muted small" }, "No causal path: the treatment cannot affect the outcome."),
        backdoor.slice(0, 40).map((p) => row(p, "back-door")));
    }
  }

  MG.ui.CausalPanel = CausalPanel;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/exporter.js ---- */
/* Export: figures (SVG, PNG, PDF), a standalone HTML snapshot, and DAGitty text.
 *
 * Figures are built from a scene (MG.scene) of the current graph, layout and highlighted
 * statement, always on white so they drop into a paper. PDF is vector, written in the
 * browser with the built-in Helvetica fonts. The HTML snapshot is this page with the current
 * state (model, edits, layout, statement, filters) saved inside it.
 */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { h, icon, download, toast, slug } = MG.ui;

  const FORMATS = {
    svg: { label: "SVG", note: "vector, editable" },
    png: { label: "PNG", note: "image" },
    pdf: { label: "PDF", note: "vector, for print" },
    html: { label: "HTML", note: "this explorer" },
  };

  class Exporter {
    constructor(store, app) {
      this.store = store;
      this.app = app;
      this.dialog = document.getElementById("export-dialog");
      this.opts = { format: "svg", legend: true, caption: true, dim: true, scale: 2, title: "", spacing: "auto" };
      this.dialog.addEventListener("close", () => {
        if (this._previewUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(this._previewUrl);
      });
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- figure scene

    scene({ legend = true, caption = true, dim = true, title = "", spacing = "auto" } = {}) {
      const store = this.store;
      const graph = store.graph();
      const hidden = new Set(store.state.hiddenRoles);
      if (store.state.filters.hideProxies) hidden.add("recorded proxy");
      const a = store.analysis();
      let highlight = null;
      const lines = [];
      if (a && a.statement) {
        const focus = store.transient.focusPath;
        highlight = { x: a.statement.x, y: a.statement.y, z: a.statement.z, openEdges: a.openEdges, focusEdges: focus ? MG.ui.edgeSet([focus]) : new Set(), blockers: focus && focus.blocker ? new Set([focus.blocker]) : new Set(), dim };
        if (caption) lines.push(...MG.scene.statementCaption(graph, a.statement, { implied: a.implied, openCount: a.open.paths.length, truncated: a.open.truncated }));
      }
      return MG.scene.buildScene({ graph, positions: store.positions(), hiddenRoles: hidden, highlight, legend, title: title || null, caption: lines, spacing });
    }

    baseName() {
      const graph = this.store.graph();
      const a = this.store.analysis();
      let name = slug(graph.name);
      if (a && a.statement) name += "-" + slug(a.statement.toString().replace("_||_", "indep").replace("|", "given")).slice(0, 60);
      return name;
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- save

    async save(format, opts = this.opts, filename = null) {
      const name = filename || `${this.baseName()}.${format}`;
      try {
        if (format === "html") return this.saveHtml(name);
        const scene = this.scene(opts);
        const title = opts.title || this.store.graph().name;
        if (format === "svg") download(name, MG.svgWriter.toSvg(scene, { title }), "image/svg+xml");
        else if (format === "pdf") download(name, MG.pdfWriter.toBytes(MG.pdfWriter.toPdf(scene, { title })), "application/pdf");
        else if (format === "png") download(name, await this.png(scene, opts.scale || 2), "image/png");
        toast(`Saved ${name}`);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
        toast(`Could not save: ${e.message}`, "error");
      }
    }

    png(scene, scale) {
      const svg = MG.svgWriter.toSvg(scene);
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const img = new Image();
        img.onload = () => {
          const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
          canvas.width = Math.round(scene.width * scale);
          canvas.height = Math.round(scene.height * scale);
          const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
          ctx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
          ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          ctx.scale(scale, scale);
          ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, scene.width, scene.height);
          canvas.toBlob((blob) => (blob ? resolve(blob) : reject(new Error("the browser could not encode the PNG"))), "image/png");
        };
        img.onerror = () => reject(new Error("the browser could not draw the figure"));
        img.src = "data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8," + encodeURIComponent(svg);
      });
    }

    snapshotHtml() {
      const pristine = MG.pristineHTML;
      if (!pristine) throw new Error("the page source is not available");
      const state = JSON.stringify(this.store.serialize()).replace(/</g, "\\u003c").replace(/>/g, "\\u003e").replace(/&/g, "\\u0026");
      const open = '<script type="application/json" id="mg-state">';
      const i = pristine.indexOf(open);
      const j = pristine.indexOf("<\/script>", i);
      if (i < 0 || j < 0) throw new Error("the page source has no state block");
      return pristine.slice(0, i + open.length) + state + pristine.slice(j);
    }

    saveHtml(name) {
      download(name, this.snapshotHtml(), "text/html");
      toast(`Saved ${name} with the current view`);
    }

    dagitty() {
      const graph = this.store.graph();
      const pos = this.store.positions();
      const scale = this.store.data.scale || 90;
      const lines = ["dag {"];
      for (const n of MG.sorted(graph.ids)) {
        const attrs = [];
        if (graph.latent.has(n)) attrs.push("latent");
        const p = pos.get(n);
        if (p) attrs.push(`pos="${(p.x / scale).toFixed(3)},${(p.y / scale).toFixed(3)}"`);
        lines.push(attrs.length ? `${n} [${attrs.join(",")}]` : n);
      }
      lines.push("");
      for (const [a, b] of graph.edges.slice().sort((e, f) => (e[0] + "\u0001" + e[1] < f[0] + "\u0001" + f[1] ? -1 : 1))) lines.push(`${a} -> ${b}`);
      lines.push("}");
      return lines.join("\n") + "\n";
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- dialog

    open(format = "svg") {
      this.opts.format = format;
      this.opts.title = this.opts.title || "";
      this._render();
      if (!this.dialog.open) this.dialog.showModal();
    }

    _render() {
      const o = this.opts;
      const a = this.store.analysis();
      const hasStatement = !!(a && a.statement);
      const isFigure = o.format !== "html";
      const fmtButtons = h("div", { class: "format-grid", role: "group", "aria-label": "Format" }, Object.entries(FORMATS).map(([k, f]) =>
        h("button", { type: "button", "aria-pressed": String(o.format === k), onClick: () => { o.format = k; this._render(); } }, h("b", null, f.label), h("span", null, f.note))));
      const name = h("input", { class: "input mono", value: `${this.baseName()}.${o.format}`, "aria-label": "File name" });
      const title = h("input", { class: "input", value: o.title, placeholder: "No title", "aria-label": "Figure title" });
      title.addEventListener("input", MG.ui.debounce(() => { o.title = title.value; this._preview(); }, 200));
      const tog = (key, label, disabled = false) => h("label", { class: "toggle", style: disabled ? { opacity: 0.45 } : null }, h("input", { type: "checkbox", checked: !!o[key] && !disabled, disabled, onChange: (e) => { o[key] = e.target.checked; this._preview(); } }), label);
      const spacingSel = h("select", { class: "select", "aria-label": "Spacing" },
        [["auto", "Compact"], ["1", "As on screen"], ["0.8", "80%"], ["0.6", "60%"]].map(([v, l]) => h("option", { value: v, selected: String(o.spacing) === v }, l)));
      spacingSel.addEventListener("change", (e) => { o.spacing = e.target.value === "auto" ? "auto" : +e.target.value; this._preview(); });
      const scaleSel = h("select", { class: "select", "aria-label": "PNG scale" }, [1, 2, 3, 4].map((s) => h("option", { value: s, selected: o.scale === s }, `${s}×`)));
      scaleSel.addEventListener("change", (e) => { o.scale = +e.target.value; this._preview(); });
      this._meta = h("div", { class: "preview-meta" });
      this._img = h("div", { class: "preview" });
      const body = h("div", { class: "dialog-body" },
        fmtButtons,
        isFigure ? [
          this._img, this._meta,
          h("label", { class: "block" }, "Title", title),
          h("div", { class: "filters" }, tog("legend", "Legend"), tog("caption", "Caption", !hasStatement), tog("dim", "Fade unrelated nodes", !hasStatement), h("span", { class: "field" }, h("span", { class: "muted" }, "Spacing"), spacingSel), o.format === "png" ? h("span", { class: "field" }, h("span", { class: "muted" }, "Scale"), scaleSel) : null),
          !hasStatement ? h("p", { class: "faint", style: { margin: 0, fontSize: "12px" } }, "Select a statement first to include its highlight and caption.") : null,
        ] : h("p", { class: "muted", style: { margin: 0 } }, "A single offline file with this explorer and its current state: model, edits, node layout, the selected statement and filters. Open it in any browser."),
        h("label", { class: "block" }, "File name", name));
      const save = h("button", { class: "btn primary", type: "button", onClick: async () => { this.dialog.close(); await this.save(o.format, o, name.value.trim() || null); } }, icon("download"), "Save");
      this.dialog.replaceChildren(
        h("header", null, h("h2", null, "Export"), MG.ui.iconButton("close", "Close", { onClick: () => this.dialog.close() })),
        body,
        h("footer", null, h("button", { class: "btn", type: "button", onClick: () => this.dialog.close() }, "Cancel"), save));
      if (isFigure) this._preview();
      setTimeout(() => save.focus(), 0);
    }

    _preview() {
      if (!this._img) return;
      const scene = this.scene(this.opts);
      const svg = MG.svgWriter.toSvg(scene);
      if (this._previewUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(this._previewUrl);
      this._previewUrl = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([svg], { type: "image/svg+xml" }));
      this._img.replaceChildren(h("img", { src: this._previewUrl, alt: "Figure preview" }));
      const s = this.opts.format === "png" ? this.opts.scale : 1;
      this._meta.textContent = this.opts.format === "png" ? `${Math.round(scene.width * s)} × ${Math.round(scene.height * s)} px` : `${scene.width} × ${scene.height} pt`;
    }
  }

  MG.ui.Exporter = Exporter;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/importer.js ---- */
/* Import dialog: paste, open or drop a DAG (DAGitty, Graphviz digraph, JSON or an edge list),
 * see a live preview with any warnings, and open it as a new graph. */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { h, icon, debounce } = MG.ui;

  const EXAMPLES = {
    DAGitty: `dag {
  Z [pos="1,0"]
  X [exposure,pos="0,1"]
  Y [outcome,pos="2,1"]
  Z -> X
  Z -> Y
  X -> Y
  X -> R_Y
}`,
    "edge list": `# one edge per line; <-> is an unrecorded common cause
A -> B
B -> C
A <-> C
C -> R_C`,
    JSON: `{
  "nodes": ["X", "M", "Y"],
  "edges": [["X", "M"], ["M", "Y"], {"from": "X", "to": "Y", "type": "<->"}]
}`,
    Graphviz: `digraph G {
  age -> obesity;
  gender -> obesity;
  age -> R_obesity;
}`,
  };

  class Importer {
    constructor(store, app) {
      this.store = store;
      this.app = app;
      this.dialog = document.getElementById("import-dialog");
      this.result = null;
    }

    open(text = "") {
      const area = h("textarea", { class: "input mono import-text", spellcheck: false, placeholder: "Paste DAGitty text, an edge list such as  A -> B, JSON, or a Graphviz digraph. You can also drop a file here.", "aria-label": "Graph text" });
      area.value = text;
      const name = h("input", { class: "input", placeholder: "Name of the graph", "aria-label": "Graph name" });
      const file = h("input", { type: "file", accept: ".txt,.dag,.dagitty,.dot,.gv,.json,.csv,text/plain,application/json", style: { display: "none" } });
      this.status = h("div", { class: "import-status", role: "status" });
      this.preview = h("div", { class: "preview import-preview" }, h("span", { class: "faint" }, "Preview"));
      const openBtn = h("button", { class: "btn primary", type: "button", disabled: true, onClick: () => this._open(name.value.trim()) }, "Open graph");
      this.openBtn = openBtn;
      const update = () => this._check(area.value, name.value.trim());
      area.addEventListener("input", debounce(update, 180));
      name.addEventListener("input", debounce(update, 180));
      file.addEventListener("change", async () => {
        const f = file.files[0];
        if (!f) return;
        area.value = await f.text();
        if (!name.value) name.value = f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, "");
        update();
      });
      for (const ev of ["dragenter", "dragover"]) area.addEventListener(ev, (e) => { e.preventDefault(); area.classList.add("is-drop"); });
      for (const ev of ["dragleave", "drop"]) area.addEventListener(ev, () => area.classList.remove("is-drop"));
      area.addEventListener("drop", async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const f = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
        if (f) {
          area.value = await f.text();
          if (!name.value) name.value = f.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, "");
        } else area.value = e.dataTransfer.getData("text");
        update();
      });
      const examples = h("div", { class: "filters" }, h("span", { class: "muted", style: { fontSize: "12px" } }, "Examples:"),
        Object.keys(EXAMPLES).map((k) => h("button", { type: "button", class: "btn small", onClick: () => { area.value = EXAMPLES[k]; update(); } }, k)));
      this.dialog.replaceChildren(
        h("header", null, h("h2", null, "Import a DAG"), MG.ui.iconButton("close", "Close", { onClick: () => this.dialog.close() })),
        h("div", { class: "dialog-body" },
          h("div", { class: "import-grid" },
            h("div", { class: "import-col" }, area, h("div", { class: "filters" }, h("button", { type: "button", class: "btn small", onClick: () => file.click() }, icon("download"), "Open a file"), file), examples),
            h("div", { class: "import-col" }, this.preview, this.status)),
          h("label", { class: "block" }, "Name", name),
          h("p", { class: "faint", style: { margin: 0, fontSize: "12px" } },
            "Missing data: name a variable's missingness indicator R_X (1 = missing). Its recorded proxy X_star is added for you. Mark unmeasured variables [latent] in DAGitty, or use A <-> B for an unrecorded common cause.")),
        h("footer", null, h("button", { class: "btn", type: "button", onClick: () => this.dialog.close() }, "Cancel"), openBtn));
      this.dialog.classList.add("wide");
      if (!this.dialog.open) this.dialog.showModal();
      area.focus();
      if (text) update();
    }

    _check(text, name) {
      this.result = null;
      this.openBtn.disabled = true;
      if (!text.trim()) {
        this.status.replaceChildren();
        this.preview.replaceChildren(h("span", { class: "faint" }, "Preview"));
        return;
      }
      try {
        const res = MG.parse.graphText(text, { name: name || "Imported graph" });
        this.result = res;
        const g = new MG.Graph({ nodes: res.spec.nodes, edges: res.spec.edges, latent: res.spec.nodes.filter((n) => n.latent).map((n) => n.id) });
        const scene = MG.scene.buildScene({ graph: g, positions: new Map(res.spec.nodes.map((n) => [n.id, { x: n.x, y: n.y }])), legend: false, spacing: "auto" });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([MG.svgWriter.toSvg(scene)], { type: "image/svg+xml" }));
        if (this._url) URL.revokeObjectURL(this._url);
        this._url = url;
        this.preview.replaceChildren(h("img", { src: url, alt: "Preview of the graph" }));
        const counts = MG.ROLES.map((r) => [r, g.ids.filter((id) => g.role(id) === r).length]).filter(([, n]) => n);
        this.status.replaceChildren(
          h("div", { class: "problem ok" }, h("b", null, `${res.format}: `), `${g.ids.length} nodes, ${g.edgeCount} edges. `, counts.map(([r, n]) => `${n} ${r}`).join(", "), "."),
          ...res.warnings.map((w) => h("div", { class: "problem" }, w)),
          ...g.validate().map((w) => h("div", { class: "problem" }, w)));
        this.openBtn.disabled = false;
      } catch (e) {
        this.preview.replaceChildren(h("span", { class: "faint" }, "No preview"));
        this.status.replaceChildren(h("div", { class: "problem error" }, h("b", null, "Can't read this graph: "), e.message));
      }
    }

    _open(name) {
      if (!this.result) return;
      const spec = { ...this.result.spec, name: name || this.result.spec.name, source: "user" };
      this.dialog.close();
      this.app.openSpec(spec);
    }
  }

  MG.ui.Importer = Importer;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/toolbar.js ---- */
/* Top bar: model switch, command box (find a node or check a statement), and actions. */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { h, icon, iconButton } = MG.ui;

  class Toolbar {
    constructor(store, app) {
      this.store = store;
      this.app = app;
      this.switchEl = document.getElementById("model-switch");
      this.actionsEl = document.getElementById("actions");
      this.input = document.getElementById("command-input");
      this.suggest = document.getElementById("command-suggest");
      this.sub = document.getElementById("model-sub");
      this._sel = 0;
      this._buildActions();
      this._bindCommand();
      store.on((changed) => {
        if (["modelKey", "edits", "theme", "userOrder"].some((k) => changed.has(k)) || changed.has("t:editMode")) this.render();
        if (changed.has("statement") && document.activeElement !== this.input) this.input.value = store.state.statement || "";
      });
      this.render();
    }

    render() {
      const store = this.store;
      const cur = store.model;
      const key = store.state.modelKey;
      const picker = h("button", { type: "button", class: "graph-picker", "aria-haspopup": "true", title: "Open another graph, import your own, or start a new one", onClick: (e) => this._graphMenu(e.currentTarget) },
        h("span", { class: "graph-picker-name" }, store.graph().name), h("span", { class: "chev", "aria-hidden": "true" }, "▾"));
      const parts = [picker];
      if (store.isBuiltin(key) && cur.family) {
        const models = store.data.models;
        const families = [...new Set(models.map((m) => m.family))];
        const ups = [...new Set(models.filter((m) => m.family === cur.family).map((m) => m.upstream))].filter((u) => u != null);
        parts.push(h("div", { class: "segmented" }, families.map((f) =>
          h("button", { type: "button", "aria-pressed": String(cur.family === f), title: modelTitle(f), onClick: () => this.app.switchModel(this._find(f, cur.upstream)) }, f))));
        if (ups.length > 1) {
          const upSel = h("select", { class: "select", "aria-label": "Upstream antibiotics" },
            ups.sort((a, b) => b - a).map((u) => h("option", { value: u, selected: u === cur.upstream }, u === 1 ? "single upstream" : `${u} upstream`)));
          upSel.addEventListener("change", (e) => this.app.switchModel(this._find(cur.family, +e.target.value)));
          parts.push(upSel);
        }
      }
      this.switchEl.replaceChildren(...parts);
      const g = store.graph();
      this.sub.textContent = `${g.ids.length} nodes · ${g.edgeCount} edges${store.isEdited() ? " · edited" : ""}`;
      this.editBtn.setAttribute("aria-pressed", String(store.transient.editMode));
      this.undoBtn.disabled = !store.history.past.length;
      this.redoBtn.disabled = !store.history.future.length;
      const t = store.state.theme;
      this.themeBtn.replaceChildren(icon(t === "dark" ? "moon" : t === "light" ? "sun" : "auto"));
      this.themeBtn.title = `Theme: ${t === "auto" ? "follows system" : t}`;
    }

    /** The graph menu: every graph by group, with import, new and duplicate. */
    _graphMenu(anchor) {
      if (this._menuEl) return this._closeMenu();
      const store = this.store;
      const cur = store.state.modelKey;
      const loaded = new Map(store.listModels().map((m) => [m.key, m]));
      const item = (key, label, sub, fn, current) =>
        h("li", null, h("button", { type: "button", class: current ? "is-current" : null, onClick: () => { this._closeMenu(); fn(); } },
          h("span", { class: "menu-label" }, label), sub ? h("span", { class: "menu-desc" }, sub) : null));
      const groups = [];
      const builtins = store.data.models;
      if (builtins.length) groups.push(["AMR models", builtins.map((m) => item(m.key, store.graph(m.key).name, loaded.get(m.key).edited ? "edited" : "", () => this.app.switchModel(m.key), m.key === cur))]);
      const galleryGroups = [...new Set(MG.gallery.ENTRIES.map((e) => e.group))];
      for (const gname of galleryGroups)
        groups.push([gname, MG.gallery.ENTRIES.filter((e) => e.group === gname).map((e) =>
          item(e.key, e.name, loaded.has(e.key) && loaded.get(e.key).edited ? "edited" : "", () => this.app.openGallery(e.key), e.key === cur))]);
      const users = store.listModels().filter((m) => !m.builtin && m.source !== "gallery");
      if (users.length) groups.push(["Your graphs", users.map((m) => item(m.key, m.name, `${m.nodes.length} nodes`, () => this.app.switchModel(m.key), m.key === cur))]);
      const search = h("input", { class: "input", type: "search", placeholder: "Find a graph", "aria-label": "Find a graph", style: { width: "100%" } });
      const list = h("div", { class: "menu-scroll" }, groups.map(([title, items]) => h("div", { class: "menu-group" }, h("div", { class: "menu-heading" }, title), h("ul", null, items))));
      search.addEventListener("input", () => {
        const q = search.value.trim().toLowerCase();
        for (const li of list.querySelectorAll("li")) li.hidden = !!q && !li.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        for (const g of list.querySelectorAll(".menu-group")) g.hidden = ![...g.querySelectorAll("li")].some((li) => !li.hidden);
      });
      const actions = h("div", { class: "menu-actions" },
        h("button", { type: "button", class: "btn small primary", onClick: () => { this._closeMenu(); this.app.importer.open(); } }, icon("download"), "Import a DAG"),
        h("button", { type: "button", class: "btn small", onClick: () => { this._closeMenu(); this.app.newGraph(); } }, icon("plus"), "New graph"),
        h("button", { type: "button", class: "btn small", onClick: () => { this._closeMenu(); this.app.duplicateGraph(); } }, icon("copy"), "Duplicate"),
        !store.isBuiltin() && store.model.source !== "gallery" ? h("button", { type: "button", class: "btn small", onClick: () => { this._closeMenu(); this.app.removeGraph(); } }, icon("close"), "Remove") : null);
      const menu = h("div", { class: "menu graph-menu", role: "menu" }, actions, search, list);
      const r = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
      menu.style.top = r.bottom + 6 + "px";
      menu.style.left = Math.max(8, r.left) + "px";
      document.body.appendChild(menu);
      this._menuEl = menu;
      search.focus();
      this._bindMenuClose(anchor);
    }

    _buildActions() {
      this.editBtn = iconButton("edit", "Edit the graph (E)", { text: "Edit", onClick: () => this.app.toggleEdit() });
      this.undoBtn = iconButton("undo", "Undo edit (⌘Z)", { onClick: () => this.store.undo() });
      this.redoBtn = iconButton("redo", "Redo edit (⇧⌘Z)", { onClick: () => this.store.redo() });
      this.exportBtn = iconButton("download", "Export", { text: "Export", onClick: (e) => this._menu(e.currentTarget) });
      this.themeBtn = iconButton("auto", "Theme", { onClick: () => this.app.cycleTheme() });
      const help = iconButton("help", "Help and shortcuts (?)", { onClick: () => this.app.showHelp() });
      this.actionsEl.replaceChildren(this.editBtn, this.undoBtn, this.redoBtn, this.exportBtn, this.themeBtn, help);
    }

    _menu(anchor) {
      if (this._menuEl) return this._closeMenu();
      const ex = this.app.exporter;
      const item = (ext, label, desc, fn) => h("li", null, h("button", { type: "button", onClick: () => { this._closeMenu(); fn(); } }, h("span", { class: "menu-ext" }, ext), label, h("span", { class: "menu-desc" }, desc)));
      const menu = h("ul", { class: "menu", role: "menu" },
        item("SVG", "Figure", "vector", () => ex.open("svg")),
        item("PNG", "Image", "1–4×", () => ex.open("png")),
        item("PDF", "Document", "vector", () => ex.open("pdf")),
        item("HTML", "This explorer", "with state", () => ex.save("html")),
        h("hr"),
        item("CSV", "All conditions", "table", () => this.app.panels.conditions._csv(this.store.catalogue())),
        item("TXT", "DAGitty graph", "download", () => MG.ui.download(`${MG.ui.slug(this.store.graph().name)}.txt`, ex.dagitty(), "text/plain")),
        item("", "Copy DAGitty text", "clipboard", () => MG.ui.copyText(ex.dagitty())));
      const r = anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
      menu.style.top = r.bottom + 6 + "px";
      menu.style.right = Math.max(8, window.innerWidth - r.right) + "px";
      document.body.appendChild(menu);
      this._menuEl = menu;
      menu.querySelector("button").focus();
      this._bindMenuClose(anchor);
    }

    _bindMenuClose(anchor) {
      const menu = this._menuEl;
      this._outside = (e) => {
        if (!menu.contains(e.target) && e.target !== anchor && !anchor.contains(e.target)) this._closeMenu();
      };
      this._esc = (e) => e.key === "Escape" && this._closeMenu();
      setTimeout(() => {
        document.addEventListener("pointerdown", this._outside);
        document.addEventListener("keydown", this._esc);
      });
    }

    _closeMenu() {
      if (!this._menuEl) return;
      this._menuEl.remove();
      this._menuEl = null;
      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", this._outside);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", this._esc);
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- command box

    _bindCommand() {
      const input = this.input;
      input.addEventListener("input", () => {
        input.classList.remove("is-error");
        this._sel = 0;
        this._renderSuggest();
      });
      input.addEventListener("focus", () => this._renderSuggest());
      input.addEventListener("blur", () => setTimeout(() => (this.suggest.hidden = true), 150));
      input.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
        const items = this._items || [];
        if (e.key === "ArrowDown") {
          e.preventDefault();
          this._sel = Math.min(items.length - 1, this._sel + 1);
          this._renderSuggest(false);
        } else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") {
          e.preventDefault();
          this._sel = Math.max(0, this._sel - 1);
          this._renderSuggest(false);
        } else if (e.key === "Enter") {
          e.preventDefault();
          const it = items[this._sel];
          if (it) it.run();
          else if (input.value.trim()) input.classList.add("is-error");
        } else if (e.key === "Escape") {
          this.suggest.hidden = true;
          input.blur();
        }
      });
    }

    _renderSuggest(rebuild = true) {
      const text = this.input.value.trim();
      const graph = this.store.graph();
      if (rebuild) {
        const items = [];
        if (/_\|\|_|⊥|⫫/.test(text)) {
          let ok = null;
          try {
            MG.Statement.parse(text).check(graph);
            ok = true;
          } catch (err) {
            ok = err.message;
          }
          items.push({ label: ok === true ? "Check this statement" : `Can't check: ${ok}`, hint: "↵", run: () => { if (ok === true) { this.app.showStatement(text); this.input.blur(); } else this.input.classList.add("is-error"); } });
        } else if (text) {
          const q = text.toLowerCase();
          const nodes = graph.ids.filter((n) => n.toLowerCase().includes(q)).sort((a, b) => (a.toLowerCase().startsWith(q) ? 0 : 1) - (b.toLowerCase().startsWith(q) ? 0 : 1) || a.length - b.length || (a < b ? -1 : 1)).slice(0, 8);
          for (const n of nodes) items.push({ node: n, label: n, hint: graph.role(n), run: () => { this.app.selectNode(n, { center: true }); this.input.value = ""; this.input.blur(); } });
          const desc = graph.ids.filter((n) => !nodes.includes(n) && ((graph.meta.get(n) || {}).description || "").toLowerCase().includes(q)).slice(0, 5);
          for (const n of desc) items.push({ node: n, label: n, hint: graph.meta.get(n).description, run: () => { this.app.selectNode(n, { center: true }); this.input.value = ""; this.input.blur(); } });
        }
        this._items = items;
      }
      const items = this._items || [];
      if (!items.length || document.activeElement !== this.input) {
        this.suggest.hidden = true;
        return;
      }
      this.suggest.replaceChildren(...items.map((it, i) => {
        const li = h("li", { role: "option", "aria-selected": String(i === this._sel) },
          it.node ? MG.ui.roleGlyph(graph.role(it.node), 18, 11) : icon("query"),
          h("span", { class: it.node ? "mono" : null }, it.label), h("span", { class: "hint" }, it.hint));
        li.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => { e.preventDefault(); it.run(); });
        return li;
      }));
      this.suggest.hidden = false;
    }
  }

  function modelTitle(f) {
    return { M1: "M1: primary model", M2: "M2: unmeasured clinical selection (U_C)", M3: "M3: downstream self-masking (Yk -> R_Yk)" }[f] || f;
  }

  MG.ui.Toolbar = Toolbar;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/ui/keyboard.js ---- */
/* Keyboard shortcuts. Ignored while typing in a field, except Escape. */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";

  const SHORTCUTS = [
    ["/", "Find a node or type a statement"],
    ["1 – 5", "Node, Conditions, Query, Missing data, Causal effect tabs"],
    ["F", "Fit the graph to the window"],
    ["+  −", "Zoom in, zoom out"],
    ["E", "Edit mode: click two nodes to join them, click an edge to delete it, double-click to add a node"],
    ["B", "In edit mode, switch between → (cause) and ↔ (common cause)"],
    ["Delete", "In edit mode, delete the selected node"],
    ["⌘Z  ⇧⌘Z", "Undo, redo an edit"],
    ["⌘S", "Export"],
    ["Shift + click", "Add a node to, or remove it from, the conditioning set Z"],
    ["Esc", "Clear the selection, picking or edit mode"],
    ["?", "This help"],
  ];

  function bindKeyboard(app) {
    document.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
      const typing = e.target.closest && e.target.closest("input, textarea, select, [contenteditable]");
      const mod = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;
      if (e.key === "Escape") {
        if (document.querySelector("dialog[open]")) return;
        if (typing) return e.target.blur();
        return app.escape();
      }
      if (mod && e.key.toLowerCase() === "z" && !typing) {
        e.preventDefault();
        return e.shiftKey ? app.store.redo() : app.store.undo();
      }
      if (mod && e.key.toLowerCase() === "s") {
        e.preventDefault();
        return app.exporter.open("svg");
      }
      if (typing || mod || e.altKey) return;
      const k = e.key;
      if (k === "/") {
        e.preventDefault();
        document.getElementById("command-input").focus();
      } else if (k === "f" || k === "F") app.view.fit(true);
      else if (k === "+" || k === "=") app.view.zoomBy(1.25);
      else if (k === "-" || k === "_") app.view.zoomBy(0.8);
      else if (k === "e" || k === "E") app.toggleEdit();
      else if (k === "?") app.showHelp();
      else if (["1", "2", "3", "4", "5"].includes(k)) app.showTab(["node", "conditions", "query", "missing", "causal"][+k - 1]);
      else if ((k === "Delete" || k === "Backspace") && app.store.transient.editMode && app.store.state.node) app.store.deleteNode(app.store.state.node);
      else if ((k === "b" || k === "B") && app.store.transient.editMode) app.store.setTransient({ edgeKind: app.store.transient.edgeKind === "bidirected" ? "directed" : "bidirected" });
    });
  }

  MG.ui.bindKeyboard = bindKeyboard;
  MG.ui.SHORTCUTS = SHORTCUTS;
})(globalThis.MG);

;
/* ---- js/app.js ---- */
/* The explorer application: wires the store, graph view, panels, toolbar and exporter. */
(function (MG) {
  "use strict";
  const { h, icon, iconButton, roleGlyph } = MG.ui;
  const $ = (sel) => document.querySelector(sel);

  // the page as delivered, before any DOM changes: the base for "Export > HTML"
  MG.pristineHTML = "<!doctype html>\n" + document.documentElement.outerHTML;

  const TABS = [
    ["node", "Node"],
    ["conditions", "Conditions"],
    ["query", "Query"],
    ["missing", "Missing data"],
    ["causal", "Causal effect"],
  ];

  class App {
    constructor() {
      const data = JSON.parse($("#mg-data").textContent);
      let saved = {};
      try {
        saved = JSON.parse($("#mg-state").textContent) || {};
      } catch (e) {
        saved = {};
      }
      if (!saved.theme) {
        try {
          saved.theme = localStorage.getItem("mg-theme") || "auto";
        } catch (e) {
          /* storage blocked: follow the system */
        }
      }
      const store = (this.store = new MG.ui.Store(data, saved));
      this.body = $("#panel-body");
      this.tabsEl = $("#tabs");
      this._applyTheme();
      this._applyPanelWidth();

      this.view = new MG.ui.GraphView($("#canvas"), store, {
        onNodeClick: (id, e) => this._nodeClick(id, e),
        onEdgeClick: (a, b) => this._edgeClick(a, b),
        onBackgroundClick: () => this._backgroundClick(),
        onBackgroundDblClick: (pt, e) => this._askNodeName(pt, e),
      });
      this.view.onViewChange = () => this._status();
      this.panels = {
        node: new MG.ui.InspectorPanel(store, this),
        conditions: new MG.ui.ConditionsPanel(store, this),
        query: new MG.ui.QueryPanel(store, this),
        missing: new MG.ui.MissingPanel(store, this),
        causal: new MG.ui.CausalPanel(store, this),
      };
      this.exporter = new MG.ui.Exporter(store, this);
      this.importer = new MG.ui.Importer(store, this);
      this.toolbar = new MG.ui.Toolbar(store, this);
      this._controls();
      this._splitter();
      store.on((changed) => this._onChange(changed));
      MG.ui.bindKeyboard(this);
      this._tabs();
      this._legend();
      this._banner();
      this._status();
      this.renderPanel();
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- navigation

    showTab(tab) {
      if (this.store.state.tab !== tab) this.store.set({ tab });
    }

    renderPanel() {
      if (!this.panels[this.store.state.tab]) this.store.state.tab = "node";
      const panel = this.panels[this.store.state.tab];
      panel.render(this.body);
    }

    selectNode(id, { center = false } = {}) {
      const st = this.store.state;
      if (st.tab === "conditions") this.store.set({ node: id, filters: { ...st.filters, node: id } });
      else this.store.set({ node: id, tab: "node" });
      if (center) this.view.centerOn(id);
    }

    showStatement(text, { stay = false } = {}) {
      this.store.set({ statement: text, ...(stay ? {} : { tab: "query" }) });
      this.store.setTransient({ focusPath: null });
    }

    queryFrom(id) {
      const cur = MG.Statement.parseLoose(this.store.state.statement || "");
      const st = new MG.Statement([id], [...cur.y].filter((n) => n !== id), [...cur.z].filter((n) => n !== id));
      this.store.set({ statement: st.toLooseString(), tab: "query" });
      if (!st.y.size) this.store.setTransient({ pick: "y" });
    }

    toggleInSlot(slot, n) {
      const cur = MG.Statement.parseLoose(this.store.state.statement || "");
      const had = cur[slot].has(n);
      for (const s of ["x", "y", "z"]) cur[s].delete(n);
      if (!had) cur[slot].add(n);
      const text = cur.toLooseString();
      this.store.set({ statement: text === "_||_" ? null : text });
    }

    switchModel(key) {
      if (!key || key === this.store.state.modelKey) return;
      this.store.setTransient({ editSource: null, focusPath: null, pick: null });
      this.store.switchModel(key);
      this._afterSwitch();
    }

    openGallery(key) {
      this.store.setTransient({ editSource: null, focusPath: null, pick: null });
      this.store.openGallery(key);
      this._afterSwitch();
      const spec = this.store.model;
      if (spec.note) MG.ui.toast("This graph has a note about the paper: see the Node tab", "");
    }

    /** Open a graph the user imported. */
    openSpec(spec) {
      this.store.addUserGraph(spec);
      this._afterSwitch();
      MG.ui.toast(`Opened ${this.store.graph().name}`);
    }

    newGraph() {
      this.store.addUserGraph({ name: "New graph", nodes: [], edges: [], source: "user" });
      this._afterSwitch();
      this.toggleEdit(true);
    }

    duplicateGraph() {
      this.store.duplicate();
      this._afterSwitch();
      MG.ui.toast(`Made ${this.store.graph().name}`);
    }

    removeGraph() {
      const name = this.store.graph().name;
      if (!window.confirm(`Remove "${name}" from this page? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
      this.store.removeModel();
      this._afterSwitch();
      MG.ui.toast(`Removed ${name}`);
    }

    /** After changing graph: drop selections that do not exist in the new graph. */
    _afterSwitch() {
      const g = this.store.graph();
      const patch = {};
      if (this.store.state.node && !g.has(this.store.state.node)) patch.node = null;
      if (this.store.state.statement) {
        const st = MG.Statement.parseLoose(this.store.state.statement);
        if (![...st.x, ...st.y, ...st.z].every((n) => g.has(n))) patch.statement = null;
      }
      if (this.store.state.filters.node && !g.has(this.store.state.filters.node)) patch.filters = { ...this.store.state.filters, node: "" };
      if (Object.keys(patch).length) this.store.set(patch);
    }

    /** In edit mode, double-click on the canvas asks for a new node's name. */
    _askNodeName(pt, e) {
      const canvas = $("#canvas");
      const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      const box = h("div", { class: "node-namer", style: { left: e.clientX - r.left - 80 + "px", top: e.clientY - r.top - 18 + "px" } });
      const input = h("input", { class: "input mono", placeholder: "Node name", "aria-label": "New node name" });
      const hint = h("div", { class: "faint", style: { fontSize: "11px", marginTop: "3px" } }, "Enter to add · Esc to cancel");
      box.append(input, hint);
      canvas.appendChild(box);
      input.focus();
      const done = (ok) => {
        const name = input.value.trim();
        box.remove();
        if (ok && name && this.store.addNode(name, Math.round(pt.x), Math.round(pt.y))) {
          MG.ui.toast(`Added ${name}`);
          this.store.set({ node: name });
        }
      };
      input.addEventListener("keydown", (ev) => {
        if (ev.key === "Enter") done(true);
        if (ev.key === "Escape") {
          ev.stopPropagation();
          done(false);
        }
        const problem = MG.ui.nameProblem(input.value.trim(), this.store.graph());
        input.classList.toggle("is-error", !!problem && !!input.value.trim());
      });
      input.addEventListener("blur", () => setTimeout(() => box.isConnected && done(true), 120));
    }

    toggleEdit(on = !this.store.transient.editMode) {
      this.store.setTransient({ editMode: on, editSource: null, pick: null });
      this.view.clearGhost();
    }

    escape() {
      const tr = this.store.transient;
      if (tr.editSource) {
        this.store.setTransient({ editSource: null });
        this.view.clearGhost();
      } else if (tr.editMode) this.toggleEdit(false);
      else if (tr.pick) this.store.setTransient({ pick: null });
      else if (this.store.state.statement) this.store.set({ statement: null });
      else if (this.store.state.node) this.store.set({ node: null });
    }

    cycleTheme() {
      const order = ["auto", "light", "dark"];
      const theme = order[(order.indexOf(this.store.state.theme) + 1) % order.length];
      this.store.set({ theme });
      try {
        localStorage.setItem("mg-theme", theme);
      } catch (e) {
        /* storage blocked */
      }
    }

    showHelp() {
      const d = $("#help-dialog");
      d.replaceChildren(
        h("header", null, h("h2", null, "Using the explorer"), iconButton("close", "Close", { onClick: () => d.close() })),
        h("div", { class: "dialog-body" },
          h("p", { class: "muted", style: { margin: 0 } }, "Every independence shown is computed from the graph with d-separation, in this page, so it updates as soon as you edit the graph. The same algorithms run in the Python package, and the test suite checks that both give the same answers."),
          h("dl", { class: "shortcut-list" }, MG.ui.SHORTCUTS.map(([k, v]) => [h("dt", null, h("kbd", null, k)), h("dd", null, v)])),
          h("p", { class: "faint", style: { fontSize: "12px", margin: "4px 0 0" } }, `Generated ${this.store.data.generated || ""} by amr-identifiability ${this.store.data.version || ""}.`)),
        h("footer", null, h("button", { class: "btn primary", onClick: () => d.close() }, "Done")));
      d.showModal();
    }

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- graph clicks

    _nodeClick(id, e) {
      const tr = this.store.transient;
      if (tr.editMode) {
        if (!tr.editSource) return this.store.setTransient({ editSource: id });
        if (tr.editSource === id) return this.store.setTransient({ editSource: null });
        const bi = tr.edgeKind === "bidirected";
        const ok = bi ? this.store.addBidirected(tr.editSource, id) : this.store.addEdge(tr.editSource, id);
        if (ok) MG.ui.toast(bi ? `Added ${tr.editSource} ↔ ${id} (an unrecorded common cause)` : `Added ${tr.editSource} → ${id}`);
        this.store.setTransient({ editSource: e.shiftKey ? tr.editSource : null });
        if (!e.shiftKey) this.view.clearGhost();
        return;
      }
      if (tr.pick) return this.toggleInSlot(tr.pick, id);
      if (e.shiftKey && this.store.state.statement) return this.toggleInSlot("z", id);
      this.selectNode(id);
    }

    _edgeClick(a, b) {
      if (this.store.transient.editMode) {
        if (this.store.removeEdge(a, b)) MG.ui.toast(`Deleted ${a} → ${b}`);
      }
    }

    _backgroundClick() {
      const tr = this.store.transient;
      if (tr.editSource) {
        this.store.setTransient({ editSource: null });
